01

Simulation core

Particle motion, periodic contacts and runtime reporting.

modified_engine.pyHard-particle dynamics, contact mechanics and drag couplingSee code ↗

Periodic coordinates are normalised in place before neighbour and contact calculations.

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""Hard-sphere dynamics on a wall-parallel plane.

Particle centres move in the ``(x, z)`` plane. The right-handed frame uses
``e_x`` in the streamwise direction, ``e_y`` away from the wall, and ``e_z``
in the spanwise direction. In-plane arrays therefore store ``[x, z]`` even
where older local names use ``y`` for the second component. Angular velocity
is stored as ``[omega_x, omega_z, omega_y]``: rotations about the streamwise,
spanwise, and wall-normal axes.

For in-plane vectors ``A`` and ``B``, ``(A x B)_y = A_z B_x - A_x B_z`` and
``e_y x A = (A_z, 0, -A_x)``. Thus the horizontal part of ``omega x A``
depends on ``omega_y``, while its vertical part depends on ``omega_x`` and
``omega_z``.

The model hierarchy is selected by the following arguments::

    model  rotation  e_tan       mu_pp    wall_friction  mu_w, g_eff
    A      False     1 (unused)  -1       False          --
    B      True      1           -1       False          --
    C      True      < 1         -1       False          --
    D      True      any         >= 0     False          --
    E      True      any         >= 0     True           > 0

Here ``e_tan`` is the tangential restitution ``e_par`` in ``[-1, 1]``;
``e_tan = 1`` denotes smooth contact and ``mu_pp < 0`` disables the Coulomb
cap. Models C--E require rotational state.

For a homogeneous sphere, ``I = (2/5) m r^2`` and
``tau_rot = (3/10) tau_p``. Translational and rotational drag follow

``dU/dt = (U_f^h - U)/tau_p`` and
``d omega/dt = (omega_f - omega)/tau_rot``,

where ``omega_f = (1/2) curl U_f`` is supplied in the storage order above.

For contact pair ``(i, j)``, let
``n = (X_j - X_i)/|X_j - X_i|`` and ``t = e_y x n = (n_z, -n_x)``. The
impulse on particle ``j`` is ``P = P_perp n + P_par t``. The normal impulse is

``P_perp = -(1 + e_perp) U_c,perp^- / (1/m_i + 1/m_j)``.

For rough contact, the tangential surface slip and impulse are

``U_c,par^- = (U_j - U_i).t - (r_i omega_y,i + r_j omega_y,j)``,
``P_par = -(1 - e_par) U_c,par^- / k``,

with ``k = 1/m_i + 1/m_j + r_i^2/I_i + r_j^2/I_j``. Models D and E impose
``|P_par| <= mu_pp P_perp`` with sign opposing the slip. Tangential contact
changes only ``omega_y``.

Model E uses wall-contact slip
``s = U - r omega x e_y = (U_x + r omega_z, U_z - r omega_x)``. Rolling
requires
``F_req = -(2m/7)(A - r B x e_y)`` with ``|F_req| <= mu_w m g_eff``, where
``A = (U_f^h - U)/tau_p`` and ``B = (omega_f - omega)/tau_rot``. Otherwise,
``F = -mu_w m g_eff s/|s|``. Only the product ``mu_w g_eff`` enters. The
rolling/sliding regime is evaluated at the start of each half-kick, so a
regime change is localized only to that half-kick.
The wall model has no twisting resistance, so it does not change ``omega_y``.

The recorded vertical diagnostic uses the full tangential slip
``U~_T = U_c,par^- t + U~_y e_y``, where
``U~_y = -[r_i(omega_z,i n_x - omega_x,i n_z) +
r_j(omega_z,j n_x - omega_x,j n_z)]``. The corresponding hypothetical impulse
``P~_T`` is stored for analysis but never applied. The planar impulse is
computed independently rather than by projecting this diagnostic quantity.

Time integration uses a half kick, a force-free drift with exact contact
events, and a second half kick (Strang splitting). Carrier fields are frozen
during each kick. The optional ``mu_frict`` and ``g`` arguments implement a
separate centre-speed substrate-friction model.

The drag closure follows Minier & Peirano, Physics Reports 352 (2001),
1--214. Numba kernels are compiled on first use.
"""

import numpy as np

    print("Install with: pip install scikit-learn")

try:
    from numba import njit

    print("Numba available — physics functions will be JIT-compiled.")
except ImportError:
    NUMBA_AVAILABLE = False
    print("WARNING: Numba not installed. Running in pure Python (slow).")
    print("Install with: pip install numba")

    def njit(*args, **kwargs):
        if len(args) == 1 and callable(args[0]):
            return args[0]
        def decorator(f):
            return f
        return decorator


class Tee:
    """Send printed output to both the console and a text file."""
    def __init__(self, *streams):
        self.streams = streams

    def write(self, data):
        for s in self.streams:
            s.write(data)
            s.flush()

    def flush(self):
        for s in self.streams:
            s.flush()


# Homogeneous-sphere constants fixed by the particle model.
INERTIA_COEFF = 2.0 / 5.0      # I = (2/5) m r^2
TAU_ROT_FACTOR = 3.0 / 10.0    # tau_rot = I / (8 pi rho_f nu r^3) = (3/10) tau_p

os.makedirs(SNAPSHOT_DIR, exist_ok=True)


# Input validation

def validate_parameters(L, W, masses, radii, T_final, dt,
                        tau, mu_frict, g,
                        e_ball, e_wall=1.0, boundary="periodic"):
    """Validate run parameters and report the first inadmissible value."""
    if L <= 0 or W <= 0:
        raise ValueError(f"Domain size must be positive: got L = {L}, "
                         f"W = {W}.")
    masses = np.asarray(masses, dtype=float)
    radii = np.asarray(radii, dtype=float)
    if masses.ndim != 1 or radii.ndim != 1:
        raise ValueError(f"masses and radii must be 1-D arrays: got shapes "
                         f"{masses.shape} and {radii.shape}.")
    if len(masses) != len(radii):
        raise ValueError(f"masses and radii must have the same length: got "
                         f"{len(masses)} and {len(radii)}.")
    if np.any(masses <= 0):
        raise ValueError("Every mass must be positive (found a mass <= 0).")
    if np.any((radii <= 0) | (2 * radii >= min(L, W))):
        raise ValueError(f"Every radius must be positive and its diameter "
                         f"smaller than min(L, W) = {min(L, W)}.")
    if T_final <= 0:
        raise ValueError(f"T_final must be positive: got {T_final}.")
    if dt <= 0:
        raise ValueError(f"dt must be positive: got {dt}.")
    tau_arr = np.atleast_1d(np.asarray(tau, dtype=float))
    if np.any(tau_arr <= 0):
        raise ValueError("tau (particle response time) must be positive; "
                         "for a tracer pass a tiny value such as 1e-12.")
    if mu_frict < 0:
        raise ValueError(f"mu_frict must be >= 0: got {mu_frict}.")
    if g < 0:
        raise ValueError(f"g must be >= 0: got {g}.")
    if not (0.0 <= e_ball <= 1.0):
        raise ValueError(f"e_ball (normal restitution) must be in [0, 1]: "
                         f"got {e_ball}.")
    if boundary not in ("wall", "periodic"):
        raise ValueError(f"boundary must be 'wall' or 'periodic': got "
                         f"{boundary!r}.")
    if boundary == "wall" and not (0.0 <= e_wall <= 1.0):
        raise ValueError(f"e_wall (wall restitution) must be in [0, 1]: "
                         f"got {e_wall}.")


# Periodic-domain helpers

def wrap_positions(pos, L, W):
    """Map positions in place to the periodic box ``[0, L) x [0, W)``."""
    pos[:, 0] = np.mod(pos[:, 0], L)
    pos[:, 1] = np.mod(pos[:, 1], W)
    return pos


def surface_concentration_relation(L, W, N=None, radius=None, eta=None,
                                   round_N=True):
    """Return the missing quantity in ``eta = N pi r^2 / (L W)``.

    Exactly two of ``N``, ``radius``, and ``eta`` must be provided.
    """
    if L <= 0 or W <= 0:
        raise ValueError("Dimensions should be positive.")
    area = L * W
    given = [N is not None, radius is not None, eta is not None]
    if sum(given) != 2:
        raise ValueError("Exactly two of N, radius, eta must be provided.")
    if N is not None and N <= 0:
        raise ValueError("N should be a positive integer.")
    if radius is not None and radius <= 0:
        raise ValueError("Radius should be positive.")
    if eta is not None and not (0 < eta < 1):
        raise ValueError("eta should lie between 0 and 1.")
    if eta is None:
        eta = N * np.pi * radius ** 2 / area
    elif N is None:
        N_float = eta * area / (np.pi * radius ** 2)
        N = int(np.ceil(N_float)) if round_N else N_float
    elif radius is None:
        radius = np.sqrt(eta * area / (N * np.pi))
    return {"L": L, "W": W, "area": area, "N": N,
            "radius": radius, "eta": eta}


# Numba physics kernels

@njit(cache=False)
def _resolve_collision_kernel(vi0, vi1, mi, vj0, vj1, mj,
                              e_ball, dx0, dx1, eps):
    """Return velocities after a normal hard-disk collision."""
    dist = (dx0 * dx0 + dx1 * dx1) ** 0.5
    if dist < eps:
        return vi0, vi1, vj0, vj1
    n0 = dx0 / dist
    n1 = dx1 / dist
    rel = (vi0 - vj0) * n0 + (vi1 - vj1) * n1   # relative normal velocity
    if rel >= 0.0:                              # separating
        return vi0, vi1, vj0, vj1
    f = (1.0 + e_ball) * rel / (mi + mj)
    return (vi0 - mj * f * n0, vi1 - mj * f * n1,
            vj0 + mi * f * n0, vj1 + mi * f * n1)


@njit(cache=False)
def _earliest_ball_collision(pi, pj, pos, vel, radii,
                             L, W, periodic, t_max, eps):
    """Find the earliest pair contact during a constant-velocity drift.

    An overlapping, approaching pair contacts at ``t = 0``; separating pairs
    are ignored. ``best_k = -1`` indicates that no contact was found.
    """
    best_t = t_max
    best_k = -1
    for k in range(pi.shape[0]):
        i = pi[k]
        j = pj[k]
        dx0 = pos[i, 0] - pos[j, 0]
        dx1 = pos[i, 1] - pos[j, 1]
        if periodic:
            dx0 -= L * np.floor(dx0 / L + 0.5)
            dx1 -= W * np.floor(dx1 / W + 0.5)
        dv0 = vel[i, 0] - vel[j, 0]
        dv1 = vel[i, 1] - vel[j, 1]
        R = radii[i] + radii[j]
        a = dv0 * dv0 + dv1 * dv1
        if a < eps:
            continue
        b = 2.0 * (dx0 * dv0 + dx1 * dv1)
        c = dx0 * dx0 + dx1 * dx1 - R * R
        if c <= 0.0:                       # currently overlapping
            if b < 0.0 and best_t > 0.0:   # approaching -> collide now
                best_t = 0.0
                best_k = k
            continue
        disc = b * b - 4.0 * a * c
        if disc < 0.0:
            continue
        sq = disc ** 0.5
        t1 = (-b - sq) / (2.0 * a)          # entry root
        if t1 > eps and t1 <= best_t and b < 0.0:
            best_t = t1
            best_k = k
    return best_t, best_k


@njit(cache=False)
def _earliest_wall_collision(pos, vel, radii, L, W, t_max, eps):
    """Find the earliest wall contact within ``[0, t_max]``.

    The returned axis is 0 for an x wall and 1 for a z wall.
    """
    best_t = t_max
    best_i = -1
    best_axis = -1
    for i in range(pos.shape[0]):
        x = pos[i, 0]; y = pos[i, 1]
        vx = vel[i, 0]; vy = vel[i, 1]
        r = radii[i]
        if vx < -eps:
            t = (r - x) / vx
            if eps < t <= best_t:
                best_t = t; best_i = i; best_axis = 0
        if vx > eps:
            t = (L - r - x) / vx
            if eps < t <= best_t:
                best_t = t; best_i = i; best_axis = 0
        if vy < -eps:
            t = (r - y) / vy
            if eps < t <= best_t:
                best_t = t; best_i = i; best_axis = 1
        if vy > eps:
            t = (W - r - y) / vy
            if eps < t <= best_t:
                best_t = t; best_i = i; best_axis = 1
    return best_t, best_i, best_axis

# Periodic cell list for candidate pairs. ``fast_pairs.py`` can replace this
# routine with a ``cKDTree`` search when the cutoff is much smaller than the
# mean particle spacing.

@njit(cache=False)
def _celllist_pairs_periodic(pos, L, W, r_query):
    """Return unordered pairs with periodic separation below ``r_query``.

    Cells are at least ``r_query`` wide, so only the local nine-cell
    neighbourhood must be searched. The result is returned as two ``int64``
    index arrays.
    """
    N = pos.shape[0]
    # Use at least one cell per axis, with cell width >= r_query.
    ncx = int(L / r_query)
    ncy = int(W / r_query)
    if ncx < 1:
        ncx = 1
    if ncy < 1:
        ncy = 1
    # With fewer than three cells, periodic neighbours repeat; use brute force.
    if ncx < 3 or ncy < 3:
        pi_list = []
        pj_list = []
        r2 = r_query * r_query
        for i in range(N):
            for j in range(i + 1, N):
                dx = pos[i, 0] - pos[j, 0]
                dy = pos[i, 1] - pos[j, 1]
                dx -= L * np.floor(dx / L + 0.5)
                dy -= W * np.floor(dy / W + 0.5)
                if dx * dx + dy * dy < r2:
                    pi_list.append(i)
                    pj_list.append(j)
        npair = len(pi_list)
        pi = np.empty(npair, dtype=np.int64)
        pj = np.empty(npair, dtype=np.int64)
        for k in range(npair):
            pi[k] = pi_list[k]
            pj[k] = pj_list[k]
        return pi, pj

    csx = L / ncx
    csy = W / ncy
    ncell = ncx * ncy

    # Bin particles by counting sort.
    cell_of = np.empty(N, dtype=np.int64)
    counts = np.zeros(ncell, dtype=np.int64)
    for p in range(N):
        cx = int(pos[p, 0] / csx)
        cy = int(pos[p, 1] / csy)
        if cx >= ncx:
            cx = ncx - 1
        if cy >= ncy:
            cy = ncy - 1
        if cx < 0:
            cx = 0
        if cy < 0:
            cy = 0
        c = cy * ncx + cx
        cell_of[p] = c
        counts[c] += 1

    # Prefix sums locate each cell in the sorted particle list.
    starts = np.zeros(ncell + 1, dtype=np.int64)
    for c in range(ncell):
        starts[c + 1] = starts[c] + counts[c]
    # Fill the sorted particle-index array.
    fill = starts[:ncell].copy()
    order = np.empty(N, dtype=np.int64)
    for p in range(N):
        c = cell_of[p]
        order[fill[c]] = p
        fill[c] += 1

    r2 = r_query * r_query
    pi_list = []
    pj_list = []

    # The forward half-stencil visits each neighbouring cell pair once.
    off = ((0, 0), (1, 0), (-1, 1), (0, 1), (1, 1))

    for cy in range(ncy):
        for cx in range(ncx):
            c = cy * ncx + cx
            s_c = starts[c]
            e_c = starts[c + 1]
            for oidx in range(5):
                dxc = off[oidx][0]
                dyc = off[oidx][1]
                ncx_ = cx + dxc
                ncy_ = cy + dyc
                # Wrap cell coordinates periodically.
                if ncx_ < 0:
                    ncx_ += ncx
                elif ncx_ >= ncx:
                    ncx_ -= ncx
                if ncy_ < 0:
                    ncy_ += ncy
                elif ncy_ >= ncy:
                    ncy_ -= ncy
                cc = ncy_ * ncx + ncx_
                s_n = starts[cc]
                e_n = starts[cc + 1]

                if oidx == 0:
                    # Test unique pairs within one cell.
                    for a in range(s_c, e_c):
                        i = order[a]
                        for b in range(a + 1, e_c):
                            j = order[b]
                            dx = pos[i, 0] - pos[j, 0]
                            dy = pos[i, 1] - pos[j, 1]
                            dx -= L * np.floor(dx / L + 0.5)
                            dy -= W * np.floor(dy / W + 0.5)
                            if dx * dx + dy * dy < r2:
                                if i < j:
                                    pi_list.append(i); pj_list.append(j)
                                else:
                                    pi_list.append(j); pj_list.append(i)
                else:
                    # Test all pairs across distinct cells.
                    for a in range(s_c, e_c):
                        i = order[a]
                        for b in range(s_n, e_n):
                            j = order[b]
                            dx = pos[i, 0] - pos[j, 0]
                            dy = pos[i, 1] - pos[j, 1]
                            dx -= L * np.floor(dx / L + 0.5)
                            dy -= W * np.floor(dy / W + 0.5)
                            if dx * dx + dy * dy < r2:
                                if i < j:
                                    pi_list.append(i); pj_list.append(j)
                                else:
                                    pi_list.append(j); pj_list.append(i)

    npair = len(pi_list)
    pi = np.empty(npair, dtype=np.int64)
    pj = np.empty(npair, dtype=np.int64)
    for k in range(npair):
        pi[k] = pi_list[k]
        pj[k] = pj_list[k]
    return pi, pj

@njit(cache=False)
def _push_apart(pi, pj, pos, radii, inv_m, L, W, periodic, eps, iterations):
    """Separate any residual overlapping pairs, split by inverse mass."""
    for _ in range(iterations):
        for k in range(pi.shape[0]):
            i = pi[k]; j = pj[k]
            dx0 = pos[i, 0] - pos[j, 0]
            dx1 = pos[i, 1] - pos[j, 1]
            if periodic:
                dx0 -= L * np.floor(dx0 / L + 0.5)
                dx1 -= W * np.floor(dx1 / W + 0.5)
            d = (dx0 * dx0 + dx1 * dx1) ** 0.5
            R = radii[i] + radii[j]
            if d < R - eps:
                if d < eps:
                    n0 = 1.0; n1 = 0.0; d = eps
                else:
                    n0 = dx0 / d; n1 = dx1 / d
                pen = R - d
                wi = inv_m[i]; wj = inv_m[j]; ws = wi + wj
                pos[i, 0] += n0 * pen * wi / ws
                pos[i, 1] += n1 * pen * wi / ws
                pos[j, 0] -= n0 * pen * wj / ws
                pos[j, 1] -= n1 * pen * wj / ws


def warmup_numba():
    """Compile the Numba kernels with a small representative state."""
    if not NUMBA_AVAILABLE:
        return
    print("Compiling Numba kernels... ", end="", flush=True)
    t0 = _time.perf_counter()
    pos = np.array([[1.0, 1.0], [2.0, 1.0]])
    vel = np.array([[1.0, 0.0], [-1.0, 0.0]])
    radii = np.array([0.3, 0.3])
    inv_m = np.array([1.0, 1.0])
    pi = np.array([0]); pj = np.array([1])
    _resolve_collision_kernel(1.0, 0.0, 1.0, -1.0, 0.0, 1.0,
                              1.0, 1.0, 0.0, 1e-12)
    _earliest_ball_collision(pi, pj, pos, vel, radii, 10.0, 5.0,
                             True, 1.0, 1e-12)
    _earliest_wall_collision(pos, vel, radii, 10.0, 5.0, 1.0, 1e-12)
    _push_apart(pi, pj, pos, radii, inv_m, 10.0, 5.0, True, 1e-12, 1)
    print(f"done in {_time.perf_counter() - t0:.1f}s")


# Fluid velocity fields: U_f(X, t) -> (N, 2)

def quiescent_fluid():
    """Return a quiescent carrier field, ``U_f = 0``."""
    def field(pos, t):
        return np.zeros_like(pos)
    return field


def uniform_fluid(U0):
    """Return a uniform carrier field, ``U_f = U0``."""
    U0 = np.asarray(U0, dtype=float)

    def field(pos, t):
        out = np.empty_like(pos)
        out[:] = U0
        return out
    return field


def shear_fluid(shear_rate):
    """Return the linear shear field ``U_f = (shear_rate z, 0)``."""
    def field(pos, t):
        out = np.zeros_like(pos)
        out[:, 0] = shear_rate * pos[:, 1]
        return out
    return field


def taylor_green_fluid(U0, L, W):
    """Return a steady Taylor--Green field periodic over the box."""
    kx = 2.0 * np.pi / L
    ky = 2.0 * np.pi / W

    def field(pos, t):
        x = pos[:, 0]; y = pos[:, 1]
        out = np.empty_like(pos)
        out[:, 0] = U0 * np.sin(kx * x) * np.cos(ky * y)
        out[:, 1] = -U0 * np.cos(kx * x) * np.sin(ky * y)
        return out
    return field


# Diagnostics

def kinetic_energy(vel, masses):
    """Return total translational kinetic energy, ``sum_i m_i |U_i|^2 / 2``."""
    vel = np.asarray(vel, dtype=float)
    masses = np.asarray(masses, dtype=float)
    return 0.5 * np.sum(masses * np.sum(vel ** 2, axis=1))


def total_momentum(vel, masses):
    """Return total linear momentum as an ``(x, z)`` pair."""
    vel = np.asarray(vel, dtype=float)
    masses = np.asarray(masses, dtype=float)
    return np.sum(masses[:, None] * vel, axis=0)


def polarization(vel, eps=1e-12):
    """Return directional order, circular mean, and circular spread.

    ``phi = |<U/|U|>|`` lies in ``[0, 1]``; both angles are in radians.
    """
    speed = np.linalg.norm(vel, axis=1)
    moving = speed > eps
    if not np.any(moving):
        return 0.0, np.nan, np.nan
    u = vel[moving] / speed[moving, None]
    phi = float(np.linalg.norm(u.mean(axis=0)))
    angles = np.arctan2(vel[moving, 1], vel[moving, 0])
    c, s = np.cos(angles).mean(), np.sin(angles).mean()
    mean_angle = float(np.arctan2(s, c))
    R = np.hypot(c, s)
    std_angle = float(np.sqrt(-2.0 * np.log(max(R, eps))))
    return phi, mean_angle, std_angle


def COM(positions, masses=None):
    """Centre of mass (plain mean when masses is None). Not periodic-aware."""
    positions = np.asarray(positions, dtype=float)
    if masses is None:
        return np.mean(positions, axis=0)
    masses = np.asarray(masses, dtype=float)
    return np.sum(masses[:, None] * positions, axis=0) / np.sum(masses)


# State checks


def check_initial_data(pos, vel, L, W, radii, eps=1e-12, boundary="periodic"):
    """Validate particle placement before integration.

    Wall-bounded particles must lie fully inside the box, and particles may
    not overlap. Periodic separations use the minimum-image convention.
    """
    pos   = np.asarray(pos,   dtype=float)
    vel   = np.asarray(vel,   dtype=float)
    radii = np.asarray(radii, dtype=float)
    N = len(pos)
    if boundary == "wall":
        for i in range(N):
            x, y = pos[i]; r = radii[i]
            if x < r - eps or x > L - r + eps:
                raise ValueError(f"Particle {i} sticks out of the box in x "
                                 f"(x = {x:.6g}, radius {r:.3g}, L = {L}).")
            if y < r - eps or y > W - r + eps:
                raise ValueError(f"Particle {i} sticks out of the box in z "
                                 f"(z = {y:.6g}, radius {r:.3g}, W = {W}).")
    else:
        wrap_positions(pos, L, W)

    # Check overlaps one vectorized particle row at a time, using minimum images
    # for a periodic domain.
    if boundary == "periodic":
        for i in range(N):
            dx = pos[i+1:, 0] - pos[i, 0]
            dy = pos[i+1:, 1] - pos[i, 1]
            dx -= L * np.round(dx / L)
            dy -= W * np.round(dy / W)
            dist     = np.sqrt(dx*dx + dy*dy)
            min_dist = radii[i] + radii[i+1:]
            bad      = np.where(dist < min_dist - eps)[0]
            if len(bad) > 0:
                j = i + 1 + bad[0]
                raise ValueError(
                    f"Particles {i} and {j} overlap in the initial state; "
                    f"seed with overlap rejection (dns_fluid.seed_particles).")
    else:
        for i in range(N):
            dx   = pos[i+1:, 0] - pos[i, 0]
            dy   = pos[i+1:, 1] - pos[i, 1]
            dist = np.sqrt(dx*dx + dy*dy)
            min_dist = radii[i] + radii[i+1:]
            bad  = np.where(dist < min_dist - eps)[0]
            if len(bad) > 0:
                j = i + 1 + bad[0]
                raise ValueError(
                    f"Particles {i} and {j} overlap in the initial state; "
                    f"seed with overlap rejection (dns_fluid.seed_particles).")


def check_runtime_overlaps(pos, radii, L, W, boundary, eps=1e-6):
    """Largest pair penetration depth (sum of radii minus distance) in the
    current configuration; 0 or negative means no overlap. Diagnostic only."""
    N = len(pos)
    worst = 0.0
    for i in range(N):
        dx = pos[i + 1:, 0] - pos[i, 0]
        dy = pos[i + 1:, 1] - pos[i, 1]
        if boundary == "periodic":
            dx -= L * np.round(dx / L)
            dy -= W * np.round(dy / W)
        dist = np.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy)
        min_dist = radii[i] + radii[i + 1:]
        pen = min_dist - dist
        if len(pen) and pen.max() > worst:
            worst = pen.max()
    return worst


# Kick--drift simulation

def simulate_drag_friction(
    # ── particle state and domain ───────────────────────────────────
    positions, velocities, masses, radii,
    L, W, T_final, dt,
    # Drag and optional centre-speed substrate friction
    tau, mu_frict=0.0, g=0.0,
    fluid_velocity=None,
    # ── contact ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    e_ball=1.0, e_wall=1.0,
    boundary="periodic",
    collisions_enabled=True,      # False disables contact but retains radii,
                                  # preserving paired initial conditions.
    # ── storage of the trajectory ───────────────────────────────────
    stream_dir=None,              # None stores histories in memory; a path
                                  # stores frames in .npy memory maps.
    stream_dtype="float32",       # dtype of the streamed copy (the
                                  # integration itself is always float64)
    # ── numerics ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    integrator="strang",          # "strang" (2nd order) or "lie" (1st)
    collision_cap_factor=100,     # max collisions per step = factor * N
    eps=1e-12,                    # geometric tolerance of the event search
    record_target=2000,           # approx. number of stored states
    snapshot_times=None,          # extra exact-time position snapshots
    progress_interval=500,        # print a progress line every n steps
                                  # (0 = silent)
    debug_overlaps=False,         # check overlaps 50 times per run (slow)
    csv_filename="diagnostics_log.csv",   # per-frame diagnostics table,
                                          # written beside this file
    log_collisions=True,          # keep the per-contact event log
    max_collision_log=5_000_000,  # rows kept in that log before truncation
    # Rotation: models B--E
    rotation=False,               # False = model A (no angular state at all)
    fluid_omega=None,             # callable (pos, t) -> (N, 3): fluid angular
                                  # velocity omega_f = 1/2 curl U_f at the
                                  # particle centre, stored as
                                  # (about streamwise, about spanwise, about
                                  # wall-normal). None -> omega_f = 0.
    omega0=None,                  # (N, 3) initial spins; None -> zeros
    e_tan=1.0,                    # e_parallel in [-1, 1]. 1 = smooth contact
                                  # (model B); < 1 gives tangential restitution
                                  # (model C).
    mu_pp=-1.0,                   # Coulomb cap |P_par| <= mu_pp * P_perp
                                  # (models D, E). Negative = no cap (B, C).
    # Wall friction: model E
    wall_friction=False,          # continuous Coulomb friction at the
                                  # sphere-wall point contact: sliding at
                                  # mu_w N, N = m g_eff, rolling when the
                                  # required force stays below mu_w N
    mu_w=0.0,                     # wall friction coefficient (static = sliding)
    g_eff=0.0,                    # (1 - rho_f/rho_p) g in the run's units; only
                                  # the product mu_w * g_eff enters
    # Diagnostic vertical impulse
    pz_threshold=0.0,             # > 0: count, per recorded frame, the
                                  # contacts whose hypothetical vertical
                                  # impulse |Pz~| (per unit mass, i.e. the
                                  # vertical velocity jump an unconstrained
                                  # 3-D law would give) exceeds this value
                                  # for the diagnostic exceedance rate.
):
    """Integrate hard spheres on a wall-parallel plane.

    Each Strang step consists of a half kick, a force-free drift with exact
    collision events, and a second half kick. See the module docstring for the
    governing equations and model hierarchy.

    Parameters (all lengths, times and velocities in the caller's units)
    ----------
    positions, velocities : (N, 2) arrays, in-plane (x, z) components.
    masses, radii         : (N,) arrays, all positive. The pipeline uses m = 1.
    L, W                  : box size along x (streamwise) and z (spanwise).
    T_final, dt           : simulated time and engine time step.
    tau                   : particle response time tau_p, scalar or (N,).
                            Must be > 0; a tracer is run with tau = 1e-12.
    fluid_velocity        : callable (pos (N, 2), t) -> U_f (N, 2).
                            None = fluid at rest.
    e_ball, e_wall        : normal restitution, particle-particle and
                            particle-wall (wall used only if boundary="wall").
    boundary              : ``"periodic"`` or ``"wall"``.
    collisions_enabled    : False runs the collisionless control leg.
    rotation ... pz_threshold : the model hierarchy; see the table in the
                            module docstring for which values give A-E.
    mu_frict, g           : optional centre-speed substrate friction.

    Returns
    -------
    dict. The keys used downstream are
      event_times                 recorded times (len = n_frames)
      position_history            (n_frames, N, 2)   list or memmap
      velocity_history            (n_frames, N, 2)
      omega_history, omega_final  spins, or None for model A
      collision_events            (n_contacts, 12) event log, columns
                                  described in the comment above _EV_COLS
      total_collisions, cap_hits  counters for the whole run
      diagnostics                 pandas DataFrame, one row per frame
      wall_work, pz_exceed        model-E and sec.-8 totals
    The result also records the input parameters needed to interpret the run.

    Raises
    ------
    ValueError for inadmissible inputs (see validate_parameters, and the
    model-consistency checks below); OSError if the stream folder cannot
    be created.
    """
    validate_parameters(L, W, masses, radii, T_final, dt,
                        tau, mu_frict, g, e_ball, e_wall, boundary)
    if not (-1.0 <= float(e_tan) <= 1.0):
        raise ValueError(f"e_tan (tangential restitution) must be in "
                         f"[-1, 1]: got {e_tan}.")
    if float(mu_w) < 0.0 or float(g_eff) < 0.0:
        raise ValueError(f"mu_w and g_eff must be >= 0: got mu_w = {mu_w}, "
                         f"g_eff = {g_eff}.")

    pos = np.asarray(positions, dtype=float).copy()
    vel = np.asarray(velocities, dtype=float).copy()
    masses = np.asarray(masses, dtype=float)
    radii = np.asarray(radii, dtype=float)
    N = len(pos)

    tau = (np.full(N, float(tau)) if np.isscalar(tau)
           else np.asarray(tau, dtype=float))
    inv_m = 1.0 / masses
    r_max = float(radii.max())
    periodic = (boundary == "periodic")
    boxsize = np.array([L, W]) if periodic else None

    if fluid_velocity is None:
        fluid_velocity = quiescent_fluid()

    if periodic:
        wrap_positions(pos, L, W)
    check_initial_data(pos, vel, L, W, radii, eps=eps, boundary=boundary)

    if not collisions_enabled:
        print(f"  COLLISIONLESS MODE: radius kept at {r_max:.4e} for drag "
              f"sampling, seeding and phi; particles pass through one "
              f"another. total_collisions will be 0 by construction.")

    # Angular state is stored as (streamwise, spanwise, wall-normal). A
    # horizontal contact impulse changes only the wall-normal component because
    # n x P is wall-normal; the in-plane spin components remain passive.
    # The right-handed frame has e_y directed away from the wall and e_z along
    # the spanwise coordinate pos[:, 1]. The contact tangent is
    # t = e_y x n = (n_z, -n_x) in engine (x, z) components, so a particle
    # spinning at omg[:, 2] = Omega has surface velocity r * Omega * t at
    # the point r * n of its surface.
    # For a homogeneous sphere, I = 2/5 m r^2 and the Stokes torque gives
    # rotational response time is tau_rot = I/(8 pi rho nu r^3) = 3/10 tau_p.
    # These material relations are fixed across all models.
    rotation = bool(rotation)
    e_tan = float(e_tan)
    mu_pp = float(mu_pp)
    kappa = INERTIA_COEFF
    tau_rot = TAU_ROT_FACTOR * tau                        # (N,) array
    inertia = kappa * masses * radii ** 2                 # (N,) array
    if rotation:
        omg = (np.zeros((N, 3)) if omega0 is None
               else np.asarray(omega0, dtype=float).reshape(N, 3).copy())
        if fluid_omega is None:
            print("  ROTATION ON with fluid_omega=None: fluid torque is "
                  "zero, spins only relax to rest and exchange at contact.")
        rough = e_tan < 1.0 or mu_pp >= 0.0
        print(f"  ROTATION ON: tau_rot = 3/10 tau_p, I = 2/5 m r^2 (solid "
              f"sphere, fixed), e_tan = {e_tan:g}"
              + (f", mu_pp = {mu_pp:g}" if mu_pp >= 0.0 else "")
              + ("  -> model " + ("D (E if wall friction is on, see below)"
                                  if mu_pp >= 0.0 else "C")
                 + ", tangential impulse at contact" if rough else
                 "  -> model B (smooth contact, spins passive)"))
    else:
        omg = None
        rough = False
        if e_tan != 1.0 or mu_pp >= 0.0:
            raise ValueError(f"e_tan = {e_tan:g} / mu_pp = {mu_pp:g} select "
                             f"model C or D, which need rotation=True: a "
                             f"tangential impulse changes the spins. Use "
                             f"e_tan = 1 and mu_pp < 0 for model A.")
    # Wall friction (model E). The contact arm is -r e_y, so the slip is
    #     s = U - r omega x e_y = (U_x + r omega_z, U_z - r omega_x)
    # (omega_x = omg[:, 0] about the streamwise axis, omega_z = omg[:, 1]
    # about the spanwise axis). A wall force F changes the slip at the rate
    # (1/m + r^2/I) F = (1 + 1/kappa) F / m  (= 7 F / 2m for a solid sphere),
    # which is what SLIP_FAC below is. Only mu_w * g_eff enters.
    wall_friction = bool(wall_friction)
    mu_w, g_eff = float(mu_w), float(g_eff)
    mu_w_g = mu_w * g_eff
    SLIP_FAC = 1.0 + 1.0 / kappa
    wall_work = 0.0                 # cumulative work of the wall force, <= 0
    rolling_frac = 0.0              # fraction rolling after the last substep
    if wall_friction:
        if not rotation:
            raise ValueError("wall_friction=True (model E) needs "
                             "rotation=True: rolling is a statement about "
                             "the spins.")
        print(f"  WALL FRICTION ON (model E): mu_w = {mu_w:g}, g_eff = {g_eff:g}"
              f" -> mu_w g_eff = {mu_w_g:g}; sliding decelerates the contact "
              f"slip at (1 + 1/kappa) mu_w g_eff = {SLIP_FAC * mu_w_g:.4g}, "
              f"rolling when the slip can be removed within a substep")
        if mu_w_g == 0.0:
            print("  NOTE: mu_w * g_eff = 0 -- the wall exerts no force; "
                  "this run is physically model D.")

    n_steps = int(np.ceil(T_final / dt))
    record_every = max(1, n_steps // max(record_target, 1))
    cap = collision_cap_factor * N

    # Requested snapshot times.
    snap_queue = []
    if snapshot_times is not None:
        snap_queue = sorted(float(s) for s in snapshot_times
                            if 0.0 <= float(s) <= T_final)
    snap_idx = 0
    snapshots = []

    # Histories remain compatible with interpolation and plotting routines.
    # When stream_dir is set, frames are preallocated as disk-backed arrays;
    # integration remains float64 and only the stored copy uses stream_dtype.
    # Two extra slots cover both the initial frame and a final frame when the
    # step count is not divisible by record_every. The returned slice removes
    # any unused slot.
    _n_frames = n_steps // record_every + 2
    _stream = None
    if stream_dir is not None:
        import os as _os
        from numpy.lib.format import open_memmap as _omm
        _os.makedirs(stream_dir, exist_ok=True)
        _shape = (_n_frames, N, 2)
        try:
            _stream = {
                "pos": _omm(_os.path.join(stream_dir, "positions.npy"),
                            mode="w+", dtype=np.dtype(stream_dtype),
                            shape=_shape),
                "vel": _omm(_os.path.join(stream_dir, "velocities.npy"),
                            mode="w+", dtype=np.dtype(stream_dtype),
                            shape=_shape),
                "omg": (_omm(_os.path.join(stream_dir, "spins.npy"),
                             mode="w+", dtype=np.dtype(stream_dtype),
                             shape=(_n_frames, N, 3)) if rotation else None),
                "k": 0, "dir": stream_dir, "n": _n_frames,
            }
        except OSError as _e:
            # On Windows, errno 22 can indicate that a previous run still has
            # the target memory maps open. Errno 28 indicates insufficient disk
            # space. The raised message explains both cases.
            raise OSError(
                f"streaming: cannot create memmaps in {stream_dir} "
                f"({_e}).\n"
                f"  errno 22 on Windows usually means a PREVIOUS run's "
                f"frames at this path are still memory-mapped by this "
                f"Python session (Spyder keeps results alive). Restart the "
                f"kernel, or use a fresh stream folder.\n"
                f"  errno 28 means the disk is full: this allocation needs "
                f"{2 * _n_frames * N * 2 * np.dtype(stream_dtype).itemsize / 1e9:.2f} "
                f"GB free.") from _e
        print(f"  STREAMING {_n_frames} frames x {N} particles to "
              f"{stream_dir}/ as {stream_dtype} "
              f"({2 * _n_frames * N * 2 * np.dtype(stream_dtype).itemsize / 1e9:.2f} GB on disk, "
              f"~{2 * N * 2 * 8 / 1e6:.1f} MB in RAM)")

    def _record_frame(_p, _v, _o=None):
        """Append one frame to memory or to the configured disk arrays."""
        if _stream is None:
            position_history.append(_p.copy())
            velocity_history.append(_v.copy())
            if _o is not None:
                omega_history.append(_o.copy())
            return
        k = _stream["k"]
        if k >= _stream["n"]:
            # The allocation is an upper bound; exceeding it would lose frames.
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"streaming: frame {k} exceeds the {_stream['n']}-frame "
                f"allocation (n_steps={n_steps}, record_every={record_every}). "
                f"Frames would be silently dropped -- aborting instead.")
        _stream["pos"][k] = _p
        _stream["vel"][k] = _v
        if _o is not None and _stream["omg"] is not None:
            _stream["omg"][k] = _o
        _stream["k"] = k + 1

    event_times = [0.0]
    position_history = [] if stream_dir is not None else [pos.copy()]
    velocity_history = [] if stream_dir is not None else [vel.copy()]
    omega_history = ([] if (stream_dir is not None or not rotation)
                     else [omg.copy()])
    if stream_dir is not None:
        _record_frame(pos, vel, omg)
    energy_history = [kinetic_energy(vel, masses)]
    momentum_history = [total_momentum(vel, masses).copy()]
    phi0, ma0, sa0 = polarization(vel)
    polarization_history = [phi0]
    mean_angle_history = [ma0]
    std_angle_history = [sa0]
    diag_rows = []
    _pz_prev = 0
    pz_threshold = float(pz_threshold)

    total_collisions = 0
    cap_hits = 0
    t = 0.0
    t_start = _time.perf_counter()

    # Collision-event log. Columns 0--6 are t, i, j, line-of-centres angle,
    # normal relative velocity, and the x/z contact midpoint. Columns 7--11
    # hold rotational-contact diagnostics and are zero for model A:
    #   7  w_t     pre-collision tangential relative contact velocity
    #              U^-_{c,ij,par} = (U_j - U_i).t - (r_i w_i + r_j w_j)
    #              (translational slip only when rotation is off)
    #   8  P_perp  normal impulse magnitude on j  (>0, separates)
    #   9  P_par   tangential impulse on j along t (0 for models A, B)
    #  10  Pz_diag hypothetical vertical impulse on j from a 3-D contact law;
    #              recorded for analysis but not applied
    #  11  Uy_diag the vertical component of the full pre-collision relative
    #              contact velocity, U~_y = -[r_i (w_i,z n_x - w_i,x n_z)
    #              + r_j (w_j,z n_x - w_j,x n_z)] (sec. 8, step 1). With
    #              w_t gives |U~_T| = hypot(w_t, U~_y), allowing Pz~ to be
    #              recomputed for other (e_tan, mu_pp) values.
    # The array representation uses 96 MB per million rows; max_collision_log
    # bounds this allocation and sets collision_log_truncated when reached.
    _EV_COLS = 12
    _pz_exceed = 0                  # contacts with |Pz~| > pz_threshold
    _pz_abs_sum = 0.0               # running sum of |Pz~| (rough contacts)
    _pz_abs_max = 0.0
    _ev_buf = (np.empty((65536, _EV_COLS), dtype=float) if log_collisions
               else None)
    _ev_n = 0
    _ev_truncated = False

    # Per-particle mean-free-path bookkeeping.
    lambda_current = np.zeros(N, dtype=float)   # path since last bb collision
    lambda_sum = np.zeros(N, dtype=float)       # sum of completed free paths
    lambda_count = np.zeros(N, dtype=int)       # number of completed segments
    bb_count = np.zeros(N, dtype=int)           # bb collisions per particle
    lambda_times = [0.0]
    lambda_mean_history = [np.nan]

    def compute_mfp():
        valid = lambda_count > 0
        if np.any(valid):
            per = np.full(N, np.nan)
            per[valid] = lambda_sum[valid] / lambda_count[valid]
            return float(np.nanmean(per))
        return np.nan

    # Continuous-force kick.
    def apply_kick(dt_k, t_cur):
        """Advance continuous forces over one fixed-position half-kick."""
        nonlocal vel, omg, wall_work, rolling_frac
        # Freeze the carrier fields at the current particle positions.
        Uf = fluid_velocity(pos, t_cur)
        decay = np.exp(-dt_k / tau)[:, None]
        _wall = wall_friction and mu_w_g > 0.0
        if _wall:                      # Model E selects the regime from the
            _vel_pre = vel.copy()      # state entering the half-kick.
            _omg_pre = omg.copy()
        # Translational Stokes relaxation, integrated exactly:
        #       dU/dt = (U_f - U)/tau_p   ->   U(h) = U_f + (U_0 - U_f) e^{-h/tau_p}
        vel = Uf + (vel - Uf) * decay
        # Rotational Stokes relaxation, also integrated exactly:
        #       d omega/dt = (omega_f - omega)/tau_rot,  tau_rot = (3/10) tau_p
        #       -> omega(h) = omega_f + (omega_0 - omega_f) e^{-h/tau_rot}
        # This update does not modify position or translational velocity.
        # fluid_omega=None represents zero fluid angular velocity.
        Of = None
        if rotation:
            if fluid_omega is not None:
                Of = np.asarray(fluid_omega(pos, t_cur), dtype=float)
                if Of.shape != (N, 3):
                    raise ValueError(f"fluid_omega must return (N, 3), got "
                                     f"{Of.shape}")
                omg = Of + (omg - Of) * np.exp(-dt_k / tau_rot)[:, None]
            else:
                omg = omg * np.exp(-dt_k / tau_rot)[:, None]
        if _wall:
            # Model-E wall contact over one half-kick.
            # Slip at the wall contact (arm -r e_y):
            #     s = U - r omega x e_y = (U_x + r omega_z, U_z - r omega_x)   [10]
            # with omega_x = omg[:, 0], omega_z = omg[:, 1]. A wall force F
            # changes s at the rate (1/m + r^2/I) F = SLIP_FAC F/m,
            # SLIP_FAC = 1 + 1/kappa = 7/2 for a solid sphere            [13].
            # The entering state determines the regime while U_f and omega_f
            # remain frozen. For rolling (|s_0| = 0):
            #    F_req = -(2m/7)(A - r B x e_y),  |F_req| <= mu_w m g_eff  [14]
            #    A = (U_f - U)/tau_p,  B = (omega_f - omega)/tau_rot,
            #    (A - r B x e_y) = (A_x + r B_z, A_z - r B_x).
            #    With r omega x e_y = U, the motion reduces to the linear ODE
            #      dU/dt = -lam U + c,
            #      lam = (5/7)/tau_p + (2/7)/tau_rot,
            #      c   = (5/7) U_f/tau_p + (2/7)(r/tau_rot)(omega_f x e_y),
            #      omega_f x e_y = (-omega_f,z, +omega_f,x) in (x, z);
            #    integrated exactly over the half-kick. The constraints then
            #    give omega_x = U_z/r and omega_z = -U_x/r. A particle slides
            #    if the static bound fails at the start; a later failure is
            #    detected at the next half-kick.
            # For sliding, the free relaxations remain in effect and
            #    Coulomb force F = -mu_w N s/|s| acts for the whole half-kick
            #    and by [13] shrinks the slip along itself by
            #    SLIP_FAC mu_w g_eff dt_k. If that would overshoot zero, the
            #    slip is removed by the stick impulse J = -(2m/7) s.
            #
            # Either way, per unit mass j = J/m:  U += j  [11], and by [12]
            #   d omega_x = -(r/I) J_z = -j_z/(kappa r),
            #   d omega_z = +(r/I) J_x = +j_x/(kappa r),   omega_y untouched.
            _Of = Of if Of is not None else np.zeros((N, 3))
            # slip entering the half-kick, Eq. [10]
            _sx0 = _vel_pre[:, 0] + radii * _omg_pre[:, 1]
            _sz0 = _vel_pre[:, 1] - radii * _omg_pre[:, 0]
            _scale = (np.hypot(_vel_pre[:, 0], _vel_pre[:, 1])
                      + radii * np.hypot(_omg_pre[:, 0], _omg_pre[:, 1]))
            _was_rolling = np.hypot(_sx0, _sz0) <= 1e-9 * _scale + 1e-300
            # required force per unit mass, Eq. [14], from the entering state
            _Ax = (Uf[:, 0] - _vel_pre[:, 0]) / tau
            _Az = (Uf[:, 1] - _vel_pre[:, 1]) / tau
            _Bx = (_Of[:, 0] - _omg_pre[:, 0]) / tau_rot
            _Bz = (_Of[:, 1] - _omg_pre[:, 1]) / tau_rot
            _Gx = _Ax + radii * _Bz                 # (A - r B x e_y)_x
            _Gz = _Az - radii * _Bx                 # (A - r B x e_y)_z
            # |F_req|/m = |A - r B x e_y| / SLIP_FAC  <=  mu_w g_eff
            _roll = _was_rolling & (np.hypot(_Gx, _Gz) / SLIP_FAC <= mu_w_g)
            if np.any(_roll):
                # Replace free relaxation with the constrained rolling map.
                _lam = (1.0 - 1.0 / SLIP_FAC) / tau + (1.0 / SLIP_FAC) / tau_rot
                _cx = ((1.0 - 1.0 / SLIP_FAC) * Uf[:, 0] / tau
                       - (1.0 / SLIP_FAC) * radii * _Of[:, 1] / tau_rot)
                _cz = ((1.0 - 1.0 / SLIP_FAC) * Uf[:, 1] / tau
                       + (1.0 / SLIP_FAC) * radii * _Of[:, 0] / tau_rot)
                _e = np.exp(-_lam * dt_k)
                _ux = _cx / _lam + (_vel_pre[:, 0] - _cx / _lam) * _e
                _uz = _cz / _lam + (_vel_pre[:, 1] - _cz / _lam) * _e
                vel[_roll, 0] = _ux[_roll]
                vel[_roll, 1] = _uz[_roll]
                omg[_roll, 0] = _uz[_roll] / radii[_roll]    # omega_x =  U_z / r
                omg[_roll, 1] = -_ux[_roll] / radii[_roll]   # omega_z = -U_x / r
                # The wall-normal spin retains its relaxed value.
            _slide = ~_roll
            # Slip after relaxation; rolling particles have s = 0 to roundoff.
            _sx = vel[:, 0] + radii * omg[:, 1]
            _sz = vel[:, 1] - radii * omg[:, 0]
            _sm = np.hypot(_sx, _sz)
            _cap = SLIP_FAC * mu_w_g * dt_k          # slip removable in dt_k
            _stick = _slide & (_sm <= _cap)           # arrival at zero slip
            with np.errstate(divide="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
                # j = -(1/SLIP_FAC) s  (stick: removes s exactly)   or
                # j = -mu_w g_eff dt_k s/|s|  (Coulomb, constant direction)
                _f = np.where(_stick, 1.0 / SLIP_FAC,
                              np.where(_slide & (_sm > 0.0),
                                       mu_w_g * dt_k / _sm, 0.0))
            _jx = -_f * _sx                     # impulse per unit mass
            _jz = -_f * _sz
            # Wall work is J . (s_pre + s_post)/2; this is exact for the
            # constant-direction force used here.
            _spx = _sx + SLIP_FAC * _jx
            _spz = _sz + SLIP_FAC * _jz
            wall_work += float(np.sum(masses * (_jx * 0.5 * (_sx + _spx)
                                                 + _jz * 0.5 * (_sz + _spz))))
            vel[:, 0] += _jx                            # Eq. [11]
            vel[:, 1] += _jz
            omg[:, 0] -= _jz / (kappa * radii)          # Eq. [12]: -(r/I) J_z
            omg[:, 1] += _jx / (kappa * radii)          # Eq. [12]: +(r/I) J_x
            rolling_frac = float(np.mean(_roll | _stick))
        # Optional centre-speed substrate friction, separate from model E.
        if mu_frict > 0.0 and g > 0.0:
            speed = np.linalg.norm(vel, axis=1)
            moving = speed > eps
            if np.any(moving):
                reduction = mu_frict * g * dt_k
                factor = np.ones(N)
                factor[moving] = np.maximum(0.0, 1.0 - reduction / speed[moving])
                vel = vel * factor[:, None]

    def advance(dt_a):
        nonlocal pos
        lambda_current[:] += np.linalg.norm(vel, axis=1) * dt_a
        pos += vel * dt_a
        if periodic:
            wrap_positions(pos, L, W)

    # Force-free drift with exact within-step collisions.
    def drift(dt_drift, t0=0.0):
        nonlocal pos, vel, omg, total_collisions, cap_hits
        nonlocal _ev_buf, _ev_n, _ev_truncated
        nonlocal _pz_exceed, _pz_abs_sum, _pz_abs_max
        # Collisionless controls retain radii so their sampling height,
        # excluded-volume seeding, and area fraction match the paired run.
        free = (not collisions_enabled) or (r_max <= 1e-6)

        if free and periodic:
            advance(dt_drift)          # nothing can interrupt a free drift
            return

        if periodic:
            wrap_positions(pos, L, W)
        max_speed = float(np.linalg.norm(vel, axis=1).max()) if N else 0.0

        if free:
            # Wall contact remains active when pair collisions are disabled.
            pi = np.empty(0, dtype=np.int64)
            pj = np.empty(0, dtype=np.int64)
        elif periodic:
            r_query = min(2.0 * r_max + 2.0 * max_speed * dt_drift,
                          0.49 * min(L, W))
            pi, pj = _celllist_pairs_periodic(pos, L, W, r_query)
        else:
            r_query = 2.0 * r_max + 2.0 * max_speed * dt_drift
            tree = cKDTree(pos, boxsize=boxsize)
            pairs = tree.query_pairs(r=r_query, output_type="ndarray")
            if len(pairs):
                pi = np.ascontiguousarray(pairs[:, 0])
                pj = np.ascontiguousarray(pairs[:, 1])
            else:
                pi = np.empty(0, dtype=np.int64)
                pj = np.empty(0, dtype=np.int64)

        remaining = dt_drift
        n_events = 0
        while remaining > eps and n_events < cap:
            bt, bk = _earliest_ball_collision(
                pi, pj, pos, vel, radii, L, W, periodic, remaining, eps)

            if not periodic:
                # Only particles within this sub-step's wall-reachable strip
                # need to be searched.
                reach = max_speed * remaining + r_max
                near = np.where(
                    (pos[:, 0] < radii + reach) |
                    (pos[:, 0] > L - radii - reach) |
                    (pos[:, 1] < radii + reach) |
                    (pos[:, 1] > W - radii - reach)
                )[0]
                if len(near):
                    wt, wloc, waxis = _earliest_wall_collision(
                        np.ascontiguousarray(pos[near]),
                        np.ascontiguousarray(vel[near]),
                        np.ascontiguousarray(radii[near]),
                        L, W, remaining, eps)
                    wi = int(near[wloc]) if wloc != -1 else -1
                else:
                    wt, wi, waxis = remaining, -1, -1
            else:
                wt, wi, waxis = remaining, -1, -1

            ball_ok = (bk != -1)
            wall_ok = (wi != -1)
            if not ball_ok and not wall_ok:
                advance(remaining)
                remaining = 0.0
                break

            if wall_ok and (not ball_ok or wt <= bt):
                advance(wt)
                if waxis == 0:
                    vel[wi, 0] = -e_wall * vel[wi, 0]
                    pos[wi, 0] = min(max(pos[wi, 0], radii[wi]), L - radii[wi])
                else:
                    vel[wi, 1] = -e_wall * vel[wi, 1]
                    pos[wi, 1] = min(max(pos[wi, 1], radii[wi]), W - radii[wi])
                remaining -= wt
            else:
                advance(bt)
                i = int(pi[bk]); j = int(pj[bk])
                dx0 = pos[i, 0] - pos[j, 0]
                dx1 = pos[i, 1] - pos[j, 1]
                if periodic:
                    dx0 -= L * np.floor(dx0 / L + 0.5)
                    dx1 -= W * np.floor(dx1 / W + 0.5)
                # Contact frame. With dx = X_i - X_j (minimum image), the normal
                # n = (X_j - X_i)/|X_j - X_i| is -dx/|dx|, and the horizontal
                # tangent is t = e_y x n = (n_z, -n_x); (t, e_y, n) is right-
                # handed. The impulse on j is P = P_perp n + P_par t, on i -P.
                _rn = max(np.hypot(dx0, dx1), eps)
                _n0 = -dx0 / _rn; _n1 = -dx1 / _rn          # n, i -> j
                _t0 = _n1;        _t1 = -_n0                # t = e_y x n
                # relative centre velocity and its normal / tangential parts
                _dv0 = vel[j, 0] - vel[i, 0]
                _dv1 = vel[j, 1] - vel[i, 1]
                _u_perp = _dv0 * _n0 + _dv1 * _n1           # U_c,perp^- < 0: approaching
                _u_par = _dv0 * _t0 + _dv1 * _t1            # centre slip along t
                if rotation:
                    # surface slip, Eq. [7]: the spin adds omega x (r n) on i
                    # and omega x (-r n) on j; (omega x n).t = omega_y, so
                    #   U_c,par^- = (U_j - U_i).t - (r_i omega_y,i + r_j omega_y,j)
                    _u_par -= radii[i] * omg[i, 2] + radii[j] * omg[j, 2]
                # normal impulse, Eq. [6] (general masses): P_perp > 0 separates
                _p_perp = (-(1.0 + e_ball) * _u_perp
                           / (1.0 / masses[i] + 1.0 / masses[j]))
                _p_par = 0.0
                _pz_diag = 0.0
                _uy = 0.0
                if rotation:
                    # Vertical component of the full surface slip. Here
                    # (omega x n)_y = omega_z n_x - omega_x n_z, with
                    # omega_z = omg[:, 1] (spanwise), omega_x = omg[:, 0], so
                    #   U~_y = -[r_i (w_i,z n_x - w_i,x n_z) + r_j (w_j,z n_x - w_j,x n_z)]
                    # This quantity is recorded for every rotating model.
                    _uy = -(radii[i] * (omg[i, 1] * _n0 - omg[i, 0] * _n1)
                            + radii[j] * (omg[j, 1] * _n0 - omg[j, 0] * _n1))
                if rough and rotation:
                    # Diagnostic 3-D impulse, Eq. [15]:
                    #   |U~_T| = hypot(U_c,par^-, U~_y)
                    #   P~_T = -(1 - e_par) U~_T / k,  |P~_T| <= mu_pp P_perp (cap)
                    #   P~_z = P~_T . e_y   (on j; -P~_z on i)
                    # It is not applied; only its diagnostic threshold is counted.
                    _uT = np.hypot(_u_par, _uy)
                    if _uT > 0.0:
                        _kk = (1.0 / masses[i] + 1.0 / masses[j]) * (1.0 + 1.0 / kappa)
                        _pT = (1.0 - e_tan) * _uT / _kk
                        if mu_pp >= 0.0:
                            _pT = min(_pT, mu_pp * (-(1.0 + e_ball) * _u_perp
                                                    / (1.0 / masses[i] + 1.0 / masses[j])))
                        _pz_diag = -_pT * _uy / _uT
                        _pz_abs_sum += abs(_pz_diag)
                        if abs(_pz_diag) > _pz_abs_max:
                            _pz_abs_max = abs(_pz_diag)
                        if pz_threshold > 0.0 and abs(_pz_diag) > pz_threshold:
                            _pz_exceed += 1
                if rough and _u_par != 0.0:
                    # tangential impulse, Eqs. [8]--[9]:
                    #   P_par = -(1 - e_par) U_c,par^- / k,
                    #   k = 1/m_i + 1/m_j + r_i^2/I_i + r_j^2/I_j
                    #     = (1/m_i + 1/m_j)(1 + 1/kappa)   [= 7/m, equal spheres]
                    #   Coulomb cap: |P_par| <= mu_pp P_perp, sign opposing the slip.
                    # This uses the pre-collision state and is independent of
                    # the diagnostic 3-D impulse above.
                    _k = (1.0 / masses[i] + 1.0 / masses[j]) * (1.0 + 1.0 / kappa)
                    _p_par = -(1.0 - e_tan) * _u_par / _k
                    if mu_pp >= 0.0 and abs(_p_par) > mu_pp * _p_perp:
                        _p_par = -np.sign(_u_par) * mu_pp * _p_perp
                if log_collisions:
                    # Event-log row from the pre-collision state:
                    #  0 t  1 i  2 j  3 angle of the line of centres (i-j)
                    #  4 w_r normal relative velocity (<0)  5,6 contact point
                    #  7 w_t = U_c,par^-  8 P_perp  9 P_par  10 P~_z  11 U~_y
                    if _ev_n < max_collision_log:
                        if _ev_n == len(_ev_buf):          # grow: double
                            _nb = np.empty((2 * len(_ev_buf), _EV_COLS),
                                           dtype=float)
                            _nb[:_ev_n] = _ev_buf
                            _ev_buf = _nb
                        _w = ((vel[i, 0] - vel[j, 0]) * dx0
                              + (vel[i, 1] - vel[j, 1]) * dx1) / _rn
                        _ev_buf[_ev_n, 0] = t0 + (dt_drift - remaining) + bt
                        _ev_buf[_ev_n, 1] = i
                        _ev_buf[_ev_n, 2] = j
                        _ev_buf[_ev_n, 3] = np.arctan2(dx1, dx0)
                        _ev_buf[_ev_n, 4] = _w
                        # contact location: i's position plus half the
                        # (minimum-image) separation, wrapped into the box
                        _ev_buf[_ev_n, 5] = (pos[i, 0] - 0.5 * dx0) % L
                        _ev_buf[_ev_n, 6] = (pos[i, 1] - 0.5 * dx1) % W
                        _ev_buf[_ev_n, 7] = _u_par
                        _ev_buf[_ev_n, 8] = _p_perp
                        _ev_buf[_ev_n, 9] = _p_par
                        _ev_buf[_ev_n, 10] = _pz_diag
                        _ev_buf[_ev_n, 11] = _uy
                        _ev_n += 1
                    else:
                        _ev_truncated = True
                # Normal impulse from Eq. [3] with P_perp n.
                vi0, vi1, vj0, vj1 = _resolve_collision_kernel(
                    vel[i, 0], vel[i, 1], masses[i],
                    vel[j, 0], vel[j, 1], masses[j],
                    e_ball, dx0, dx1, eps)
                vel[i, 0] = vi0; vel[i, 1] = vi1
                vel[j, 0] = vj0; vel[j, 1] = vj1
                if _p_par != 0.0:
                    # Tangential impulse P_par t on j and -P_par t on i. It may
                    # follow the normal update because the components are orthogonal and
                    # P_par comes from the pre-collision state. Equation [4] gives
                    # n x (P_par t) = P_par e_y, so only the wall-normal spins
                    # change, each by -r P_par / I.
                    vel[i, 0] -= _p_par * _t0 / masses[i]
                    vel[i, 1] -= _p_par * _t1 / masses[i]
                    vel[j, 0] += _p_par * _t0 / masses[j]
                    vel[j, 1] += _p_par * _t1 / masses[j]
                    omg[i, 2] -= radii[i] * _p_par / inertia[i]
                    omg[j, 2] -= radii[j] * _p_par / inertia[j]
                remaining -= bt
                # Close free paths, discarding the initial incomplete segment.
                for p in (i, j):
                    if bb_count[p] > 0:
                        lambda_sum[p] += lambda_current[p]
                        lambda_count[p] += 1
                    lambda_current[p] = 0.0
                    bb_count[p] += 1
            n_events += 1

        if n_events >= cap:
            cap_hits += 1
            advance(remaining)
            if len(pi):
                _push_apart(pi, pj, pos, radii, inv_m, L, W,
                            periodic, eps, 4)
                if periodic:
                    wrap_positions(pos, L, W)
        total_collisions += n_events
        return n_events

    half = (integrator == "strang")

    # Time-stepping loop.
    for step in range(1, n_steps + 1):
        t_prev = (step - 1) * dt

        if half:
            apply_kick(dt / 2.0, t_prev)
            drift(dt, t_prev)
            apply_kick(dt / 2.0, t_prev + dt)
        else:
            apply_kick(dt, t_prev)
            drift(dt, t_prev)

        t = step * dt

        # Store each snapshot at the first step crossing its requested time.
        while snap_idx < len(snap_queue) and snap_queue[snap_idx] <= t + eps:
            snapshots.append({"time": snap_queue[snap_idx],
                              "positions": pos.copy()})
            snap_idx += 1

        if step % record_every == 0 or step == n_steps:
            ke = kinetic_energy(vel, masses)
            p = total_momentum(vel, masses)
            phi, ma, sa = polarization(vel)
            event_times.append(t)
            _record_frame(pos, vel, omg)
            energy_history.append(ke)
            momentum_history.append(p.copy())
            polarization_history.append(phi)
            mean_angle_history.append(ma)
            std_angle_history.append(sa)
            lam_mean = compute_mfp()
            lambda_times.append(t)
            lambda_mean_history.append(lam_mean)
            _row = {
                "time": t, "KE": ke,
                "Px": p[0], "Py": p[1], "|P|": float(np.hypot(p[0], p[1])),
                "polarization": phi,
                "mean_angle": ma, "std_angle": sa,
                "collisions_cumulative": total_collisions,
                "mean_free_path": lam_mean,
            }
            if rotation:
                # Rotational energy uses all three spin components; report rms
                # of the contact-active (wall-normal) and passive (in-plane)
                # spins
                _row["KE_rot"] = float(0.5 * np.sum(inertia[:, None]
                                                    * omg * omg))
                _row["omega_wn_rms"] = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(omg[:, 2] ** 2)))
                _row["omega_inplane_rms"] = float(
                    np.sqrt(np.mean(omg[:, 0] ** 2 + omg[:, 1] ** 2)))
            if rotation and rough:
                # Per-frame vertical-impulse diagnostics: cumulative exceedance
                # whose |Pz~| exceeds the threshold, the rate of those since
                # the previous frame per particle per unit time, and the
                # running mean / max of |Pz~| over all rough contacts so far
                _row["pz_abs_mean"] = (_pz_abs_sum / total_collisions
                                       if total_collisions else 0.0)
                _row["pz_abs_max"] = _pz_abs_max
                if pz_threshold > 0.0:
                    _row["pz_exceed_cum"] = _pz_exceed
                    _dt_rec = t - event_times[-2] if len(event_times) > 1 else t
                    _row["pz_exceed_rate"] = ((_pz_exceed - _pz_prev) / (N * _dt_rec)
                                              if _dt_rec > 0 else 0.0)
                    _pz_prev = _pz_exceed
            if wall_friction:
                _row["wall_work_cum"] = wall_work
                _row["rolling_frac"] = rolling_frac
                _sx = vel[:, 0] + radii * omg[:, 1]
                _sz = vel[:, 1] - radii * omg[:, 0]
                _row["slip_rms"] = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(_sx ** 2 + _sz ** 2)))
            diag_rows.append(_row)

        if progress_interval and step % progress_interval == 0:
            elapsed = _time.perf_counter() - t_start
            rate = step / max(elapsed, 1e-9)
            eta = (n_steps - step) / max(rate, 1e-9)
            print(f"  step {step:7d}/{n_steps} | t={t:8.3f}/{T_final} "
                  f"({100 * t / T_final:5.1f}%) | KE={energy_history[-1]:.4g} "
                  f"| phi={polarization_history[-1]:.3f} "
                  f"| col={total_collisions} | {rate:.0f} st/s "
                  f"| ETA {eta:.0f}s")

        if debug_overlaps and step % max(1, n_steps // 50) == 0:
            worst = check_runtime_overlaps(pos, radii, L, W, boundary)
            if worst > 0.05 * r_max:
                print(f"    [warn] worst overlap {worst:.3e} at t={t:.3f} "
                      f"(consider smaller dt)")

    elapsed = _time.perf_counter() - t_start
    print(f"Done: {n_steps} steps in {elapsed:.2f}s "
          f"({n_steps / max(elapsed, 1e-9):.0f} steps/s), "
          f"{total_collisions} collision impulses, {cap_hits} cap hits"
          + (f", model {'E' if wall_friction else 'D' if mu_pp >= 0 else ('C' if rough else 'B')}"
             if rotation else ", model A") + ".")

    diagnostics = pd.DataFrame(diag_rows)
    diag_path = os.path.join(_SCRIPT_DIR, csv_filename)
    diagnostics.to_csv(diag_path, index=False)

    return {
        "event_times": event_times,
        # Return only written frames from the preallocated memory maps.
        "position_history": (position_history if _stream is None
                             else _stream["pos"][:_stream["k"]]),
        "velocity_history": (velocity_history if _stream is None
                             else _stream["vel"][:_stream["k"]]),
        # Spins per frame in (streamwise, spanwise, wall-normal) order.
        "omega_history": (None if not rotation else
                          (omega_history if _stream is None
                           else _stream["omg"][:_stream["k"]])),
        "omega_final": None if not rotation else omg.copy(),
        "rotation": rotation,
        "e_tan": e_tan, "mu_pp": mu_pp,
        "wall_friction": wall_friction, "mu_w": mu_w, "g_eff": g_eff,
        "wall_work": wall_work,
        "pz_threshold": pz_threshold, "pz_exceed": _pz_exceed,
        # Fixed model constants retained with the results.
        "tau_rot_factor": TAU_ROT_FACTOR,
        "inertia_coeff": INERTIA_COEFF,
        "stream_dir": None if _stream is None else _stream["dir"],
        "energy_history": energy_history,
        "momentum_history": momentum_history,
        "boundary": boundary, "L": L, "W": W,
        "snapshots": snapshots,
        "polarization_times": list(event_times),
        "polarization_history": polarization_history,
        "mean_angle_history": mean_angle_history,
        "std_angle_history": std_angle_history,
        "diagnostics": diagnostics,
        "diagnostics_csv": diag_path,
        "total_collisions": total_collisions,
        "collisions_enabled": bool(collisions_enabled),
        "collision_events": (_ev_buf[:_ev_n].copy() if log_collisions
                             else None),
        "collision_log_truncated": _ev_truncated,
        "cap_hits": cap_hits,
        "dt": dt, "n_steps": n_steps,
        "tau": tau, "mu_frict": mu_frict, "g": g,
        "e_ball": e_ball, "e_wall": e_wall,
        "wall_time_s": elapsed,
        # Dynamic mean-free-path results.
        "lambda_times": lambda_times,
        "mean_free_path_history": lambda_mean_history,
        "lambda_sum_final": lambda_sum.copy(),
        "lambda_count_final": lambda_count.copy(),
        "bb_count_final": bb_count.copy(),
    }


def terminal_speed(U_f_mag, mu, g, tau):
    """Return ``max(0, |U_f| - mu g tau)`` for a single particle."""
    return max(0.0, U_f_mag - mu * g * tau)


# Stand-alone analysis and plotting utilities. These support the demonstration
# below; the DNS study uses snapshots.py, figures.py, and viz.py instead.

# Linear interpolation between recorded states

def interpolate_state(results, t_query):
    """Positions and velocities at time t_query, by constant-velocity
    extrapolation from the last recorded frame (clamped to the first and
    last frames). Wrapped into the box for a periodic run."""
    ev_times = np.array(results["event_times"], dtype=float)
    pos_hist = results["position_history"]
    vel_hist = results["velocity_history"]
    boundary = results.get("boundary", "periodic")
    L = results.get("L"); W = results.get("W")

    if len(ev_times) < 2 or t_query <= ev_times[0]:
        pos = np.asarray(pos_hist[0], dtype=float).copy()
        vel = np.asarray(vel_hist[0], dtype=float).copy()
    elif t_query >= ev_times[-1]:
        pos = np.asarray(pos_hist[-1], dtype=float).copy()
        vel = np.asarray(vel_hist[-1], dtype=float).copy()
    else:
        k = np.searchsorted(ev_times, t_query) - 1
        k = max(0, min(k, len(ev_times) - 2))
        dt = t_query - ev_times[k]
        pos = (np.asarray(pos_hist[k], dtype=float)
               + np.asarray(vel_hist[k], dtype=float) * dt)
        vel = np.asarray(vel_hist[k], dtype=float).copy()

    if boundary == "periodic":
        pos = wrap_positions(pos.copy(), L, W)
    return pos, vel.copy()


# Plotting

def plot_table_snapshot(results, radii, L=None, W=None, time=None,
                        title=None, show_ball_labels=True, filename=None):
    if L is None: L = results["L"]
    if W is None: W = results["W"]
    if time is None: time = results["event_times"][-1]
    pos, _ = interpolate_state(results, time)
    N = len(radii)
    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(10, 5))
    ax.add_patch(Rectangle((0, 0), L, W, fill=False,
                           linewidth=1.8, edgecolor="black"))
    colors = plt.cm.tab20.colors
    for i in range(N):
        ax.add_patch(Circle((pos[i, 0], pos[i, 1]), radii[i],
                            facecolor=colors[i % len(colors)],
                            edgecolor="black", linewidth=0.4, alpha=0.8))
        if show_ball_labels and N <= 50:
            ax.text(pos[i, 0], pos[i, 1], str(i + 1),
                    ha="center", va="center", fontsize=6)
    ax.set_xlim(0, L); ax.set_ylim(0, W); ax.set_aspect("equal")
    ax.set_xlabel("x", fontsize=12); ax.set_ylabel("y", fontsize=12)
    if title is None:
        title = f"Snapshot t={time:.3f}, boundary={results.get('boundary')}"
    ax.set_title(title, fontsize=13, pad=10)
    ax.grid(True, linestyle="--", linewidth=0.5, alpha=0.3)
    fig.tight_layout()
    if filename:
        fig.savefig(os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, filename),
                    dpi=300, bbox_inches="tight")
    plt.show()
    plt.close(fig)


def plot_energy_history(results, filename=None):
    times = np.array(results["event_times"], dtype=float)
    energy = np.array(results["energy_history"], dtype=float)
    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(10, 4.8))
    ax.plot(times, energy, linewidth=1.6, color="black", label="KE")
    ax.scatter(times[0], energy[0], color="red", s=40, zorder=5)
    ax.set_xlabel("Time", fontsize=12)
    ax.set_ylabel("Kinetic energy", fontsize=12)
    ax.set_title("Kinetic Energy vs Time (drag/friction/restitution: not conserved)",
                 fontsize=12)
    ax.grid(True, linestyle="--", linewidth=0.5, alpha=0.3)
    fig.tight_layout()
    if filename:
        fig.savefig(os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, filename),
                    dpi=300, bbox_inches="tight")
    plt.show()
    plt.close(fig)


def plot_momentum_magnitude(results, filename=None):
    mom = np.array(results["momentum_history"], dtype=float)
    pnorm = np.linalg.norm(mom, axis=1)
    times = np.array(results["event_times"], dtype=float)
    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(10, 4.8))
    ax.plot(times, pnorm, linewidth=1.6, color="black", label=r"$|\mathbf{P}|$")
    ax.scatter(times[0], pnorm[0], s=42, color="red", zorder=4)
    ax.set_xlabel("Time", fontsize=12)
    ax.set_ylabel(r"$|\mathbf{P}|$", fontsize=12)
    ax.set_title("Total Momentum Magnitude (driven by external drag/friction)",
                 fontsize=12)
    ax.grid(True, linestyle="--", linewidth=0.5, alpha=0.3)
    fig.tight_layout()
    if filename:
        fig.savefig(os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, filename),
                    dpi=300, bbox_inches="tight")
    plt.show()
    plt.close(fig)


def plot_momentum_components(results, filename=None):
    mom = np.array(results["momentum_history"], float)
    times = np.array(results["event_times"], float)
    fig, axes = plt.subplots(1, 2, figsize=(13, 4.5))
    for ax, idx, color, label in [(axes[0], 0, "tab:blue", "Px"),
                                  (axes[1], 1, "tab:orange", "Py")]:
        ax.plot(times, mom[:, idx], color=color, linewidth=1.6)
        ax.scatter(times[0], mom[0, idx], color="red", s=40, zorder=4)
        ax.set_xlabel("Time", fontsize=11)
        ax.set_ylabel(label, fontsize=11)
        ax.set_title(f"{label} vs Time", fontsize=12)
        ax.grid(True, linestyle="--", alpha=0.35)
    fig.tight_layout()
    if filename:
        fig.savefig(os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, filename),
                    dpi=300, bbox_inches="tight")
    plt.show()
    plt.close(fig)


def plot_ball_speeds(results, filename=None):
    times = np.array(results["event_times"], float)
    vel_hist = [np.asarray(v, float) for v in results["velocity_history"]]
    N = vel_hist[0].shape[0]
    speeds = np.array([np.linalg.norm(v, axis=1) for v in vel_hist])
    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(10, 5))
    sample = list(range(min(N, 20)))
    for i in sample:
        ax.plot(times, speeds[:, i], alpha=0.7,
                label=f"Ball {i + 1}" if N <= 20 else None)
    ax.set_xlabel("Time"); ax.set_ylabel("Speed")
    ax.set_title(f"Ball speeds (showing {len(sample)} of {N})")
    ax.grid(True, linestyle="--", alpha=0.35)
    fig.tight_layout()
    if filename:
        fig.savefig(os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, filename),
                    dpi=300, bbox_inches="tight")
    plt.show()
    plt.close(fig)

def plot_speed_distribution(results, filename=None, configuration="final",
                            bins=40, as_probability=True):
    idx = 0 if configuration == "initial" else -1
    vel = np.asarray(results["velocity_history"][idx], float)
    speeds = np.linalg.norm(vel, axis=1)
    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(8, 5))
    if speeds.size == 0 or np.allclose(speeds, speeds[0]):
        ax.text(0.5, 0.5, "speeds all equal / empty",
                ha="center", va="center", transform=ax.transAxes)
        ax.set_ylabel("Probability" if as_probability else "Probability density")
    elif as_probability:
        w = np.full(len(speeds), 1.0 / max(len(speeds), 1))
        ax.hist(speeds, bins=bins, weights=w, color="tab:blue",
                edgecolor="black", alpha=0.75)
        ax.set_ylabel("Probability", fontsize=12)
    else:
        ax.hist(speeds, bins=bins, density=True, color="tab:blue",
                edgecolor="black", alpha=0.75)
        ax.set_ylabel("Probability density", fontsize=12)
    ax.set_xlabel("Speed", fontsize=12)
    ax.set_title(f"Speed distribution ({configuration})", fontsize=13)
    ax.grid(True, linestyle="--", alpha=0.3)
    fig.tight_layout()
    if filename:
        fig.savefig(os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, filename), dpi=300,
                    bbox_inches="tight")
    plt.show(); plt.close(fig)


def plot_polarization(results, filename=None):
    """Collective-motion order parameter phi(t) and circular std of angle."""
    times = np.array(results["polarization_times"], float)
    phi = np.array(results["polarization_history"], float)
    std_angle = np.array(results["std_angle_history"], float)
    fig, ax1 = plt.subplots(figsize=(10, 4.8))
    ax1.plot(times, phi, color="tab:red", linewidth=1.8,
             label=r"polarization $\varphi$")
    ax1.set_xlabel("Time", fontsize=12)
    ax1.set_ylabel(r"order parameter $\varphi$", color="tab:red", fontsize=12)
    ax1.set_ylim(-0.02, 1.02)
    ax1.tick_params(axis="y", labelcolor="tab:red")
    ax1.grid(True, linestyle="--", alpha=0.3)
    ax2 = ax1.twinx()
    ax2.plot(times, std_angle, color="tab:blue", linewidth=1.4, alpha=0.8,
             label=r"angular std")
    ax2.set_ylabel("circular std of velocity angle (rad)",
                   color="tab:blue", fontsize=12)
    ax2.tick_params(axis="y", labelcolor="tab:blue")
    ax1.set_title("Collective Motion: Order Parameter & Angular Spread",
                  fontsize=13)
    fig.tight_layout()
    if filename:
        fig.savefig(os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, filename),
                    dpi=300, bbox_inches="tight")
    plt.show()
    plt.close(fig)

def plot_velocity_angle_pdf(results, filename=None, configuration="final",
                            bins=36):
    idx = 0 if configuration == "initial" else -1
    vel = np.asarray(results["velocity_history"][idx], float)
    speed = np.linalg.norm(vel, axis=1)
    moving = speed > 1e-9
    angles = np.arctan2(vel[moving, 1], vel[moving, 0])
    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(7, 7), subplot_kw={"projection": "polar"})
    if len(angles) == 0:
        ax.set_title(f"Velocity-angle PDF ({configuration}) — "
                     f"no moving particles", fontsize=12, pad=18)
    else:
        counts, edges = np.histogram(angles, bins=bins,
                                     range=(-np.pi, np.pi), density=True)
        counts = np.nan_to_num(counts)          # guard against /0
        centers = 0.5 * (edges[:-1] + edges[1:])
        width = (2 * np.pi) / bins
        ax.bar(centers, counts, width=width, bottom=0.0,
               color="tab:purple", edgecolor="black", alpha=0.7)
        ax.set_title(f"Velocity-angle PDF ({configuration})",
                     fontsize=13, pad=18)
    fig.tight_layout()
    if filename:
        fig.savefig(os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, filename),
                    dpi=300, bbox_inches="tight")
    plt.show()
    plt.close(fig)

def plot_velocity_field_grid(results, times=None, fluid_velocity=None,
                             grid=(24, 12), source="particle",
                             ncols=3, filename="velocity_field_grid.png",
                             output_dir="velocity_field_plots",
                             dpi=200, show=True):
    """Plot coarse-grained velocity fields at several times.

    Particle velocities are averaged over an ``(nx, ny)`` grid. All panels
    share a speed colour scale. ``source="particle"`` plots cell means;
    ``source="fluid"`` evaluates the supplied carrier field.

    Parameters
    ----------
    times : list of floats or None
        Time points (one subplot each). None -> 6 evenly spaced times.
        These may be chosen to match the stored snapshots.
    fluid_velocity : callable(pos, t) -> (N,2)
        Required only for ``source="fluid"``; use the simulated carrier field.
    grid : (nx, ny)
        Coarse-graining cells in x and y.
    ncols : int
        Number of columns in the subplot grid.

    Returns the saved file path.
    """
    L = results["L"]; W = results["W"]
    if times is None:
        ev = np.array(results["event_times"], float)
        times = list(np.linspace(ev[0], ev[-1], 6))
    times = list(np.atleast_1d(times))
    n = len(times)
    ncols = min(ncols, n)
    nrows = int(np.ceil(n / ncols))

    nx, ny = grid
    xs = (np.arange(nx) + 0.5) * (L / nx)
    ys = (np.arange(ny) + 0.5) * (W / ny)
    GX, GY = np.meshgrid(xs, ys)
    cw, ch = L / nx, W / ny

    # First pass: compute fields and their shared maximum speed.
    fields = []
    smax = 1e-9
    for t in times:
