        if source == "fluid":
            if fluid_velocity is None:
                raise ValueError("source='fluid' requires fluid_velocity=...")
            UV = fluid_velocity(np.column_stack([GX.ravel(), GY.ravel()]), t)
            U = UV[:, 0].reshape(ny, nx); V = UV[:, 1].reshape(ny, nx)
            count = np.ones((ny, nx))
        else:
            pos, vel = interpolate_state(results, t)
            ix = np.clip((pos[:, 0] / cw).astype(int), 0, nx - 1)
            iy = np.clip((pos[:, 1] / ch).astype(int), 0, ny - 1)
            U = np.zeros((ny, nx)); V = np.zeros((ny, nx))
            count = np.zeros((ny, nx))
            np.add.at(U, (iy, ix), vel[:, 0])
            np.add.at(V, (iy, ix), vel[:, 1])
            np.add.at(count, (iy, ix), 1.0)
            nz = count > 0
            U[nz] /= count[nz]; V[nz] /= count[nz]
        sp = np.hypot(U, V)
        smax = max(smax, sp.max())
        fields.append((t, U, V, sp, count))

    # Second pass: draw the panels.
    fig, axes = plt.subplots(nrows, ncols, figsize=(5.2 * ncols, 3.0 * nrows),
                             dpi=dpi, squeeze=False)
    q_last = None
    for idx, (t, U, V, sp, count) in enumerate(fields):
        ax = axes[idx // ncols][idx % ncols]
        q = ax.quiver(GX, GY, U, V, sp, cmap="viridis", clim=(0, smax),
                      scale_units="xy", angles="xy", pivot="mid", width=0.005)
        q_last = q
        empty = (count == 0)
        if np.any(empty):
            ax.scatter(GX[empty], GY[empty], s=5, c="lightgray",
                       marker="x", zorder=1)
        ax.set_xlim(0, L); ax.set_ylim(0, W); ax.set_aspect("equal")
        ax.set_title(f"t = {t:.2f}", fontsize=11)
        if idx // ncols == nrows - 1:
            ax.set_xlabel("x", fontsize=10)
        if idx % ncols == 0:
            ax.set_ylabel("y", fontsize=10)
    # Hide unused panels.
    for j in range(n, nrows * ncols):
        axes[j // ncols][j % ncols].axis("off")

    lab = "fluid $U_f$" if source == "fluid" else "particle velocity"
    fig.suptitle(f"Velocity field ({lab}) — grid {nx}×{ny}", fontsize=14)
    fig.tight_layout(rect=[0, 0, 0.92, 0.97])
    cax = fig.add_axes([0.94, 0.15, 0.015, 0.7])
    cb = fig.colorbar(q_last, cax=cax)
    cb.set_label("mean speed", fontsize=11)

    if not os.path.isabs(output_dir):
        output_dir = os.path.join(_SCRIPT_DIR, output_dir)
    os.makedirs(output_dir, exist_ok=True)
    fn = os.path.join(output_dir, filename)
    fig.savefig(fn, dpi=dpi, bbox_inches="tight")
    if show:
        plt.show()
    plt.close(fig)
    return fn

def plot_all_graphics(results, radii, L=None, W=None, show_ball_labels=False):
    if L is None: L = results["L"]
    if W is None: W = results["W"]
    plot_table_snapshot(results, radii, L, W,
                        time=results["event_times"][0],
                        title="Initial configuration",
                        show_ball_labels=show_ball_labels,
                        filename="initial_snapshot.png")
    plot_table_snapshot(results, radii, L, W,
                        time=results["event_times"][-1],
                        title="Final configuration",
                        show_ball_labels=show_ball_labels,
                        filename="final_snapshot.png")
    plot_energy_history(results, filename="energy_history.png")
    plot_momentum_components(results, filename="momentum_components.png")
    plot_momentum_magnitude(results, filename="momentum_magnitude.png")
    # Optional additional figures:
    #plot_ball_speeds(results, filename="ball_speeds.png"),
    #plot_speed_distribution(results, filename="speed_distribution_final.png",
                            #configuration="final")
    #plot_polarization(results, filename="polarization.png")
    #plot_velocity_angle_pdf(results, filename="velocity_angle_pdf_final.png",
                            #configuration="final")


# Cumulative radial density

def cumulative_radial_density(reference_point, R, N_bins, positions,
                              L=None, W=None, boundary="periodic"):
    reference_point = np.asarray(reference_point, float)
    positions = np.asarray(positions, float)
    if boundary == "periodic":
        dx = positions[:, 0] - reference_point[0]
        dy = positions[:, 1] - reference_point[1]
        dx -= L * np.round(dx / L)
        dy -= W * np.round(dy / W)
        distances = np.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy)
    else:
        distances = np.linalg.norm(positions - reference_point, axis=1)
    rdf_radii = np.linspace(R / N_bins, R, N_bins)
    dist_sorted = np.sort(distances)
    counts = np.array([np.searchsorted(dist_sorted, Ri, side="right")
                       for Ri in rdf_radii])
    return rdf_radii, counts, counts / (np.pi * rdf_radii ** 2)


def plot_cumulative_radial_density(results, reference_point=None, R=3.0,
                                   N_bins=100, masses=None,
                                   configuration="initial", case_name="case",
                                   output_dir="radial_density_plots",
                                   filename=None, dpi=300, show_plot=True):
    positions = np.asarray(
        results["position_history"][0 if configuration == "initial" else -1],
        float)
    boundary = results.get("boundary", "periodic")
    L = results.get("L"); W = results.get("W")
    if reference_point is None:
        reference_point = COM(positions, masses)
    if boundary == "periodic":
        reference_point = wrap_positions(reference_point.reshape(1, 2), L, W)[0]
    rdf_radii, counts, densities = cumulative_radial_density(
        reference_point, R, N_bins, positions, L, W, boundary)
    if not os.path.isabs(output_dir):
        output_dir = os.path.join(_SCRIPT_DIR, output_dir)
    os.makedirs(output_dir, exist_ok=True)
    if filename is None:
        filename = f"radial_density_{case_name}_{configuration}.png"
    save_path = os.path.join(output_dir, filename)
    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(8, 5), dpi=dpi)
    ax.plot(rdf_radii, densities, linewidth=2.0, marker="o", markersize=3)
    ax.set_xlabel("Radius", fontsize=12)
    ax.set_ylabel("Cumulative radial density", fontsize=12)
    ax.set_title(f"Cumulative Radial Density: {case_name}, {configuration}",
                 fontsize=13)
    ax.grid(True, linestyle="--", alpha=0.5)
    fig.tight_layout()
    fig.savefig(save_path, dpi=dpi, bbox_inches="tight")
    if show_plot:
        plt.show()
    plt.close(fig)
    print(f"RDF saved: {save_path}")
    return rdf_radii, counts, densities, save_path


# Snapshot plots

def save_snapshot_plots(results, radii, output_dir="billiard_snapshots",
                        dpi=200, figsize=(12, 6), image_format="png",
                        save_pdf=False, show_ball_labels=False,
                        title_fontsize=14, axis_fontsize=12,
                        tick_fontsize=10, grid=False, facecolor="white"):
    if not os.path.isabs(output_dir):
        output_dir = os.path.join(_SCRIPT_DIR, output_dir)
    os.makedirs(output_dir, exist_ok=True)
    snapshots = results.get("snapshots", [])
    if len(snapshots) == 0:
        print("No snapshots found.")
        return
    L = results["L"]; W = results["W"]
    boundary = results.get("boundary", "periodic")
    radii = np.asarray(radii, dtype=float)
    N = len(radii)
    cmap = plt.cm.get_cmap("tab20", max(N, 20))
    ball_colors = [cmap(i % 20) for i in range(N)]
    for k, snap in enumerate(snapshots):
        t = float(snap["time"])
        pos = np.asarray(snap["positions"], dtype=float)
        fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=figsize, dpi=dpi)
        fig.patch.set_facecolor(facecolor)
        ax.set_facecolor(facecolor)
        ax.add_patch(Rectangle((0, 0), L, W, fill=False,
                               edgecolor="black", linewidth=1.8, zorder=2))
        for i in range(N):
            ax.add_patch(Circle((pos[i, 0], pos[i, 1]), radii[i],
                                facecolor=ball_colors[i], edgecolor="black",
                                linewidth=0.45, alpha=0.90, zorder=3))
            if show_ball_labels and N <= 100:
                ax.text(pos[i, 0], pos[i, 1], str(i + 1), ha="center",
                        va="center", fontsize=6, color="black", zorder=4)
        ax.set_xlim(0, L); ax.set_ylim(0, W)
        ax.set_aspect("equal", adjustable="box")
        ax.set_xlabel("x", fontsize=axis_fontsize)
        ax.set_ylabel("y", fontsize=axis_fontsize)
        ax.tick_params(axis="both", labelsize=tick_fontsize)
        if grid:
            ax.grid(True, linestyle="--", linewidth=0.5, alpha=0.25)
        else:
            ax.grid(False)
        ax.set_title(f"Snapshot at $t = {t:.4f}$   |   "
                     f"{boundary.capitalize()} boundary   |   $N={N}$",
                     fontsize=title_fontsize, pad=12)
        ax.text(0.985, 0.985, f"Snapshot {k}\n$t={t:.4f}$\nN={N}",
                transform=ax.transAxes, ha="right", va="top", fontsize=9,
                bbox=dict(boxstyle="round,pad=0.3", facecolor="white",
                          edgecolor="gray", alpha=0.9))
        fig.tight_layout()
        base = f"snapshot_{k:04d}_t_{t:.4f}"
        fig.savefig(os.path.join(output_dir, f"{base}.{image_format}"),
                    dpi=dpi, bbox_inches="tight",
                    facecolor=fig.get_facecolor())
        if save_pdf:
            fig.savefig(os.path.join(output_dir, f"{base}.pdf"),
                        bbox_inches="tight", facecolor=fig.get_facecolor())
        plt.close(fig)
    print(f"Saved {len(snapshots)} snapshot plots to: {output_dir}")


# Animation
def animate_billiards(results, radii, L=None, W=None, n_frames=1800, fps=60,
                      dpi=100, show_ball_labels=False,
                      filename="billiard_animation.mp4",
                      progress_interval=100, color_by_angle=True,
                      fluid_velocity=None,        # use the simulated carrier field
                      flow_label=None,            # e.g. r"Shear flow $U_f=(\dot\gamma y,0)$, $\dot\gamma=0.1$"
                      term_tol=0.05,              # |speed - U_term| tolerance (enter band)
                      term_rate_tol=0.02,         # steadiness tolerance
                      rest_thresh=1e-2,           # speed below this = "at rest" (white)
                      show_legend=True,           # show the colour-key legend
                      show_terminal_counter=True):# show the "at terminal: n/N" readout
    if L is None: L = results["L"]
    if W is None: W = results["W"]
    boundary = results.get("boundary", "periodic")
    t0 = results["event_times"][0]
    tf = results["event_times"][-1]
    N = len(radii)
    radii = np.asarray(radii, dtype=float)

    print("\nPreparing animation...")
    print(f"  Balls: {N}  frames: {n_frames}  fps: {fps}  dpi: {dpi}")

    times = np.linspace(t0, tf, n_frames)
    ev_times = np.array(results["event_times"], dtype=float)
    pos_hist = np.array(results["position_history"], dtype=float)
    vel_hist = np.array(results["velocity_history"], dtype=float)
    k_idx = np.clip(np.searchsorted(ev_times, times, side="right") - 1,
                    0, len(ev_times) - 2)
    dt_arr = (times - ev_times[k_idx])[:, None, None]
    all_pos = pos_hist[k_idx] + vel_hist[k_idx] * dt_arr
    if boundary == "periodic":
        all_pos[..., 0] = np.mod(all_pos[..., 0], L)
        all_pos[..., 1] = np.mod(all_pos[..., 1], W)

    # Reserve a right margin only when the legend is shown.
    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(13.5, 6), dpi=dpi)
    if show_legend:
        fig.subplots_adjust(left=0.04, right=0.80, top=0.90, bottom=0.05)
    else:
        fig.subplots_adjust(left=0.04, right=0.97, top=0.90, bottom=0.05)
    fig.patch.set_facecolor("black")
    ax.set_facecolor("#0d1b2a")
    ax.set_xlim(0, L); ax.set_ylim(0, W)
    ax.set_aspect("equal"); ax.set_xticks([]); ax.set_yticks([])
    for sp in ax.spines.values():
        sp.set_edgecolor("#4a9eff"); sp.set_linewidth(2.5)

    # Title summarizing the flow field and model parameters.
    mu_g = float(results.get("mu_frict", 0.0)) * float(results.get("g", 0.0))
    tau_val = np.atleast_1d(np.asarray(results.get("tau", 1.0), float))
    tau_str = (f"{tau_val[0]:.3g}" if np.allclose(tau_val, tau_val[0])
               else f"{tau_val.mean():.3g}(mean)")
    if flow_label is None:
        flow_label = "fluid field $U_f$"
    title = (f"{flow_label}\n"
             rf"$N={N}$,  $\tau={tau_str}$,  $\mu g={mu_g:.3g}$,  "
             rf"$e_{{ball}}={results.get('e_ball', 1.0):.2g}$,  {boundary} BC")
    ax.set_title(title, fontsize=12, color="white", pad=14)

    pts_per_unit = (12 * 72) / L
    marker_size = (radii * pts_per_unit) ** 2

    if color_by_angle:
        import matplotlib.cm as cm
        from matplotlib.lines import Line2D
        hsv = cm.get_cmap("hsv")
        rest_rgba = np.array([1.0, 1.0, 1.0, 1.0])        # white : stopped
        term_rgba = np.array([0.90, 0.10, 0.10, 1.0])     # red   : terminal

        tau_arr = (np.full(N, tau_val[0]) if tau_val.size == 1
                   else np.asarray(tau_val, float))
        ff = (fluid_velocity if fluid_velocity is not None
              else (lambda p, t: np.zeros_like(p)))

        vx = vel_hist[k_idx][..., 0]
        vy = vel_hist[k_idx][..., 1]
        ang = np.arctan2(vy, vx)
        hue = (ang + np.pi) / (2 * np.pi)
        spd = np.hypot(vx, vy)                              # (n_frames, N)

        was_term = np.zeros(N, dtype=bool)                 # hysteresis memory

        def frame_colors(f):
            nonlocal was_term
            cols = hsv(hue[f]); cols[:, 3] = 0.92
            s = spd[f]
            Uf = ff(all_pos[f], times[f])
            Uf_mag = np.hypot(Uf[:, 0], Uf[:, 1])
            U_term = np.maximum(0.0, Uf_mag - mu_g * tau_arr)
            rate = np.abs(s - spd[f - 1]) if f > 0 else np.full(N, np.inf)

            has_term = U_term > term_tol      # a genuinely nonzero terminal speed exists
            # Enter the terminal state under the strict proximity and steadiness test.
            enter = (has_term & (np.abs(s - U_term) < term_tol)
                     & (rate < term_rate_tol))
            # Retain it within a wider band to avoid visual flicker.
            stay = has_term & (np.abs(s - U_term) < 2.0 * term_tol)
            at_term = enter | (was_term & stay)
            was_term = at_term.copy()

            resting = s < rest_thresh
            at_term = at_term & ~resting       # rest (white) always wins
            cols[at_term] = term_rgba          # red
            cols[resting] = rest_rgba          # white
            return cols

        scat = ax.scatter(all_pos[0, :, 0], all_pos[0, :, 1], s=marker_size,
                          c=frame_colors(0), linewidths=0.15,
                          edgecolors="white", alpha=None, zorder=3)
        all_col = "angle"

        # Place the legend in the right figure margin.
        if show_legend:
            legend_elems = [
                Line2D([0], [0], marker="o", linestyle="",
                       markerfacecolor=(0.90, 0.10, 0.10), markeredgecolor="white",
                       markersize=10, label="terminal velocity"),
                Line2D([0], [0], marker="o", linestyle="",
                       markerfacecolor="white", markeredgecolor="gray",
                       markersize=10, label="at rest (stopped)"),
                Line2D([0], [0], marker="o", linestyle="",
                       markerfacecolor=(0.30, 0.55, 0.95), markeredgecolor="white",
                       markersize=10, label="evolving (hue = direction)"),
            ]
            leg = fig.legend(handles=legend_elems,
                             loc="center left", bbox_to_anchor=(0.81, 0.5),
                             fontsize=10, framealpha=0.9, facecolor="#1a2a3a",
                             edgecolor="#4a9eff", labelcolor="white",
                             title="particle state", title_fontsize=11)
            leg.get_title().set_color("white")
    else:
        cmap = plt.cm.get_cmap("tab20", max(N, 20))
        colors = np.array([cmap(i % 20) for i in range(N)])
        scat = ax.scatter(all_pos[0, :, 0], all_pos[0, :, 1], s=marker_size,
                          c=colors, linewidths=0.15, edgecolors="white",
                          alpha=0.92, zorder=3)
        all_col = None
        frame_colors = None

    time_text = ax.text(0.012, 0.97, f"t = {t0:.3f}", transform=ax.transAxes,
                        color="white", fontsize=11, fontfamily="monospace",
                        va="top", zorder=5)
    info_text = ax.text(0.012, 0.91, f"N = {N}   boundary = {boundary}",
                        transform=ax.transAxes, color="#8899aa", fontsize=8.5,
                        fontfamily="monospace", va="top", zorder=5)
    if show_terminal_counter:
        frac_text = ax.text(0.012, 0.855, "", transform=ax.transAxes,
                            color="#ff9999", fontsize=8.5,
                            fontfamily="monospace", va="top", zorder=5)
    else:
        frac_text = None
    frame_text = ax.text(0.988, 0.97, f"1 / {n_frames}",
                        transform=ax.transAxes, color="#8899aa", fontsize=8.5,
                        fontfamily="monospace", va="top", ha="right", zorder=5)
    ax.axhspan(0, W * 0.013, color="#1a2a3a", zorder=4, alpha=0.85)
    bar_fill, = ax.fill([0, 0, 0, 0], [0, W * 0.013, W * 0.013, 0],
                        color="#4a9eff", zorder=5, alpha=0.85)

    t_start = _time.perf_counter()
    last = {"frame": -1}

    def update(frame):
        scat.set_offsets(all_pos[frame, :, :])
        if all_col is not None:
            cols = frame_colors(frame)
            scat.set_facecolors(cols)
            if show_terminal_counter and frac_text is not None:
                n_term = int(np.sum(np.all(np.isclose(
                    cols, np.array([0.90, 0.10, 0.10, 1.0]), atol=1e-6), axis=1)))
                frac_text.set_text(f"at terminal: {n_term}/{N} "
                                   f"({100*n_term/max(N,1):4.1f}%)")
        pct = (frame + 1) / n_frames
        time_text.set_text(f"t = {times[frame]:.3f}")
        frame_text.set_text(f"{frame + 1} / {n_frames}")
        bar_fill.set_xy([[0, 0], [L * pct, 0],
                         [L * pct, W * 0.013], [0, W * 0.013]])
        if (frame == 0 or frame + 1 == n_frames
                or (frame + 1) % progress_interval == 0):
            if last["frame"] != frame:
                elapsed = _time.perf_counter() - t_start
                rfps = (frame + 1) / max(elapsed, 1e-12)
                print(f"  frame {frame + 1:5d}/{n_frames} "
                      f"({pct * 100:5.1f}%) | {rfps:6.1f} fps")
                last["frame"] = frame
        artists = [scat, time_text, info_text, frame_text, bar_fill]
        if frac_text is not None:
            artists.append(frac_text)
        return artists

    anim = FuncAnimation(fig, update, frames=n_frames,
                         interval=1000 / fps, blit=False, repeat=False)
    writer = FFMpegWriter(fps=fps, codec="libx264", bitrate=8000,
                          extra_args=["-pix_fmt", "yuv420p",
                                      "-preset", "slow", "-crf", "18"])
    mp4_path = os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, filename)
    anim.save(mp4_path, writer=writer)
    print(f"Animation saved: {mp4_path} "
          f"({_time.perf_counter() - t_start:.1f}s)")
    plt.close(fig)
    return anim
# Mean-free-path analysis. The first incomplete path segment is excluded.

MEAN_FREE_PATH_DIR = os.path.join(_SCRIPT_DIR, "mean_free_path_results")


def extract_mean_free_path_data(results):
    """Return mean free path as a function of time."""
    if "lambda_times" not in results or "mean_free_path_history" not in results:
        raise KeyError("results must contain 'lambda_times' and "
                       "'mean_free_path_history'.")
    time = np.asarray(results["lambda_times"], dtype=float)
    lam = np.asarray(results["mean_free_path_history"], dtype=float)
    n = min(len(time), len(lam))
    return pd.DataFrame({"time": time[:n], "mean_free_path": lam[:n]})


def estimate_mean_free_path_convergence(mfp_df, tail_fraction=0.2,
                                        tolerance=0.05, min_points=10):
    """Estimate the asymptotic mean free path and convergence time."""
    time = np.asarray(mfp_df["time"], dtype=float)
    lam = np.asarray(mfp_df["mean_free_path"], dtype=float)
    valid = np.isfinite(time) & np.isfinite(lam)
    time, lam = time[valid], lam[valid]

    if len(lam) < min_points:
        return {"lambda_inf": np.nan, "lambda_tail_std": np.nan,
                "relative_tail_std": np.nan, "t_convergence": np.nan,
                "converged": False, "tail_fraction": tail_fraction,
                "tolerance": tolerance, "n_valid_points": len(lam),
                "message": "Not enough valid points for convergence analysis."}

    tail_start = max(0, min(int((1.0 - tail_fraction) * len(lam)), len(lam) - 1))
    tail_lam = lam[tail_start:]
    lambda_inf = float(np.mean(tail_lam))
    lambda_tail_std = float(np.std(tail_lam))

    if abs(lambda_inf) < 1e-14:
        return {"lambda_inf": lambda_inf, "lambda_tail_std": lambda_tail_std,
                "relative_tail_std": np.nan, "t_convergence": np.nan,
                "converged": False, "tail_fraction": tail_fraction,
                "tolerance": tolerance, "n_valid_points": len(lam),
                "message": "Late-time mean free path too close to zero."}

    rel_err = np.abs(lam - lambda_inf) / abs(lambda_inf)
    t_conv, converged = np.nan, False
    for k in range(len(lam)):
        if np.all(rel_err[k:] <= tolerance):
            t_conv, converged = float(time[k]), True
            break

    return {"lambda_inf": lambda_inf, "lambda_tail_std": lambda_tail_std,
            "relative_tail_std": lambda_tail_std / abs(lambda_inf),
            "t_convergence": t_conv, "converged": converged,
            "tail_fraction": tail_fraction, "tolerance": tolerance,
            "n_valid_points": len(lam),
            "message": ("Convergence detected." if converged
                        else "No convergence within the simulated interval.")}


def analyse_mean_free_path(results, output_dir=None,
                           filename_prefix="mean_free_path_dynamic",
                           tail_fraction=0.2, tolerance=0.05,
                           use_step=True, show=True):
    """Extract, summarize, save, and plot the dynamic mean free path."""
    if output_dir is None:
        output_dir = MEAN_FREE_PATH_DIR
    os.makedirs(output_dir, exist_ok=True)

    mfp_df = extract_mean_free_path_data(results)
    convergence = estimate_mean_free_path_convergence(
        mfp_df, tail_fraction=tail_fraction, tolerance=tolerance)

    mfp_df.to_csv(os.path.join(output_dir,
                  f"{filename_prefix}_timeseries.csv"), index=False)
    pd.DataFrame([convergence]).to_csv(os.path.join(output_dir,
                  f"{filename_prefix}_convergence.csv"), index=False)

    time = np.asarray(mfp_df["time"], dtype=float)
    lam = np.asarray(mfp_df["mean_free_path"], dtype=float)
    valid = np.isfinite(time) & np.isfinite(lam)
    time, lam = time[valid], lam[valid]

    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(9, 5.5))
    if len(lam):
        if use_step:
            ax.step(time, lam, where="post", linewidth=2.0,
                    label=r"$\lambda_{\mathrm{mean}}(t)$")
            ax.plot(time, lam, "o", markersize=2.5, alpha=0.8)
        else:
            ax.plot(time, lam, "-o", markersize=2.5, linewidth=2.0,
                    label=r"$\lambda_{\mathrm{mean}}(t)$")
        li = convergence["lambda_inf"]
        if np.isfinite(li):
            ax.axhline(li, linestyle="--", linewidth=1.8,
                       label=rf"$\lambda_\infty \approx {li:.5g}$")
            tol = convergence["tolerance"]
            ax.axhline(li * (1 - tol), linestyle=":", linewidth=1.2,
                       label=rf"$\pm{100*tol:.1f}\%$ band")
            ax.axhline(li * (1 + tol), linestyle=":", linewidth=1.2)
        tc = convergence["t_convergence"]
        if convergence["converged"] and np.isfinite(tc):
            ax.axvline(tc, color="green", linestyle="-.", linewidth=1.8,
                       label=rf"$t_c \approx {tc:.5g}$")
    ax.set_xlabel("Time", fontsize=12)
    ax.set_ylabel("Mean free path", fontsize=12)
    ax.set_title("Dynamic Evolution of Mean Free Path", fontsize=14)
    ax.grid(True, alpha=0.3)
    ax.legend(fontsize=10)
    fig.tight_layout()
    plot_path = os.path.join(output_dir, f"{filename_prefix}_plot.png")
    fig.savefig(plot_path, dpi=300, bbox_inches="tight")
    if show:
        plt.show()
    else:
        plt.close(fig)

    print("\nMean-free-path analysis complete.")
    print(f"  lambda_inf    = {convergence['lambda_inf']}")
    print(f"  converged     = {convergence['converged']}")
    print(f"  t_convergence = {convergence['t_convergence']}")
    print(f"  plot saved    : {plot_path}")
    return {"mfp_df": mfp_df, "convergence": convergence,
            "plot_path": plot_path}


# DBSCAN cluster analysis on interpolated states

def _make_output_dir(path):
    if not os.path.isabs(path):
        path = os.path.join(_SCRIPT_DIR, path)
    os.makedirs(path, exist_ok=True)
    return path


def run_dbscan(positions, radii, eps=None, min_samples=3):
    """Apply DBSCAN to one configuration and return labels and settings."""
    if not SKLEARN_AVAILABLE:
        raise ImportError("scikit-learn is required for DBSCAN analysis.")
    positions = np.asarray(positions, dtype=float)
    radii = np.asarray(radii, dtype=float)
    if eps is None:
        eps = 2.5 * np.max(radii)
    model = DBSCAN(eps=eps, min_samples=min_samples,
                   metric="euclidean", algorithm="kd_tree")
    labels = model.fit_predict(positions)
    n_clusters = len(set(labels) - {-1})
    return labels, n_clusters, eps


def compute_cluster_metrics(labels):
    labels = np.asarray(labels)
    N = len(labels)
    cluster_labels = sorted(lab for lab in np.unique(labels) if lab != -1)
    sizes = [int(np.sum(labels == lab)) for lab in cluster_labels]
    n_clustered = int(np.sum(labels != -1))
    n_noise = int(np.sum(labels == -1))
    return {
        "n_clusters": len(cluster_labels),
        "cluster_sizes": sizes,
        "largest_cluster": max(sizes) if sizes else 0,
        "mean_cluster_size": float(np.mean(sizes)) if sizes else 0.0,
        "clustered_fraction": n_clustered / N,
        "noise_fraction": n_noise / N,
        "n_clustered_particles": n_clustered,
        "n_noise_particles": n_noise,
    }


def cluster_analysis_uniform_time(results, radii, T_final=None, n_times=300,
                                  eps=None, min_samples=3,
                                  progress_interval=25, store_positions=True):
    """Run DBSCAN on states interpolated to a uniform time grid."""
    if T_final is None:
        T_final = float(results["event_times"][-1])
    radii = np.asarray(radii, dtype=float)
    times = np.linspace(0.0, T_final, n_times)

    positions_over_time = [] if store_positions else None
    labels_over_time = []
    counts, largest, meansize = [], [], []
    cfrac, nfrac, nclust, nnoise, sizes_t = [], [], [], [], []
    eps_used = None

    print("\nRunning DBSCAN on uniform time grid...")
    print(f"  Time points : {n_times} | Particles : {len(radii)} | "
          f"eps input : {eps} | min_samples : {min_samples}")
    t0 = _time.perf_counter()

    for k, tt in enumerate(times):
        positions, _ = interpolate_state(results, tt)
        labels, _, eps_used = run_dbscan(positions, radii, eps, min_samples)
        m = compute_cluster_metrics(labels)
        if store_positions:
            positions_over_time.append(np.asarray(positions, float).copy())
        labels_over_time.append(labels.copy())
        counts.append(m["n_clusters"]); largest.append(m["largest_cluster"])
        meansize.append(m["mean_cluster_size"])
        cfrac.append(m["clustered_fraction"]); nfrac.append(m["noise_fraction"])
        nclust.append(m["n_clustered_particles"])
        nnoise.append(m["n_noise_particles"])
        sizes_t.append(m["cluster_sizes"])
        if k == 0 or k == n_times - 1 or (k + 1) % progress_interval == 0:
            el = _time.perf_counter() - t0
            print(f"  DBSCAN {k+1:5d}/{n_times} | t={tt:8.3f} | "
                  f"clusters={m['n_clusters']:4d} | "
                  f"largest={m['largest_cluster']:4d} | "
                  f"noise={m['noise_fraction']:.3f} | {el:.1f}s")

    print(f"DBSCAN uniform-time analysis done in "
          f"{_time.perf_counter()-t0:.2f}s (eps={eps_used}).")
    return {
        "times": np.asarray(times, float),
        "positions_over_time": positions_over_time,
        "labels_over_time": labels_over_time,
        "cluster_counts": np.asarray(counts, int),
        "largest_clusters": np.asarray(largest, int),
        "mean_cluster_sizes": np.asarray(meansize, float),
        "clustered_fractions": np.asarray(cfrac, float),
        "noise_fractions": np.asarray(nfrac, float),
        "n_clustered_particles": np.asarray(nclust, int),
        "n_noise_particles": np.asarray(nnoise, int),
        "cluster_sizes_over_time": sizes_t,
        "eps_used": eps_used, "min_samples": min_samples, "n_times": n_times,
    }


def save_dbscan_summary_table(cluster_summary, output_dir="dbscan_analysis",
                              filename="dbscan_summary.csv"):
    output_dir = _make_output_dir(output_dir)
    df = pd.DataFrame({
        "time": cluster_summary["times"],
        "n_clusters": cluster_summary["cluster_counts"],
        "largest_cluster": cluster_summary["largest_clusters"],
        "mean_cluster_size": cluster_summary["mean_cluster_sizes"],
        "clustered_fraction": cluster_summary["clustered_fractions"],
        "noise_fraction": cluster_summary["noise_fractions"],
        "n_clustered_particles": cluster_summary["n_clustered_particles"],
        "n_noise_particles": cluster_summary["n_noise_particles"],
    })
    path = os.path.join(output_dir, filename)
    df.to_csv(path, index=False)
    print(f"DBSCAN summary table saved: {path}")
    return path


def plot_cluster_metric(times, values, ylabel, title, case_name="case",
                        output_dir="dbscan_metric_plots", filename=None,
                        dpi=300, show_plot=False):
    output_dir = _make_output_dir(output_dir)
    if filename is None:
        filename = f"{title.lower().replace(' ', '_')}_{case_name}.png"
    path = os.path.join(output_dir, filename)
    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(8, 5), dpi=dpi)
    ax.plot(times, values, "-o", linewidth=2.0, markersize=3.0,
            label=case_name)
    ax.set_xlabel("Time", fontsize=12)
    ax.set_ylabel(ylabel, fontsize=12)
    ax.set_title(title, fontsize=13, pad=10)
    ax.grid(True, linestyle="--", linewidth=0.6, alpha=0.35)
    ax.legend(frameon=True, fontsize=10)
    fig.tight_layout()
    fig.savefig(path, dpi=dpi, bbox_inches="tight")
    if show_plot:
        plt.show()
    else:
        plt.close(fig)
    print(f"Metric plot saved: {path}")
    return path


def plot_all_cluster_metrics(cluster_summary, case_name="case",
                             output_dir="dbscan_metric_plots",
                             dpi=300, show_plot=False):
    times = cluster_summary["times"]
    paths = {}
    specs = [
        ("cluster_counts", "Number of clusters",
         "Number of DBSCAN Clusters vs Time", f"cluster_count_{case_name}.png"),
        ("largest_clusters", "Largest cluster size",
         "Largest DBSCAN Cluster Size vs Time", f"largest_cluster_{case_name}.png"),
        ("mean_cluster_sizes", "Mean cluster size",
         "Mean DBSCAN Cluster Size vs Time", f"mean_cluster_size_{case_name}.png"),
        ("clustered_fractions", "Clustered fraction",
         "DBSCAN Clustered Fraction vs Time", f"clustered_fraction_{case_name}.png"),
        ("noise_fractions", "Noise fraction",
         "DBSCAN Noise Fraction vs Time", f"noise_fraction_{case_name}.png"),
    ]
    for key, ylabel, title, fname in specs:
        paths[key] = plot_cluster_metric(
            times, cluster_summary[key], ylabel, title, case_name,
            output_dir, fname, dpi, show_plot)
    return paths


def plot_dbscan_uniform_snapshots(results, radii, cluster_summary, L=None,
                                  W=None, case_name="case",
                                  output_dir="dbscan_cluster_plots",
                                  dpi=250, figsize=(12, 6), image_format="png",
                                  max_legend_clusters=20, show_plot=False,
                                  progress_interval=10):
    output_dir = _make_output_dir(output_dir)
    if L is None:
        L = results["L"]
    if W is None:
        W = results["W"]
    radii = np.asarray(radii, float)
    N = len(radii)
    times = cluster_summary["times"]
    pos_t = cluster_summary["positions_over_time"]
    lab_t = cluster_summary["labels_over_time"]
    if pos_t is None:
        raise ValueError("cluster_summary has no stored positions "
                         "(store_positions=True).")
    palette = ["tab:blue", "tab:orange", "tab:green", "tab:red", "tab:purple",
               "tab:brown", "tab:pink", "tab:olive", "tab:cyan", "limegreen",
               "forestgreen", "seagreen", "mediumseagreen", "gold",
               "darkorange", "crimson", "deepskyblue", "mediumvioletred",
               "slateblue", "teal", "darkgreen", "chartreuse"]
    saved = []
    print(f"\nSaving {len(times)} static DBSCAN cluster plots to {output_dir}")
    for k in range(len(times)):
        tt = float(times[k])
        positions = np.asarray(pos_t[k], float)
        labels = np.asarray(lab_t[k])
        uniq = np.unique(labels)
        non_noise = [lab for lab in uniq if lab != -1]
        cmap = {}
        c = 0
        for lab in uniq:
            if lab == -1:
                cmap[lab] = "lightgray"
            else:
                cmap[lab] = palette[c % len(palette)]
                c += 1
        fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=figsize, dpi=dpi)
        ax.add_patch(Rectangle((0, 0), L, W, fill=False, linewidth=1.8,
                               edgecolor="black", zorder=2))
        colors = [cmap[lab] for lab in labels]
        pts_per_unit = (figsize[0] * 72.0) / L
        ms = (radii * pts_per_unit) ** 2
        ax.scatter(positions[:, 0], positions[:, 1], s=ms, c=colors,
                   edgecolors="black", linewidths=0.25 if N > 1000 else 0.45,
                   alpha=0.88, zorder=3)
        if N <= 1000:
            handles = []
            nnoise = int(np.sum(labels == -1))
            if nnoise > 0:
                handles.append(Line2D([0], [0], marker="o", linewidth=0,
                               color="w", markerfacecolor="lightgray",
                               markeredgecolor="black", markersize=7,
                               label=f"Noise ({nnoise})"))
            for lab in non_noise[:max_legend_clusters]:
                handles.append(Line2D([0], [0], marker="o", linewidth=0,
                               color="w", markerfacecolor=cmap[lab],
                               markeredgecolor="black", markersize=7,
                               label=f"Cluster {lab} ({int(np.sum(labels==lab))})"))
            if handles:
                ax.legend(handles=handles, fontsize=8, frameon=True,
                          loc="upper right", framealpha=0.95)
        ax.set_xlim(0, L); ax.set_ylim(0, W)
        ax.set_aspect("equal", adjustable="box")
        ax.set_xlabel("x", fontsize=12); ax.set_ylabel("y", fontsize=12)
        ax.set_title(f"DBSCAN Clusters: {case_name}\n"
                     f"$t={tt:.4f}$, clusters="
                     f"{int(cluster_summary['cluster_counts'][k])}, "
                     f"largest={int(cluster_summary['largest_clusters'][k])}, "
                     f"clustered fraction="
                     f"{float(cluster_summary['clustered_fractions'][k]):.3f}",
                     fontsize=13, pad=10)
        fig.tight_layout()
        path = os.path.join(output_dir,
               f"dbscan_{case_name}_frame_{k:04d}_t_{tt:.4f}.{image_format}")
        fig.savefig(path, dpi=dpi, bbox_inches="tight")
        if show_plot:
            plt.show()
        else:
            plt.close(fig)
        saved.append(path)
        if k == 0 or k == len(times) - 1 or (k + 1) % progress_interval == 0:
            print(f"  saved DBSCAN plot {k+1:5d}/{len(times)}")
    print(f"Saved {len(saved)} DBSCAN cluster plots in: {output_dir}")
    return saved


def animate_dbscan_uniform(results, radii, L=None, W=None, T_final=None,
                           n_frames=1800, fps=60, eps=None, min_samples=3,
                           case_name="case", output_dir="dbscan_animation",
                           filename="dbscan_animation.mp4", dpi=120,
                           figsize=(12, 6), progress_interval=100,
                           show_metrics=True):
    output_dir = _make_output_dir(output_dir)
    if L is None:
        L = results["L"]
    if W is None:
        W = results["W"]
    if T_final is None:
        T_final = float(results["event_times"][-1])
    radii = np.asarray(radii, float)
    N = len(radii)

    summary = cluster_analysis_uniform_time(
        results, radii, T_final=T_final, n_times=n_frames, eps=eps,
        min_samples=min_samples, progress_interval=progress_interval,
        store_positions=True)
    times = summary["times"]
    pos_t = summary["positions_over_time"]
    lab_t = summary["labels_over_time"]
    eps_used = summary["eps_used"]

    palette = ["tab:blue", "tab:orange", "tab:green", "tab:red", "tab:purple",
               "tab:brown", "tab:pink", "tab:olive", "tab:cyan", "limegreen",
               "forestgreen", "seagreen", "mediumseagreen", "gold",
               "darkorange", "crimson", "deepskyblue", "mediumvioletred",
               "slateblue", "teal", "darkgreen", "chartreuse"]

    def labels_to_colors(labels):
        labels = np.asarray(labels)
        cmap = {-1: "lightgray"}
        c = 0
        for lab in np.unique(labels):
            if lab == -1:
                continue
            cmap[lab] = palette[c % len(palette)]
            c += 1
        return [cmap[lab] for lab in labels]

    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=figsize, dpi=dpi)
    fig.patch.set_facecolor("black")
    ax.set_facecolor("#0d1b2a")
    ax.set_xlim(0, L); ax.set_ylim(0, W)
    ax.set_aspect("equal"); ax.set_xticks([]); ax.set_yticks([])
    for sp in ax.spines.values():
        sp.set_edgecolor("#4a9eff"); sp.set_linewidth(2.5)
    pts_per_unit = (figsize[0] * 72.0) / L
    ms = (radii * pts_per_unit) ** 2

    scat = ax.scatter(pos_t[0][:, 0], pos_t[0][:, 1], s=ms,
                      c=labels_to_colors(lab_t[0]), edgecolors="white",
                      linewidths=0.2 if N > 1000 else 0.4, alpha=0.92, zorder=3)
    time_text = ax.text(0.012, 0.97, "", transform=ax.transAxes, color="white",
                        fontsize=11, fontfamily="monospace", va="top", zorder=5)
    info_text = ax.text(0.012, 0.90,
                        f"N = {N} | eps = {eps_used:.4f} | "
                        f"min_samples = {min_samples}",
                        transform=ax.transAxes, color="#a8b8c8", fontsize=8.5,
                        fontfamily="monospace", va="top", zorder=5)
    metric_text = ax.text(0.012, 0.845, "", transform=ax.transAxes,
                          color="#a8b8c8", fontsize=8.5,
                          fontfamily="monospace", va="top", zorder=5)
    frame_text = ax.text(0.988, 0.97, "", transform=ax.transAxes,
                         color="#a8b8c8", fontsize=8.5, fontfamily="monospace",
                         va="top", ha="right", zorder=5)
    ax.axhspan(0, W * 0.013, color="#1a2a3a", zorder=4, alpha=0.85)
    bar_fill, = ax.fill([0, 0, 0, 0], [0, W*0.013, W*0.013, 0],
                        color="#4a9eff", zorder=5, alpha=0.85)
    t_render = _time.perf_counter()

    def update(frame):
        scat.set_offsets(pos_t[frame])
        scat.set_facecolors(labels_to_colors(lab_t[frame]))
        pct = (frame + 1) / n_frames
        time_text.set_text(f"t = {times[frame]:.3f}")
        frame_text.set_text(f"{frame+1} / {n_frames}")
        if show_metrics:
            metric_text.set_text(
                f"clusters = {summary['cluster_counts'][frame]} | "
                f"largest = {summary['largest_clusters'][frame]} | "
                f"clustered = {summary['clustered_fractions'][frame]:.3f} | "
                f"noise = {summary['noise_fractions'][frame]:.3f}")
        bar_fill.set_xy([[0, 0], [L*pct, 0], [L*pct, W*0.013], [0, W*0.013]])
        if frame == 0 or frame == n_frames - 1 or (frame+1) % progress_interval == 0:
            el = _time.perf_counter() - t_render
            rfps = (frame + 1) / max(el, 1e-12)
            print(f"  render frame {frame+1:5d}/{n_frames} "
                  f"({100*pct:5.1f}%) | {rfps:6.1f} fps")
        return [scat, time_text, info_text, metric_text, frame_text, bar_fill]

    anim = FuncAnimation(fig, update, frames=n_frames, interval=1000/fps,
                         blit=True, repeat=False)
    writer = FFMpegWriter(fps=fps, codec="libx264", bitrate=8000,
                          extra_args=["-pix_fmt", "yuv420p", "-preset", "slow",
                                      "-crf", "18"])
    path = os.path.join(output_dir, filename)
    anim.save(path, writer=writer)
    plt.close(fig)
    print(f"\nDBSCAN animation saved: {path}")
    return {"animation_path": path, "cluster_summary": summary}


def run_complete_dbscan_workflow(results, radii, L=None, W=None, T_final=None,
                                 eps=None, min_samples=3, case_name="case",
                                 base_output_dir="dbscan_analysis_complete",
                                 n_metric_times=300, n_plot_times=50,
                                 n_animation_frames=1800, make_static_plots=True,
                                 make_animation=True, dpi_metrics=300,
                                 dpi_static=250, dpi_animation=120, fps=60):
    """Run DBSCAN metrics, static plots, and optional animation."""
    if not SKLEARN_AVAILABLE:
        print("scikit-learn not available — skipping DBSCAN workflow.")
        return None
    base_output_dir = _make_output_dir(base_output_dir)
    if L is None:
        L = results["L"]
    if W is None:
        W = results["W"]
    if T_final is None:
        T_final = float(results["event_times"][-1])

    print("\n" + "=" * 70)
    print("COMPLETE DBSCAN WORKFLOW")
    print(f"Metric points: {n_metric_times} | Static plots: {n_plot_times} | "
          f"Animation frames: {n_animation_frames}")
    print("=" * 70)

    metric_summary = cluster_analysis_uniform_time(
        results, radii, T_final=T_final, n_times=n_metric_times, eps=eps,
        min_samples=min_samples, progress_interval=max(1, n_metric_times // 10),
        store_positions=False)
    summary_csv = save_dbscan_summary_table(
        metric_summary, output_dir=base_output_dir,
        filename=f"dbscan_metric_summary_{case_name}.csv")
    metric_paths = plot_all_cluster_metrics(
        metric_summary, case_name=case_name,
        output_dir=os.path.join(base_output_dir, "metric_plots"),
        dpi=dpi_metrics, show_plot=False)

    static_summary = static_paths = None
    if make_static_plots:
        static_summary = cluster_analysis_uniform_time(
            results, radii, T_final=T_final, n_times=n_plot_times, eps=eps,
            min_samples=min_samples,
            progress_interval=max(1, n_plot_times // 10), store_positions=True)
        static_paths = plot_dbscan_uniform_snapshots(
            results, radii, static_summary, L=L, W=W, case_name=case_name,
            output_dir=os.path.join(base_output_dir, "static_cluster_plots"),
            dpi=dpi_static, show_plot=False,
            progress_interval=max(1, n_plot_times // 10))

    animation_output = None
    if make_animation:
        animation_output = animate_dbscan_uniform(
            results, radii, L=L, W=W, T_final=T_final,
            n_frames=n_animation_frames, fps=fps, eps=eps,
            min_samples=min_samples, case_name=case_name,
            output_dir=os.path.join(base_output_dir, "animation"),
            filename=f"dbscan_animation_{case_name}.mp4", dpi=dpi_animation,
            progress_interval=max(1, n_animation_frames // 18), show_metrics=True)

    print("\n" + "=" * 70)
    print(f"DBSCAN WORKFLOW COMPLETE — outputs under: {base_output_dir}")
    print("=" * 70)
    return {"metric_summary": metric_summary, "static_summary": static_summary,
            "summary_csv": summary_csv, "metric_paths": metric_paths,
            "static_paths": static_paths, "animation_output": animation_output}



# Stand-alone demonstration

if __name__ == "__main__":

    # Demonstrate polydisperse disks in a Taylor--Green carrier flow, followed
    # by the plotting, animation, mean-free-path, and DBSCAN analyses. The DNS
    # study is launched separately through run_study.py.

    # Initial-state generators.
    def get_rng(rng=None):
        """Return a fresh, seeded, or supplied NumPy random generator."""
        if rng is None:
            return np.random.default_rng()
        if isinstance(rng, (int, np.integer)):
            return np.random.default_rng(rng)
        if isinstance(rng, np.random.Generator):
            return rng
        raise ValueError("rng must be None, int, or Generator.")

    def sample_masses(N, mass_distribution="constant",
                      mass_value=1.0, mass_low=0.5, mass_high=2.0,
                      mass_mean=1.0, mass_std=0.2, mass_rng=None):
        """Draw ``N`` constant, uniform, or positive Gaussian masses."""
        rng = get_rng(mass_rng)
        if mass_distribution == "constant":
            return np.full(N, mass_value, dtype=float)
        if mass_distribution == "uniform":
            return rng.uniform(mass_low, mass_high, size=N)
        if mass_distribution == "gaussian":
            masses = np.empty(N)
            for i in range(N):
                while True:
                    x = rng.normal(mass_mean, mass_std)
                    if x > 0: masses[i] = x; break
            return masses
        raise ValueError(f"Unknown mass_distribution {mass_distribution!r}; "
                         f"use 'constant', 'uniform' or 'gaussian'.")

    def sample_radii(
            N,
            L,
            W,
            radii_distribution="constant",
            radii_value=0.25,
            radii_low=0.1,
            radii_high=0.3,
            radii_mean=0.2,
            radii_std=0.03,
            target_eta=None,
            radii_rng=None,
            safety_factor=0.999):
        """Generate constant, uniform, or truncated-Gaussian radii.

        When ``target_eta`` is given, radii are rescaled before placement so
        that ``sum(pi r_i^2) / (L W) = target_eta``.
        """

        rng = get_rng(radii_rng)

        if N <= 0:
            raise ValueError("N must be positive.")

        if L <= 0 or W <= 0:
            raise ValueError("L and W must be positive.")

        r_max_allowed = 0.5 * min(L, W) * safety_factor

        if radii_distribution == "constant":

            if radii_value <= 0:
                raise ValueError("radii_value must be positive.")

            radii = np.full(N, radii_value, dtype=float)

        elif radii_distribution == "uniform":

            if radii_low <= 0 or radii_high <= 0:
                raise ValueError("radii_low and radii_high must be positive.")

            if radii_low >= radii_high:
                raise ValueError("radii_low must be smaller than radii_high.")

            upper = min(radii_high, r_max_allowed)

            if radii_low >= upper:
                raise ValueError("radii_low is too large for the domain.")

            radii = rng.uniform(radii_low, upper, size=N)

        elif radii_distribution == "gaussian":

            if radii_mean <= 0:
                raise ValueError("radii_mean must be positive.")

            if radii_std <= 0:
                raise ValueError("radii_std must be positive.")

            radii = np.empty(N, dtype=float)

            for i in range(N):
                while True:
                    r = rng.normal(radii_mean, radii_std)
                    if 0.0 < r < r_max_allowed:
                        radii[i] = r
                        break

        else:
            raise ValueError(f"Unknown radii_distribution "
                             f"{radii_distribution!r}; use 'constant', "
                             f"'uniform' or 'gaussian'.")

        if np.any(radii <= 0):
            raise ValueError("All radii must be positive.")

        if np.any(2.0 * radii >= min(L, W)):
            raise ValueError("At least one radius is too large for the domain.")

        # Rescale to the requested surface concentration when specified.
        if target_eta is not None:

            if not (0.0 < target_eta < 1.0):
                raise ValueError("target_eta must satisfy 0 < target_eta < 1.")

            current_eta = np.sum(np.pi * radii**2) / (L * W)

            if current_eta <= 0:
                raise ValueError("Current concentration is zero.")

            scale = np.sqrt(target_eta / current_eta)
            radii = radii * scale

            if np.any(2.0 * radii >= min(L, W)):
                raise ValueError(
                    "After scaling to target_eta, at least one radius is too large."
                )

        return radii

    def sample_positions(
            L,
            W,
            radii,
            positions_distribution="uniform",
            boundary="wall",
            mean_position=None,
            std_position=None,
            mix_proportion=0.5,
            use_grid_placement=False,
            grid_jitter=0.0,
            max_failed_trials_per_particle=10000,
            pos_rng=None,
            eps=1e-12,
            print_rejection_progress=True,
            progress_interval=500,
            spacing_radius=None):
        """Generate non-overlapping positions for polydisperse hard disks.

        Grid placement uses conservative spacing based on the largest placement
        radius. Sequential placement draws random positions until all particles
        are placed or one particle reaches the trial limit. Larger particles
        are placed first in both modes.

        ``spacing_radius`` controls grid spacing and rejection distance without
        changing the collision radius. Supplying the same physical value to a
        paired collisional and collisionless run preserves their seeded initial
        configuration.
        """

        rng = get_rng(pos_rng)

        radii = np.asarray(radii, dtype=float)
        N_requested = len(radii)

        if L <= 0 or W <= 0:
            raise ValueError("L and W must be positive.")

        if N_requested == 0:
            raise ValueError("radii must not be empty.")

        if np.any(radii <= 0):
            raise ValueError("All radii must be positive.")

        if np.any(2.0 * radii >= min(L, W)):
            raise ValueError("Some particles are too large for the domain.")

        if boundary not in ("wall", "periodic"):
            raise ValueError("boundary must be 'wall' or 'periodic'.")

        if positions_distribution not in ("uniform", "gaussian", "mixed"):
            raise ValueError(
                "positions_distribution must be 'uniform', 'gaussian', or 'mixed'."
            )

        if max_failed_trials_per_particle <= 0:
            raise ValueError("max_failed_trials_per_particle must be positive.")

        if progress_interval <= 0:
            raise ValueError("progress_interval must be positive.")

        if grid_jitter < 0:
            raise ValueError("grid_jitter must be nonnegative.")

        if positions_distribution in ("gaussian", "mixed"):

            if mean_position is None:
                mean_position = np.array([L / 2.0, W / 2.0], dtype=float)
            else:
                mean_position = np.asarray(mean_position, dtype=float)

            if mean_position.shape != (2,):
                raise ValueError("mean_position must have shape (2,).")

            if std_position is None:
                std_position = min(L, W) / 10.0

            if std_position <= 0:
                raise ValueError("std_position must be positive.")

        if positions_distribution == "mixed":
            if not (0.0 <= mix_proportion <= 1.0):
                raise ValueError("mix_proportion must satisfy 0 <= mix_proportion <= 1.")

        eta_requested = np.sum(np.pi * radii**2) / (L * W)

        # Place larger particles first because they are harder to insert later.
        placement_order = np.argsort(-radii)

        # Grid placement.

        if use_grid_placement:

            if print_rejection_progress:
                print("\nGrid placement for polydisperse disks")
                print("--------------------------------------")
                print(f"Requested particles       : {N_requested}")
                print(f"Boundary                  : {boundary}")
                print(f"Distribution              : {positions_distribution}")

            # A separate placement radius preserves the lattice of a paired
            # collisionless run.
            r_max = float(np.max(radii) if spacing_radius is None
                          else spacing_radius)

            # Conservative spacing: guarantees non-overlap for all particles
            # because it is based on the placement radius.
            safe_spacing = 2.1 * r_max

            if boundary == "wall":
                x_values = np.arange(r_max, L - r_max + eps, safe_spacing)
                y_values = np.arange(r_max, W - r_max + eps, safe_spacing)
            else:
                x_values = np.arange(0.0, L, safe_spacing)
                y_values = np.arange(0.0, W, safe_spacing)

            grid_points = np.array(
                [(x, y) for y in y_values for x in x_values],
                dtype=float
            )

            n_grid_sites = len(grid_points)

            if n_grid_sites == 0:
                raise RuntimeError("Grid placement failed: no valid grid sites available.")

            N_placed = min(N_requested, n_grid_sites)
            stopped_early = N_placed < N_requested

            # Place the largest N_placed particles.
            placed_indices = placement_order[:N_placed]

            if positions_distribution in ("uniform", "mixed"):

                chosen_sites = rng.choice(
                    n_grid_sites,
                    size=N_placed,
                    replace=False
                )

            elif positions_distribution == "gaussian":

                diff = grid_points - mean_position[None, :]

                if boundary == "periodic":
                    diff[:, 0] -= L * np.round(diff[:, 0] / L)
                    diff[:, 1] -= W * np.round(diff[:, 1] / W)

                dist2 = np.sum(diff**2, axis=1)

                weights = np.exp(-0.5 * dist2 / (std_position**2))
                weights = weights + 1e-300
                weights = weights / np.sum(weights)

                chosen_sites = rng.choice(
                    n_grid_sites,
                    size=N_placed,
                    replace=False,
                    p=weights
                )

            positions_placed = grid_points[chosen_sites].copy()

            # Jitter must remain small because only the unjittered grid has a
            # strict non-overlap guarantee.
            if grid_jitter > 0:

                jitter_scale = min(grid_jitter, 0.08) * safe_spacing

                positions_placed += rng.uniform(
                    -jitter_scale,
                    jitter_scale,
                    size=positions_placed.shape
                )

                if boundary == "periodic":
                    positions_placed = wrap_positions(positions_placed, L, W)
                else:
                    placed_radii = radii[placed_indices]

                    positions_placed[:, 0] = np.clip(
                        positions_placed[:, 0],
                        placed_radii,
                        L - placed_radii
                    )

                    positions_placed[:, 1] = np.clip(
                        positions_placed[:, 1],
                        placed_radii,
                        W - placed_radii
                    )

            eta_actual = np.sum(np.pi * radii[placed_indices]**2) / (L * W)

            placement_info = {
                "placement_mode": "grid_polydisperse",
                "N_requested": int(N_requested),
                "N_placed": int(N_placed),
                "eta_requested": float(eta_requested),
                "eta_actual": float(eta_actual),
                "grid_sites_available": int(n_grid_sites),
                "total_trials": int(N_placed),
                "failed_trials_at_stop": 0,
                "max_failed_trials_per_particle": int(max_failed_trials_per_particle),
                "stopped_early": bool(stopped_early),
                "stop_reason": (
                    "grid could not hold all requested particles"
                    if stopped_early
                    else "all requested particles placed on grid"
                ),
                "placed_indices": placed_indices.copy(),
            }

            if print_rejection_progress:
                print("\nGrid placement summary")
                print("----------------------")
                print(f"Grid sites available       : {n_grid_sites}")
                print(f"Requested particles        : {N_requested}")
                print(f"Successfully placed        : {N_placed}")
                print(f"Requested concentration    : {eta_requested:.6f}")
                print(f"Actual concentration       : {eta_actual:.6f}")
                print(f"Stopped early              : {stopped_early}")
                print(f"Stop reason                : {placement_info['stop_reason']}")

            return positions_placed, placed_indices, placement_info

        # Sequential random placement.

        positions = []
        placed_indices = []

        total_trials = 0
        stopped_early = False
        stop_reason = "all requested particles placed"
        failed_trials_at_stop = 0

        def propose_position(r):

            if positions_distribution == "uniform":

                if boundary == "wall":
                    x = rng.uniform(r, L - r)
                    y = rng.uniform(r, W - r)
                else:
                    x = rng.uniform(0.0, L)
                    y = rng.uniform(0.0, W)

                return np.array([x, y], dtype=float)

            elif positions_distribution == "gaussian":

                candidate = rng.normal(
                    loc=mean_position,
                    scale=std_position,
                    size=2
                )

                if boundary == "periodic":
                    candidate = wrap_positions(candidate.reshape(1, 2), L, W)[0]
                else:
                    if candidate[0] < r or candidate[0] > L - r:
                        return None
                    if candidate[1] < r or candidate[1] > W - r:
                        return None

                return candidate.astype(float)

            elif positions_distribution == "mixed":

                use_gaussian = rng.random() < mix_proportion

                if use_gaussian:
                    candidate = rng.normal(
                        loc=mean_position,
                        scale=std_position,
                        size=2
                    )

                    if boundary == "periodic":
                        candidate = wrap_positions(candidate.reshape(1, 2), L, W)[0]
                    else:
                        if candidate[0] < r or candidate[0] > L - r:
                            return None
                        if candidate[1] < r or candidate[1] > W - r:
                            return None

                    return candidate.astype(float)

                else:
                    if boundary == "wall":
                        x = rng.uniform(r, L - r)
                        y = rng.uniform(r, W - r)
                    else:
                        x = rng.uniform(0.0, L)
                        y = rng.uniform(0.0, W)

                    return np.array([x, y], dtype=float)

        def candidate_is_valid(candidate, r_new):

            if candidate is None:
                return False

            if len(positions) == 0:
                return True

            existing_pos = np.asarray(positions, dtype=float)
            existing_indices = np.asarray(placed_indices, dtype=int)
            # Use placement rather than collision radii so paired runs consume
            # the same random stream.
            existing_radii = (radii[existing_indices] if spacing_radius is None
                              else np.full(len(existing_indices),
                                           float(spacing_radius)))

            dx = existing_pos[:, 0] - candidate[0]
            dy = existing_pos[:, 1] - candidate[1]

            if boundary == "periodic":
                dx -= L * np.round(dx / L)
                dy -= W * np.round(dy / W)

            dist2 = dx * dx + dy * dy
            r_new_eff = (r_new if spacing_radius is None
                         else float(spacing_radius))
            min_dist = existing_radii + r_new_eff

            return not np.any(dist2 < min_dist**2 - eps)

        t0 = _time.perf_counter()

        if print_rejection_progress:
            print("\nSequential random placement for polydisperse disks")
            print("--------------------------------------------------")
            print(f"Requested particles              : {N_requested}")
            print(f"Boundary                         : {boundary}")
            print(f"Distribution                     : {positions_distribution}")
            print(f"Max failed trials per particle   : {max_failed_trials_per_particle}")

        for original_idx in placement_order:

            r = radii[original_idx]

            placed = False
            failed_trials = 0

            while failed_trials < max_failed_trials_per_particle:

                total_trials += 1

                candidate = propose_position(r)
                if candidate is None:
                    continue

                if candidate_is_valid(candidate, r):
                    positions.append(candidate)
                    placed_indices.append(original_idx)
                    placed = True
                    break

                failed_trials += 1

            if not placed:
                stopped_early = True
                failed_trials_at_stop = failed_trials
                stop_reason = (
                    f"failed to place particle {original_idx} after "
                    f"{max_failed_trials_per_particle} failed trials"
                )
                break

            if (
                print_rejection_progress
                and (
                    len(positions) == 1
                    or len(positions) == N_requested
                    or len(positions) % progress_interval == 0
                )
            ):
                elapsed = _time.perf_counter() - t0
                print(
                    f"  placed {len(positions):6d}/{N_requested} "
                    f"| total trials={total_trials:9d} "
                    f"| elapsed={elapsed:7.2f}s"
                )

        positions_placed = np.asarray(positions, dtype=float)
        placed_indices = np.asarray(placed_indices, dtype=int)

        N_placed = len(positions_placed)

        if N_placed == 0:
            raise RuntimeError("No particles could be placed.")

        eta_actual = np.sum(np.pi * radii[placed_indices]**2) / (L * W)

        placement_info = {
            "placement_mode": "random_sequential_polydisperse",
            "N_requested": int(N_requested),
            "N_placed": int(N_placed),
            "eta_requested": float(eta_requested),
            "eta_actual": float(eta_actual),
            "grid_sites_available": np.nan,
            "total_trials": int(total_trials),
            "failed_trials_at_stop": int(failed_trials_at_stop),
            "max_failed_trials_per_particle": int(max_failed_trials_per_particle),
            "stopped_early": bool(stopped_early),
            "stop_reason": stop_reason,
            "placed_indices": placed_indices.copy(),
        }

        if print_rejection_progress:
            elapsed = _time.perf_counter() - t0
            print("\nSequential random placement summary")
            print("-----------------------------------")
            print(f"Requested particles      : {N_requested}")
            print(f"Successfully placed      : {N_placed}")
            print(f"Requested concentration  : {eta_requested:.6f}")
            print(f"Actual concentration     : {eta_actual:.6f}")
            print(f"Total placement trials   : {total_trials}")
            print(f"Stopped early            : {stopped_early}")
            print(f"Stop reason              : {stop_reason}")
            print(f"Placement time           : {elapsed:.2f}s")

        return positions_placed, placed_indices, placement_info

    def sample_velocities(N, vel_distribution="gaussian",
                          vmin=-1.0, vmax=1.0,
                          vel_mean=0.0, vel_std=1.0,
                          vel_rng=None, remove_mean_velocity=False,
                          masses=None):
        """Draw ``(N, 2)`` velocities, optionally removing centre-of-mass motion."""
        rng = get_rng(vel_rng)
        if vel_distribution == "uniform":
            velocities = rng.uniform(vmin, vmax, (N, 2))
        elif vel_distribution == "gaussian":
            velocities = rng.normal(vel_mean, vel_std, (N, 2))
        else:
            raise ValueError(f"Unknown vel_distribution "
                             f"{vel_distribution!r}; use 'uniform' or "
                             f"'gaussian'.")
        if remove_mean_velocity and masses is not None:
            v_cm       = (np.sum(masses[:,None]*velocities, axis=0)
                          / np.sum(masses))
            velocities -= v_cm
        return velocities

    def generate_initial_state(
            N,
            L,
            W,
            boundary="wall",
            # masses
            mass_distribution="constant",
            mass_value=1.0,
            mass_low=0.5,
            mass_high=2.0,
            mass_mean=1.0,
            mass_std=0.2,
            mass_rng=None,
            # radii
            radii_distribution="constant",
            radii_value=0.1,
            radii_low=0.1,
            radii_high=0.3,
            radii_mean=0.2,
            radii_std=0.03,
            target_eta=None,
            radii_rng=None,
            # positions
            positions_distribution="uniform",
            mean_position=None,
            std_position=None,
            mix_proportion=0.5,
            use_grid_placement=False,
            grid_jitter=0.0,
            max_failed_trials_per_particle=10000,
            pos_rng=None,
            print_rejection_progress=True,
            placement_progress_interval=500,
            spacing_radius=None,
            # velocities
            vel_distribution="gaussian",
            vmin=-1.0,
            vmax=1.0,
            vel_mean=0.0,
            vel_std=1.0,
            vel_rng=None,
            remove_mean_velocity=False,
            # output
            return_placement_info=False):
        """Generate a polydisperse initial state.

        Placement may return fewer than ``N`` particles if the selected method
        reaches its capacity or trial limit.
        """

        masses_requested = sample_masses(
            N,
            mass_distribution,
            mass_value,
            mass_low,
            mass_high,
            mass_mean,
            mass_std,
            mass_rng
        )

        radii_requested = sample_radii(
            N=N,
            L=L,
            W=W,
            radii_distribution=radii_distribution,
            radii_value=radii_value,
            radii_low=radii_low,
            radii_high=radii_high,
            radii_mean=radii_mean,
            radii_std=radii_std,
            target_eta=target_eta,
            radii_rng=radii_rng
        )

        positions, placed_indices, placement_info = sample_positions(
            L=L,
            W=W,
            radii=radii_requested,
            positions_distribution=positions_distribution,
            boundary=boundary,
            mean_position=mean_position,
            std_position=std_position,
            mix_proportion=mix_proportion,
            use_grid_placement=use_grid_placement,
            grid_jitter=grid_jitter,
            max_failed_trials_per_particle=max_failed_trials_per_particle,
            pos_rng=pos_rng,
            print_rejection_progress=print_rejection_progress,
            progress_interval=placement_progress_interval,
            spacing_radius=spacing_radius
        )

        masses = masses_requested[placed_indices]
        radii = radii_requested[placed_indices]

        N_placed = len(radii)

        velocities = sample_velocities(
            N_placed,
            vel_distribution,
            vmin,
            vmax,
            vel_mean,
            vel_std,
            vel_rng,
            remove_mean_velocity,
            masses
        )

        placement_info["mass_distribution"] = mass_distribution
        placement_info["radii_distribution"] = radii_distribution
        placement_info["positions_distribution"] = positions_distribution
        placement_info["velocity_distribution"] = vel_distribution

        if return_placement_info:
            return masses, radii, positions, velocities, placement_info

        return masses, radii, positions, velocities

    # Console and file logging.
    report_dir = os.path.join(_SCRIPT_DIR, "text_outputs")
    os.makedirs(report_dir, exist_ok=True)
    with open(os.path.join(report_dir, "console_output.txt"), "w",
              encoding="utf-8") as log_file:
        tee = Tee(sys.stdout, log_file)
        with contextlib.redirect_stdout(tee), contextlib.redirect_stderr(tee):

            # Demonstration configuration.
            # Domain and time.
            L = 60.0                   # box length along x
            W = 30.0                   # box width along z
            T_final = 240.0            # simulated time
            dt = 0.001                 # time step; keep ~0.1 * r_mean / v_max
                                       # so no pair can tunnel through
            # Contact parameters.
            e_ball = 0.9               # particle-particle restitution, [0, 1]
            e_wall = 1.0               # particle-wall restitution (wall BC only)
            boundary = "periodic"      # "periodic" or "wall"

            # Drag and centre-speed substrate friction.
            tau = 20.0                 # particle response time tau_p
            mu_frict = 0.05            # centre-speed Coulomb substrate friction
            g = 1.0                    # gravity magnitude (friction scale mu*g)
            U_flow = np.array([1.5, 0.5])   # only used for the terminal-speed
                                            # printout; the run itself uses
                                            # the Taylor-Green field below

            # Particle ensemble.
            N_target = 2000            # requested N; the placed N can be lower
                                       # if the random placement saturates

            warmup_numba()

            print("\nGenerating initial state...")
            masses, radii, positions, velocities, placement_info = \

                    radii_distribution="gaussian", radii_mean=0.25,
                    radii_std=0.03, radii_rng=12,
                    positions_distribution="gaussian",
                    use_grid_placement=False,
                    max_failed_trials_per_particle=10000,
                    mean_position=np.array([L / 2.0, W / 2.0]),
                    std_position=min(L, W) / 6.0,
                    pos_rng=123, print_rejection_progress=True,
                    placement_progress_interval=500,
                    vel_distribution="gaussian", vel_mean=0.0, vel_std=1,
                    vel_rng=42, remove_mean_velocity=True,
                    return_placement_info=True)

            N = len(radii)
            speeds = np.linalg.norm(velocities, axis=1)
            print("\nINITIAL-CONDITION DIAGNOSTICS")
            print("=" * 55)
            print(f"  Placement mode       : {placement_info['placement_mode']}")
            print(f"  Requested N          : {placement_info['N_requested']}")
            print(f"  Placed N             : {placement_info['N_placed']}")
            print(f"  Requested eta        : {placement_info['eta_requested']:.6f}")
            print(f"  Actual eta           : {placement_info['eta_actual']:.6f}")
            print(f"  Mean / max speed     : {speeds.mean():.4f} / {speeds.max():.4f}")
            print(f"  Mean radius          : {radii.mean():.4f}")
            print(f"  Packing fraction     : {np.sum(np.pi*radii**2)/(L*W):.6f}")
            print(f"  Terminal speed check : "

            print("=" * 55)

            velocity_field_snapshot_times = np.linspace(0, T_final, 20)

                                dpi=200, image_format="png", save_pdf=False)
            for config in ["initial", "final"]:
