"""DNS-backed velocity field for the two-dimensional hard-disk engine.

The pipeline component order is streamwise, spanwise, wall-normal,
``(x, z, y)``. Engine positions are two-dimensional: ``pos[:, 0]`` is
streamwise and ``pos[:, 1]`` is spanwise. Wall-normal ``y`` is distance from
the no-slip surface. A sphere of radius ``a`` resting on the wall is sampled
at its centre height, ``y = a``.

Lengths, times, and velocities remain in the DNS file's nondimensional units,
with ``h = 1``. The engine's ``tau`` is therefore a response time in file
units; wall quantities are reporting labels only. Particle motion is confined
to the plane ``y = a`` under a one-way-coupled point-particle model, requiring
``a <= A_MAX``, where ``A_MAX`` is the slab depth. Particle-number limits are
computed from the active geometry by ``particle_window``.
"""

from __future__ import annotations
import numpy as np

import slab_interp as si
from slab_interp import (SlabInterpolator, N_SPAN, N_STREAM, LX, LZ, LY,
                         HX, HZ, Y_NODES, NU, U_TAU, DELTA_NU, T_NU, RE_TAU,
                         to_wall_units, from_wall_units)

# ------------------------------------------------------------------ record
# Record limits are read from the active dataset.
T_START = float(si.T_FIRST)
T_END = float(si.T_LAST)
T_MAX = T_END - T_START                 # usable engine time
DT_SNAP = float(si.DT_SNAP)

DOMAIN = {"L": LX, "W": LZ}             # engine (L, W) = (streamwise, spanwise)
AREA = LX * LZ


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ radius
A_MAX = float(Y_NODES[-1])
# Geometric ceiling: the particle centre must lie within the slab.
DZ_MAX = float(np.diff(Y_NODES).max())
# Largest wall-normal step within the slab.
A_DEFAULT = 0.5 * DZ_MAX
# Choosing ``a = DZ_MAX/2`` places the centre between wall-normal nodes and
# exercises the interpolation. Any radius in ``(0, A_MAX]`` is admissible.


def cropped_domain(crop):
    """
    Return ``(L, W, AREA)`` for ``crop=n`` or ``(n_stream, n_span)``.

    Cropping creates a wrapped seam in the periodic interpolant; use
    ``slab_interp.seam_jump`` to quantify it for the active dataset.
    """
    n_stream, n_span = (crop, crop) if np.isscalar(crop) else crop
    L, W = n_stream * HX, n_span * HZ
    return {"L": L, "W": W, "AREA": L * W}


# ------------------------------------------------- particle-count window
def particle_window(a=None, area=None, min_contact_pairs=20):
    """
    Compute the admissible particle-count range for a given radius.

    The three radius-dependent constraints are

      collisional          MFP = A/(2 N a) < L_span      -> N > A/(2 a L_span)
      g(sigma) measurable  at least `min_contact_pairs`  -> N > ...
      one-way coupling     phi = N pi a^2 / A < 1e-3     -> N < 1e-3 A/(pi a^2)

    Since ``phi`` scales as ``N a^2`` and the mean free path as ``1/(N a)``,
    the lower and upper bounds scale differently with radius.
    """
    a = A_DEFAULT if a is None else float(a)
    A = AREA if area is None else float(area)
    W = LZ if area is None else np.sqrt(A * LZ / LX)
    n_collisional = A / (2.0 * a * W)
    n_onewaycoup = 1e-3 * A / (np.pi * a ** 2)
    # expected contact pairs ~ N^2 * (pi (2a)^2) / (2A) for a random field
    n_pairs = np.sqrt(2.0 * A * min_contact_pairs / (np.pi * (2.0 * a) ** 2))
    return {"radius": a, "area": A,
            "N_min_collisional": n_collisional,
            "N_min_pairs": n_pairs,
            "N_min": max(n_collisional, n_pairs),
            "N_max_oneway": n_onewaycoup,
            "feasible": max(n_collisional, n_pairs) < n_onewaycoup}


def max_feasible_radius(area=None):
    """
    Largest radius for which some ``N`` satisfies both bounds.

    The window closes because the two bounds scale differently:

        collisional floor   N > A/(2 a W)          ~ 1/a
        g(sigma) floor      N > sqrt(10 A/(pi a^2)) ~ 1/a
        one-way ceiling     N < 1e-3 A/(pi a^2)     ~ 1/a^2

    The upper bound decreases faster than either lower bound. Equating it to
    the ``g(sigma)`` sampling bound gives

        a_max = sqrt(A) / 5605

    which depends only on domain area and decreases as ``sqrt(A)`` when the
    domain is cropped.
    """
    A = AREA if area is None else float(area)
    W = LZ if area is None else np.sqrt(A * LZ / LX)
    return min(np.sqrt(A) / 5605.0, 2e-3 * W / np.pi)


def report_window(a=None, area=None):
    """Print the particle-count window for the active geometry."""
    w = particle_window(a, area)
    print(f"particle window at a = {w['radius']:.5e} "
          f"(r+ = {to_wall_units(w['radius']):.4f}), area = {w['area']:.4f}")
    print(f"  collisional (MFP < L_span)      N >~ {w['N_min_collisional']:>12,.0f}")
    print(f"  g(sigma) measurable             N >~ {w['N_min_pairs']:>12,.0f}")
    print(f"  one-way coupling (phi < 1e-3)   N <~ {w['N_max_oneway']:>12,.0f}")
    if not w["feasible"]:
        amax = max_feasible_radius(area)
        print(f"  !! NO VALID N: the floor exceeds the ceiling at this "
              f"radius.\n"
              f"     Largest radius that admits any N in this domain: "
              f"{amax:.4e}\n"
              f"     (= radius_frac {amax / DZ_MAX:.3f}). You are "
              f"{w['radius'] / amax:.0f}x above it.\n"
              f"     The limit scales as sqrt(area), so UNCROPPING helps more "
              f"than\n     shrinking the radius: a_max goes as sqrt(A).")
    return w


N_DEFAULT = int(np.sqrt(particle_window()["N_min"]
                        * particle_window()["N_max_oneway"])) \
    if particle_window()["feasible"] else int(particle_window()["N_min"])
# Compatibility default used by run_dns_study.py, computed from the bounds.


# ------------------------------------------------------- inertia parameter
#
# ``tau_p`` is the particle response time in file units. Wall-unit quantities
# do not enter the engine. Interpret the sweep relative to the active record's
# measured correlation time.
#
TAU_LIST = (0.0, 0.05, 0.25, 0.5, 1.0, 2.5)


def density_ratio(tau, a=None):
    """
    rho_p/rho_f giving this tau_p for a sphere of radius a, from Stokes drag
    tau_p = (rho_p/rho_f) d^2 / (18 nu). Reporting only.

    The ratio scales as ``1/a^2``.
    """
    a = A_DEFAULT if a is None else float(a)
    return float(tau) * 18.0 * NU / (2.0 * a) ** 2


# --------------------------------------------------- Stokes closure (a, tau, rho)
#
# Stokes drag relates the three parameters by
#
#     tau_p = (2/9) * (rho_p/rho_f) * a**2 / nu
#
# equivalently ``tau_p = (rho_p/rho_f) d**2 / (18 nu)`` with ``d = 2a``.
# Specifying any two parameters determines the third:
#
#   fixed radius, sweep tau_p  -> rho_p/rho_f moves as tau_p
#   fixed rho_p/rho_f, sweep a -> tau_p moves as a**2
#
# The fixed-density sweep is also constrained by ``a <= A_MAX``.
#
def stokes_closure(tau=None, a=None, rho_ratio=None, nu=None):
    """
    Solve tau_p = (2/9)(rho_p/rho_f) a^2 / nu for whichever is missing.

    Give exactly two of ``tau``, ``a``, and ``rho_ratio``. The result includes
    all three values, wall-unit labels, and geometric feasibility.

        stokes_closure(tau=10, a=A_DEFAULT)      -> the rho ratio needed
        stokes_closure(rho_ratio=1000, a=2e-4)   -> the tau it gives
        stokes_closure(tau=1, rho_ratio=1000)    -> the radius required

    ``tau = 0`` denotes a tracer: a point particle with no cross-section or
    collisions. That is the only self-consistent reading of the relation at
    tau = 0 for a finite density ratio.
    """
    nu = NU if nu is None else float(nu)
    given = [x is not None for x in (tau, a, rho_ratio)]
    if sum(given) != 2:
        raise ValueError(
            "give exactly two of tau, a, rho_ratio -- the third follows from "
            f"tau_p = (2/9) (rho_p/rho_f) a^2 / nu. Got "
            f"tau={tau!r}, a={a!r}, rho_ratio={rho_ratio!r}.")

    notes = []
    if tau is not None and float(tau) == 0.0:
        # A finite-density tracer with tau = 0 has zero radius and cross-section.
        if a not in (None, 0.0):
            notes.append(f"tau=0 forces a=0; the given a={a:.4e} is ignored.")
        return {"tau": 0.0, "a": 0.0, "rho_ratio": float("nan"), "nu": nu,
                "is_tracer": True, "notes": notes, "feasible": True,
                "st_plus": 0.0, "a_plus": 0.0}

    if rho_ratio is None:
        a = float(a)
        rho_ratio = 4.5 * float(tau) * nu / a ** 2
    elif tau is None:
        a = float(a)
        tau = (2.0 / 9.0) * float(rho_ratio) * a ** 2 / nu
    else:
        a = float(np.sqrt(4.5 * float(tau) * nu / float(rho_ratio)))

    tau, a, rho_ratio = float(tau), float(a), float(rho_ratio)

    feasible = True
    if a > A_MAX:
        feasible = False
        notes.append(
            f"a = {a:.4e} exceeds A_MAX = {A_MAX:.4e}: the particle centre "
            f"would sit outside the slab. Largest tau reachable at this "
            f"density ratio is {(2.0/9.0)*rho_ratio*A_MAX**2/nu:.4g}.")
    if rho_ratio > 2.5e4:
        notes.append(
            f"rho_p/rho_f = {rho_ratio:.4g} is above ~2.5e4, which no real "
            f"material reaches (the densest solids in air are ~2e4). The run "
            f"is still valid as a tau_p study; it just has no material.")
    return {"tau": tau, "a": a, "rho_ratio": rho_ratio, "nu": nu,
            "is_tracer": False, "notes": notes, "feasible": feasible,
            "st_plus": tau_to_st(tau), "a_plus": to_wall_units(a)}


def max_tau_at_ratio(rho_ratio, a_max=None, nu=None):
    """Largest ``tau_p`` at this density ratio subject to ``a <= A_MAX``."""
    nu = NU if nu is None else float(nu)
    a_max = A_MAX if a_max is None else float(a_max)
    return (2.0 / 9.0) * float(rho_ratio) * a_max ** 2 / nu


def min_ratio_for_tau(tau, a_max=None, nu=None):
    """Smallest density ratio that attains ``tau_p`` within the slab."""
    nu = NU if nu is None else float(nu)
    a_max = A_MAX if a_max is None else float(a_max)
    return 4.5 * float(tau) * nu / a_max ** 2


def particle_reynolds(slip, a=None):
    """Return ``Re_p = |u - v_p| d / nu`` for reporting."""
    a = A_DEFAULT if a is None else float(a)
    return float(slip) * 2.0 * a / NU


def tau_to_st(tau):
    """Convert response time to the reporting label ``St+ = tau_p/t_nu``."""
    return float(tau) / T_NU


def st_to_tau(st_plus):
    """Convert ``St+`` to response time using the active ``t_nu``."""
    return float(st_plus) * T_NU


# ------------------------------------------------------------------- field
def _check_wall_convention():
    """
    Check the wall-distance convention used by the sphere-on-wall model.

    For a bottom-wall slab, wall distance increases with channel ``y`` and
    ``WALL_NORMAL_SIGN`` is +1; for a top-wall slab it is -1. A mismatch
    affects wall-normal derivatives but not interpolated velocities.
    """
    expect = {"bottom": +1.0, "top": -1.0}.get(si._SPEC.wall)
    if expect is not None and si.WALL_NORMAL_SIGN != expect:
        print(f"  WARNING: spec says wall={si._SPEC.wall!r}, which implies "
              f"WALL_NORMAL_SIGN = {expect:+.0f},\n"
              f"           but continuity closes with "
              f"{si.WALL_NORMAL_SIGN:+.0f}. One of the two is wrong.\n"
              f"           Velocities are unaffected; every wall-normal "
              f"derivative is not.")
    if Y_NODES[0] != 0.0:
        raise ValueError(
            f"wall_distance[0] = {Y_NODES[0]:.3e}, not 0. The sphere-on-wall "
            "model needs the wall at y = 0, so that a sphere of radius a "
            "resting on it has its centre at y = a.")


_check_wall_convention()


def dns_slab_fluid(radius=None, plane=None, t_start=None, interp=None,
                   crop=None, h5path=None):
    """
    `fluid_velocity` callable for the engine.

        field(pos, t) -> (N, 2)
        pos[:, 0] streamwise in [0, Lx)
        pos[:, 1] SPANWISE   in [0, Lz)
        returns [:, 0] streamwise and [:, 1] spanwise velocity, stored units.

    Sampling height, in order of precedence:
      radius : sphere-on-wall model, centre height y = radius. Scalar
               (monodisperse) or (N,) array (polydisperse). radius <= A_MAX.
      plane  : sample at wall-normal node `plane`, decoupled from the radius.
               Use for the collisionless tracer control, so both legs are
               height-matched.
    """
    itp = (SlabInterpolator(h5path=h5path, crop=crop) if interp is None
           else interp)
    t_start = T_START if t_start is None else float(t_start)
    t_last = float(itp.t[-1])
    Lx_d, Lz_d = itp.L1, itp.L0        # follow the interpolator, cropped or not

    if radius is not None:
        y = np.asarray(radius, float)
        if np.any(y > A_MAX + 1e-12):
            raise ValueError(
                f"radius {np.max(y):.6g} exceeds the slab depth {A_MAX:.6g}. "
                "A sphere resting on the wall has its centre at y = a, which "
                "must lie inside the data.")
        if np.any(y <= 0):
            raise ValueError("radius must be positive")
    elif plane is not None:
        if plane == 0:
            raise ValueError("plane 0 is the no-slip surface; velocity is zero.")
        y = float(itp.y_nodes[plane])
        # Node sampling differs from centre sampling unless the chosen node is
        # at the particle centre. The model uses drag =
        # (u(x_centre, t) - v)/tau_p, with the centre at y = a for a sphere
        # resting on the wall.
        print(f"  NOTE: sampling at node {plane} (y = {y:.5e}), NOT at a "
              f"particle centre.\n"
              f"        This is height-matched to radius a = {y:.5e} only. "
              f"For any other\n"
              f"        radius the collisionless leg and the collisional leg "
              f"see different\n"
              f"        fluid. Prefer radius=... so the height follows the "
              f"particle.")
    else:
        raise ValueError("give either radius=... (sphere-on-wall) or plane=...")

    def field(pos, t):
        t_dns = t_start + float(t)
        if not (itp.t[0] - 1e-9 <= t_dns <= t_last + 1e-9):
            raise ValueError(
                f"engine t={t:.4f} -> file t={t_dns:.4f} is outside the record "
                f"[{itp.t[0]:.1f}, {t_last:.1f}]. Usable engine time is "
                f"0 <= t <= {t_last - t_start:.1f}.")
        pos = np.asarray(pos, float)
        xs = np.mod(pos[:, 0], Lx_d)        # streamwise
        zs = np.mod(pos[:, 1], Lz_d)        # SPANWISE (engine calls this y)
        yy = y if np.ndim(y) == 0 else y
        f = itp(xs, zs, yy, t_dns)          # (stream, span, wall-normal)
        out = np.empty_like(pos)
        out[:, 0] = f.stream                # streamwise -> engine axis 0
        out[:, 1] = f.span                  # spanwise   -> engine axis 1
        return out

    def omega(pos, t):
        """
        Fluid angular velocity ``omega_f = 1/2 curl U_f`` at particle centres,
        with shape ``(N, 3)`` in pipeline order: about the
        streamwise axis, about the spanwise axis, about the wall-normal axis.
        It uses the same sampling height, time mapping, and cubic interpolant
        as ``field`` and can be passed to the engine as ``fluid_omega``.
        """
        t_dns = t_start + float(t)
        if not (itp.t[0] - 1e-9 <= t_dns <= t_last + 1e-9):
            raise ValueError(
                f"engine t={t:.4f} -> file t={t_dns:.4f} is outside the record "
                f"[{itp.t[0]:.1f}, {t_last:.1f}].")
        pos = np.asarray(pos, float)
        xs = np.mod(pos[:, 0], Lx_d)
        zs = np.mod(pos[:, 1], Lz_d)
        om = itp.omega(xs, zs, y, t_dns)
        out = np.empty((len(pos), 3))
        out[:, 0] = om.stream
        out[:, 1] = om.span
        out[:, 2] = om.wall_normal
        return out

    field.y = y                             # sampling height (was field.z)
    field.crop = crop
    field.domain = {"L": Lx_d, "W": Lz_d}
    field.t_max_engine = t_last - t_start
    field.interp = itp
    field.omega = omega
    return field


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ helpers
def seed_particles(N, radius, rng=None, max_tries=50, L=None, W=None):
    """
    Seed random positions and redraw particles involved in overlapping pairs.

    L, W : domain. Defaults to the full slab; pass the cropped extents when
           using ``SlabInterpolator(crop=...)``.
    """
    from scipy.spatial import cKDTree
    L = LX if L is None else float(L)
    W = LZ if W is None else float(W)
    rng = np.random.default_rng() if rng is None else rng
    a = (np.full(N, float(radius)) if np.isscalar(radius)
         else np.asarray(radius, float))
    pos = np.column_stack([rng.uniform(0, L, N), rng.uniform(0, W, N)])
    for _ in range(max_tries):
        tree = cKDTree(np.mod(pos, [L, W]), boxsize=[L, W])
        bad = {max(i, j) for i, j in tree.query_pairs(2.0 * a.max())}
        if not bad:
            break
        idx = np.fromiter(bad, int)
        pos[idx] = np.column_stack([rng.uniform(0, L, idx.size),
                                    rng.uniform(0, W, idx.size)])
    return pos, np.zeros((N, 2))


def mean_free_path(N, radius, area=None):
    """2D mean free path A/(N*2r), in h units. `area` for a cropped domain."""
    A = AREA if area is None else float(area)
    return A / (N * 2.0 * float(np.mean(radius)))


def report_setup(N, radius, tau, T_final, wall_units=False, L=None, W=None):
    """Print the configuration in the file's non-dimensional units."""
    a = float(np.mean(radius))
    Ld = LX if L is None else float(L)
    Wd = LZ if W is None else float(W)
    A_ = Ld * Wd
    mfp = A_ / (N * 2.0 * a)
    phi = N * np.pi * a ** 2 / A_
    print("DNS slab run   (non-dimensional: h = 1, velocities as stored)")
    print(f"  domain        : Lx={Ld:.4f} x Lz={Wd:.4f} periodic,"
          f" Ly={LY:.1f} (channel)"
          + ("" if L is None else f"   [cropped, {A_/(LX*LZ):.3f} of full]"))
    print(f"  grid          : {N_SPAN}(span) x {N_STREAM}(stream) x "
          f"{len(Y_NODES)}(planes)   hx={HX:.6f} hz={HZ:.6f}")
    print(f"  slab          : depth {A_MAX:.4e} = y+ {to_wall_units(A_MAX):.2f}"
          f"   ({len(Y_NODES)} nodes)")
    print(f"  particle      : N={N}, a={a:.5e}, centre at y=a"
          f"   (a/Lz={a/LZ:.2e})")
    print(f"  a / slab depth: {a/A_MAX:.3f}"
          + ("   -> centre OUTSIDE the data" if a > A_MAX else ""))
    print(f"  phi           : {phi:.3e}"
          + ("   (one-way coupling justified)" if phi < 1e-3 else
             "   -> two-way coupling may matter"))
    print(f"  mean free path: {mfp:.4f} = {mfp/Wd:.3f} Lz"
          + ("   collisionless" if mfp > Wd else "   collisional"))
    print(f"  inertia       : tau_p={tau:.4f}"
          + ("   (tracer)" if tau <= 0 else
             f"   record/tau_p={T_final/tau:.0f}"
             f"   rho_p/rho_f={density_ratio(tau, a):.0f}"))
    print(f"  time          : T={T_final:.1f} of {T_MAX:.1f} available"
          + ("   -> EXCEEDS RECORD" if T_final > T_MAX else ""))
    if wall_units:
        print(f"  [wall labels] : Re_tau={RE_TAU:.0f}  u_tau={U_TAU:.6f}"
              f"  r+={to_wall_units(a):.4f}  St+={tau_to_st(tau):.2f}")
    return {"mfp": mfp, "phi": phi, "tau": float(tau)}


if __name__ == "__main__":
    si.describe()
    print()
    print(f"record        : t = {T_START:.3f} .. {T_END:.3f}, "
          f"usable T_MAX = {T_MAX:.3f}, dt_snap = {DT_SNAP:.4f}")
    print(f"A_MAX         : {A_MAX:.6e}   (y+ = {to_wall_units(A_MAX):.3f})")
    print(f"DZ_MAX        : {DZ_MAX:.6e}")
    print(f"A_DEFAULT     : {A_DEFAULT:.6e}   (r+ = "
          f"{to_wall_units(A_DEFAULT):.4f})")
    print()
    report_window()

03

Study workflow

Configuration, parameter resolution and one-case execution.

run_study.pyMain command-line configuration and study workflowSee code ↗

One resolver translates a model label into the contact parameters used downstream.

"""
Command-line entry point for the DNS-slab particle study.

The main physical parameters and output-frame counts can be set from the
command line or through the interactive prompts.

    python run_study.py                      # interactive: prompts for each
    python run_study.py --defaults           # accept all defaults, no prompts
    python run_study.py --tau 0.5 --radius-frac 0.5 --n-snap 400
    python run_study.py --show               # print the plan (counts + physics) and exit
    python run_study.py --tau 0.5 --dry-run  # validate everything, run nothing

Physical parameters

    tau          Particle response time in the file's time units. This is the
                 inertia parameter; the study is non-dimensional and no
                 wall unit enters the run. tau = 0 is a tracer (v = u exactly).

    radius_frac  Particle radius as a fraction of the largest wall-normal
                 step, a = radius_frac * DZ_MAX. The radius sets both the
                 sampling height (sphere centre at z = a) and the collision
                 cross-section (sigma = 2a).
                 radius_frac = 0.5 puts the centre at 37.5% of the slab depth,
                 between nodes 1 and 2, so the quadratic wall-normal
                 interpolation is genuinely active (at radius_frac = 1.333 the
                 centre lands exactly on node 2 and no z-interpolation happens).
                 Geometric ceiling: radius_frac <= A_MAX / DZ_MAX = 1.333.

    N            Particle count. Once the radius is set, halving the
                 radius quarters the contact-pair count and doubles the mean
                 free path, so the lower bound on N changes with radius_frac.
                 See physics_window().

Frame counts

    n_snap        Particle frames written to .npz. A value of 400 roughly
                  matches the DNS snapshot spacing (~0.22); 50 gives a visibly
                  coarse animation.
    diag_frames   frames the summary statistics average over. The error bar is
                  the frame-to-frame spread; frames are correlated, so it
                  saturates -- more frames tighten the MEAN, not the spread.
    conv_frames   Points on the convergence curve.
    streak_snaps  DNS snapshots averaged for the two-point correlation.
                  Converged by ~60 on the previous dataset; re-check here.
    anim_frames   frames per animation. Particle animations are capped at
                  n_snap; flow animations cap at the record length.

    time_avg      Rolling window for particle density only. The flow is not
                  averaged because it has no shot noise and averaging would
                  cancel real structure.
"""

from __future__ import annotations
import os
import sys
import argparse
import inspect

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- bootstrap
# Apply dataset options before importing dns_fluid because slab_interp fixes
# the geometry when it is imported. Reading those options here keeps this
# command line consistent with dataset.py.
import dataset as ds

DATASET_KEYS = tuple(ds.DEFAULTS)


def _bootstrap_dataset(argv=None):
    argv = (sys.argv[1:] if argv is None else argv)
    over = {}
    for i, a in enumerate(argv):
        if not a.startswith("--"):
            continue
        k = a[2:].replace("-", "_")
        if k in DATASET_KEYS and i + 1 < len(argv):
            v = argv[i + 1]
            if v != "auto":
                if k in ds._INT_KEYS:
                    v = int(v)
                elif k in ds._FLOAT_KEYS:
                    v = float(v)
            over[k] = v
    if over:
        print(f"dataset overrides: {over}")
    ds.SPEC = ds.build_spec(ds.load_config(over), verbose=bool(over))
    return ds.SPEC


_bootstrap_dataset()

# Geometry and radius/count relationships do not require the engine.
from dns_fluid import (A_MAX, DZ_MAX, DOMAIN, AREA, T_MAX, NU, DT_SNAP,
                       density_ratio, cropped_domain, particle_window)
from slab_interp import Y_NODES, N_SPAN, N_STREAM, N_PLANES
import slab_interp as si

N_SNAPSHOTS = int(round(T_MAX / DT_SNAP)) + 1     # DNS frames in the record

# Flow correlation time used to form the frame-independent ratio
# tau_p / TAU_FLOW. The default was measured on Slab_complete.h5 and should be
# remeasured for another dataset with measure_tau_flow() or set explicitly.
TAU_FLOW_DEFAULT = 0.8


def _plane_series(spec, plane, n, comp=None):
    """u(t, span, stream) at one plane, read as a single hyperslab."""
    import h5py
    comp = spec.comp_stream if comp is None else comp
    sl = [slice(None)] * 4
    sl[0] = slice(0, n)
    sl[spec.axis_normal] = plane
    with h5py.File(spec.path, "r") as f:
        a = np.asarray(f[comp][tuple(sl)], float)
    # remaining axes keep their relative order; put span first, stream second
    ax_span = spec.axis_span - (1 if spec.axis_span > spec.axis_normal else 0)
    ax_str = spec.axis_stream - (1 if spec.axis_stream > spec.axis_normal else 0)
    return np.transpose(a, (0, ax_span, ax_str))


def measure_tau_flow(plane=None, n=400, spec=None, frame="comoving",
                     n_sample=64, verbose=True):
    """
    Eulerian integral time of u' at FIXED POINTS, averaged over points.

    frame :
      "fixed"    autocorrelation at a point in the lab frame.
      "comoving" the same, but following the mean flow: the field is shifted
                 streamwise by U_c * lag before correlating.

    Choice of reference frame

        A fixed-point correlation in a shear flow is dominated by sweeping,
        not by the lifetime of anything. Eddies are advected past the probe
        at U_c, so the signal decorrelates after roughly (eddy size)/U_c
        however long the eddies actually live. Near the wall U_c is large and
        the structures are long, so the fixed-point time can be an order of
        magnitude short.

        tau_p/tau_flow is meant to say how the particle's response time
        compares with the time the flow it sits in stays coherent. That is
        the eddy lifetime, not the sweeping time -- so the sweeping must be
        removed first. Both are returned so the gap is visible.

        The Lagrangian correlation time along particle paths cannot be
        obtained from the field alone; the
        comoving Eulerian time is the standard proxy for it.
    """
    spec = ds.SPEC if spec is None else spec
    g = spec.read_geometry()
    plane = (g["n_planes"] - 1) if plane is None else plane
    n = int(min(n, g["n_t"]))
    dt, hx = g["dt"], g["hx"]

    U = _plane_series(spec, plane, n)                 # (n, span, stream)
    U_c = float(U.mean())                             # convection velocity
    up = U - U.mean(axis=0, keepdims=True)            # fluctuation per point
    # Subsample only in the spanwise direction. The streamwise axis must stay at full
    # resolution: the comoving shift below is a np.roll in cells, so on a
    # subsampled axis it would translate by (stride x shift) cells and the
    # correlation would be taken between unrelated points.
    si_ = max(1, up.shape[1] // max(1, int(n_sample)))
    sub = up[:, ::si_, :]
    var = float((sub * sub).mean())
    if var <= 0 or n < 8:
        return np.nan

    n_lag = n // 2
    rho_fix = np.empty(n_lag)
    rho_com = np.empty(n_lag)
    for m in range(n_lag):
        a = sub[:n - m]
        b = sub[m:]
        rho_fix[m] = float((a * b).mean()) / var
        shift = int(round(U_c * m * dt / hx))
        rho_com[m] = float((a * np.roll(b, -shift, axis=2)).mean()) / var

    def integral(rho):
        z = np.where(rho < 0)[0]
        cut = int(z[0]) if len(z) else len(rho)
        return float(rho[:cut].sum() * dt)

    t_fix, t_com = integral(rho_fix), integral(rho_com)
    if verbose:
        print(f"  tau_flow  fixed-frame {t_fix:.4f}   comoving {t_com:.4f}"
              f"   (U_c = {U_c:.4f}, {n} snapshots, plane {plane})")
        if t_com > 3 * t_fix:
            print(f"    sweeping dominates the fixed-frame value by "
                  f"{t_com/t_fix:.1f}x -- use the comoving one")
    return t_com if frame == "comoving" else t_fix


# knobs that accept the string "auto" as well as a number
_AUTO_KEYS = ("tau_flow",)

RADIUS_CEILING = A_MAX / DZ_MAX          # a must stay <= A_MAX


def _default_N():
    """
    A starting N inside the admissible window, if one exists.

    Geometric mean of floor and ceiling, so it sits as far from both bounds
    as the window allows. If the floor exceeds the ceiling there is no valid
    N at this radius -- return the floor and let validate() say so rather
    than quietly picking something that violates one of the two.
    """
    w = particle_window()
    lo, hi = w["N_min"], w["N_max_oneway"]
    return int(np.sqrt(lo * hi)) if hi > lo else int(lo)


# ----------------------------------------------------------------- logging
# Mirror the console output to a text file so each run retains its parameters,
# diagnostics, warnings, prompts, and any traceback. Tee normally comes from
# modified_engine; the local fallback covers early import failures.
try:
    from modified_engine import Tee
except Exception:                                  # engine not importable yet
    class Tee:
        """Send printed output to both the console and a text file."""
        def __init__(self, *streams):
            self.streams = streams

        def write(self, data):
            for st_ in self.streams:
                st_.write(data)
                st_.flush()

        def flush(self):
            for st_ in self.streams:
                st_.flush()


LOG_OUTPUT = True          # set False to print to the console only


@contextlib.contextmanager
def tee_output(path):
    """
    Mirror stdout AND stderr into `path` for the duration of the block.

    stderr is included so tracebacks are recorded. The streams are restored
    in a finally block so an exception cannot leave the console redirected.
    """
    if not LOG_OUTPUT:
        yield None
        return
    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(path)) or ".", exist_ok=True)
    f = open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8", buffering=1)
    out, err = sys.stdout, sys.stderr
    sys.stdout = Tee(out, f)
    sys.stderr = Tee(err, f)
    f.write(f"# run_study.py log\n# started {datetime.datetime.now():%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S}\n")
    f.write(f"# working directory {os.getcwd()}\n\n")
    try:
        yield f
    except BaseException:
        # capture the traceback IN the log before the streams go back
        traceback.print_exc()
        raise
    finally:
        f.write(f"\n# finished {datetime.datetime.now():%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S}\n")
        sys.stdout, sys.stderr = out, err
        f.close()

# knobs that must be positive integers, and the physical ones
def _n_saved(cfg):
    """
    Frames that will actually land in the .npz.

    n_snap = -1 means "every frame the engine kept", which is only known once
    T/dt is resolved. Everything downstream -- the plan table, the frame
    warnings, the animation cap -- must use THIS, never the raw -1, or it
    silently propagates a negative frame count.
    """
    n_engine = int(np.ceil(float(cfg["T"]) / float(cfg["dt"]))) + 1
    n = int(cfg.get("n_snap", 50))
    if n < 0:
        return n_engine
    return min(n, n_engine)


def _record_target(cfg):
    """cfg int -> the driver's record_target. 0 -> None (follow n_snap),
    -1 -> None with n_snap=None handled downstream; any positive value passes
    straight through."""
    rt = int(cfg.get("record_target", 0))
    if rt == 0:
        return None                      # run_dns_study derives it from n_snap
    if rt < 0:
        return int(cfg["T"] / cfg["dt"]) + 1   # every engine step
    return rt


_INT_KEYS = ("N", "n_snap", "record_target", "progress_every", "stream",
             "diag_frames", "conv_frames",
             "streak_snaps", "view_snap",
             "crop_stream", "crop_span", "model_study",
             "anim_frames", "time_avg", "series_panels", "fps", "seed",
             "momentum_plots", "plot_particles", "pz_diag")

# Parameters used by each model, in prompt order.
MODEL_PARAMS = {"A": (), "B": ("spin_init",), "C": ("spin_init", "e_tan"),
                "D": ("spin_init", "e_tan", "mu_pp"),
                "E": ("spin_init", "e_tan", "mu_pp", "mu_w", "g_eff")}


def model_of(cfg):
    """
    (model letter, effective e_tan, effective mu_pp) from cfg["model"].
    e_tan is 1 (smooth) unless the model is C, D or E; mu_pp is -1 (no cap)
    unless the model is D or E. An unknown letter is reported as itself so
    validate() can name it.
    """
    m = str(cfg.get("model", "A")).strip().upper()
    e_tan = float(cfg.get("e_tan", 0.0)) if m in ("C", "D", "E") else 1.0
    mu = float(cfg.get("mu_pp", 0.3)) if m in ("D", "E") else -1.0
    return m, e_tan, mu


def switches_of(cfg):
    """(rotation, tan_res, p_friction, wall_friction) for cfg["model"]."""
    m = model_of(cfg)[0]
    return (m in ("B", "C", "D", "E"), m in ("C", "D", "E"), m in ("D", "E"),
            m == "E")


# --------------------------------------------------------- physics window
def resolve_closure(cfg, verbose=False):
    """
    Fix the (a, tau_p, rho_p/rho_f) triangle before anything else reads it.

        tau_p = (2/9) (rho_p/rho_f) a^2 / nu

    ONE relation, three quantities: give any TWO and the third follows. There
    is no configuration in which all three are free, so the config exposes two
    sentinels and this function fills in whatever was left blank:

      MODE A   rho_ratio = 0            radius_frac and tau are yours;
               (the default)            rho_p/rho_f is REPORTED
      MODE B   rho_ratio > 0, tau >= 0  a is DERIVED; radius_frac ignored
      MODE C   rho_ratio > 0, tau < 0   tau is DERIVED from radius_frac
                                        and rho_ratio

    Mode C is the "one material, vary the size" sweep: pin rho_p/rho_f, step
    radius_frac, and let tau_p go as a^2. Mode B is "one material, vary the
    response time" and moves the radius instead -- which runs into a <= A_MAX
    quickly, since a goes as sqrt(tau).

    Writes the resolved numbers back into cfg, so every later reader sees two
    concrete values and never has to know which mode was used. Idempotent:
    calling it again on a resolved cfg changes nothing.
    """
    rr = float(cfg.get("rho_ratio", 0) or 0.0)
    tau = float(cfg.get("tau", 0.0))
    rf = float(cfg.get("radius_frac", 0.0))

    # Resolve and retain the input mode before writing derived values to cfg;
    # this preserves which pair of closure variables the user supplied.
    prior = cfg.get("_mode")

    if prior == "C" or (prior is None and rr > 0.0 and tau < 0.0):   # MODE C
        mode = "C"
        a = rf * DZ_MAX
        tau = (2.0 / 9.0) * rr * a ** 2 / NU
        cfg["tau"] = tau
        cfg["rho_ratio_derived"] = rr
    elif rr <= 0.0:                                 # MODE A
        if tau < 0.0:
            raise SystemExit(
                "tau < 0 means 'derive tau', but that needs rho_ratio > 0. "
                "Either set rho_ratio, or give a real tau.")
        mode = "A"
        a = rf * DZ_MAX
        cfg["rho_ratio_derived"] = (4.5 * tau * NU / a ** 2 if a > 0
                                    else float("nan"))
    else:                                           # MODE B
        mode = "B"
        a = 0.0 if tau == 0.0 else float(np.sqrt(4.5 * NU * tau / rr))
        cfg["radius_frac"] = a / DZ_MAX
        cfg["rho_ratio_derived"] = rr

    # For a tracer, tau = 0 forces a = 0: a point
    # particle with no cross-section, which cannot collide in either leg.
    if float(cfg["tau"]) == 0.0:
        a = 0.0
        cfg["radius_frac"] = 0.0
        # rho_p/rho_f is undefined for a point particle: the relation gives
        # 0/0. Reporting 0 would read as "same density as the fluid", which
        # is a specific and wrong claim.
        cfg["rho_ratio_derived"] = float("nan")

    cfg["_a"] = a
    cfg["_mode"] = mode
    if verbose:
        print(f"  closure mode {mode}: a = {a:.6e} "
              f"(radius_frac {cfg['radius_frac']:.4g}), "
              f"tau = {float(cfg['tau']):.6g}, "
              f"rho_p/rho_f = {cfg['rho_ratio_derived']:.6g}")
    return cfg


def radius_of(cfg):
    """The radius this case runs at, after the closure is resolved."""
    return float(resolve_closure(cfg)["_a"])


def rho_ratio_of(cfg):
    """rho_p/rho_f for this case -- given or derived, whichever applies."""
    return float(resolve_closure(cfg)["rho_ratio_derived"])


def crop_of(cfg):
    """
    Return (n_stream, n_span), or None for the full domain.

    The wall-parallel directions have different point counts, so cropping is
    specified separately for each direction.
    """
    ns = int(cfg.get("crop_stream", 0)) or N_STREAM
    nz = int(cfg.get("crop_span", 0)) or N_SPAN
    if ns >= N_STREAM and nz >= N_SPAN:
        return None
    return (ns, nz)


def domain_of(cfg):
    """(L, W, AREA) for the chosen crop."""
    crop = crop_of(cfg)
    if crop is None:
        return DOMAIN["L"], DOMAIN["W"], AREA
    d = cropped_domain(crop)
    return d["L"], d["W"], d["AREA"]


def physics_window(cfg):
    """
    The N-window implied by the radius and crop, all in non-dimensional units.
    Returns floors/ceilings and the current standing of this N.
    """
    a = radius_of(cfg)
    L, W, A = domain_of(cfg)
    sigma = 2.0 * a
    N = int(cfg["N"])

    # collisional: mean free path A/(N sigma) < Lz
    N_collisional = A / (sigma * W)
    # g(sigma) measurable: want >= ~20 contact pairs.
    #   contact pairs ~ 0.5 * N * (N/A) * pi * sigma^2   (dilute estimate)
    N_gsigma = np.sqrt(20.0 * A / (0.5 * np.pi * sigma ** 2))
    # one-way coupling: phi = N pi a^2 / A < 1e-3
    N_oneway = 1e-3 * A / (np.pi * a ** 2)

    mfp = A / (N * sigma)
    phi = N * np.pi * a ** 2 / A
    pairs = 0.5 * N * (N / A) * np.pi * sigma ** 2

    floor = max(N_collisional, N_gsigma)     # binding lower bound
    return {
        "a": a, "sigma": sigma, "L": L, "W": W, "A": A,
        "N_collisional": N_collisional, "N_gsigma": N_gsigma,
        "N_oneway": N_oneway, "floor": floor, "ceiling": N_oneway,
        "mfp": mfp, "mfp_over_Lz": mfp / W, "phi": phi, "pairs": pairs,
        "below_floor": N < floor, "above_ceiling": N > N_oneway,
    }


def validate(cfg):
    resolve_closure(cfg)
    """Hard errors and soft warnings. Returns (errors, warnings)."""
    errs, warns = [], []
    n_engine = int(cfg["T"] / cfg["dt"])

    if cfg["T"] > T_MAX + 1e-9:
        errs.append(f"T = {cfg['T']} exceeds the record ({T_MAX}).")
    if not (0.0 <= cfg["radius_frac"] <= RADIUS_CEILING + 1e-9):
        errs.append(f"radius_frac = {cfg['radius_frac']} outside "
                    f"(0, {RADIUS_CEILING:.3f}]; the centre must lie inside "
                    f"the data (a <= A_MAX).")
    if float(cfg["tau"]) < 0 and float(cfg.get("rho_ratio", 0) or 0) <= 0:
        errs.append(f"tau = {cfg['tau']} < 0 means 'derive tau', which needs "
                    f"rho_ratio > 0.")
    # Options with sentinel values need their own checks. In particular,
    # record_target = 0 follows n_snap, n_snap = -1 keeps every engine frame,
    # and anim_frames = 0 skips animations.
    _EXEMPT = ("seed", "crop_stream", "crop_span", "model_study",
               "anim_frames", "view_snap",   # view_snap: -1 = last snapshot
               "stream",                     # 0 = histories in RAM
               "momentum_plots", "pz_diag")
    for k in _INT_KEYS:
        v = int(cfg[k])
        if k == "record_target":
            if v < -1:
                errs.append(f"record_target = {v}: use 0 to follow n_snap, "
                            f"-1 for every engine step, or a positive count.")
        elif k == "n_snap":
            if v == 0 or v < -1:
                errs.append(f"n_snap = {v}: use -1 to save every frame the "
                            f"engine kept, or a positive count.")
        elif v <= 0 and k not in _EXEMPT:
            errs.append(f"{k} = {cfg[k]} must be positive.")
    if not (0.0 <= float(cfg.get("e_ball", 1.0)) <= 1.0):
        errs.append(f"e_ball = {cfg['e_ball']} outside [0, 1].")
    elif float(cfg.get("e_ball", 1.0)) < 0.7 and \
            str(cfg.get("collisions", "on")).lower() != "off":
        warns.append(f"e_ball = {cfg['e_ball']}: strong dissipation. The "
                     "dense tracer leg is collision-dominated (tau_c ~ 3), "
                     "so inelastic collapse in filaments is a real risk -- "
                     "watch the engine's collision-cap warnings.")
    if cfg["vel_init"] not in ("fluid", "random", "zero"):
        errs.append(f"vel_init = {cfg['vel_init']!r} not in fluid|random|zero.")
    _model, _et, _mu = model_of(cfg)
    if _model not in MODEL_PARAMS:
        errs.append(f"model = {cfg.get('model')!r} not in A|B|C|D|E.")
    if _model in ("C", "D", "E") and not (-1.0 <= float(cfg.get("e_tan", 0.0)) <= 1.0):
        errs.append(f"e_tan = {cfg.get('e_tan')} outside [-1, 1].")
    if _model in ("D", "E") and float(cfg.get("mu_pp", 0.3)) < 0.0:
        errs.append(f"mu_pp = {cfg.get('mu_pp')} must be >= 0.")
    if float(cfg.get("pz_threshold", 0.0)) < 0.0:
        errs.append("pz_threshold must be >= 0 (0 = record only).")
    if _model == "E":
        if float(cfg.get("mu_w", 0.3)) < 0.0 or float(cfg.get("g_eff", 1.0)) < 0.0:
            errs.append("mu_w and g_eff must be >= 0.")
        elif float(cfg.get("mu_w", 0.3)) * float(cfg.get("g_eff", 1.0)) == 0.0:
            warns.append("model E with mu_w * g_eff = 0: the wall exerts no "
                         "force; this run is physically model D.")
        else:
            # the slip removable per substep vs. a typical fluid slip: if the
            # friction can kill a whole tau_p worth of slip in one step the
            # rolling/sliding switch is not resolved by dt
            _cap = 3.5 * float(cfg["mu_w"]) * float(cfg["g_eff"]) * float(cfg["dt"])
            warns.append(f"model E: slip removable per substep (7/2) mu_w "
                         f"g_eff dt = {_cap:.3e} in file velocity units; the "
                         f"sliding->rolling switch is resolved only if typical "
                         f"contact slips exceed this.")
    if _model in ("C", "D", "E") and _et == 1.0:
        warns.append(f"model {_model} with e_tan = 1 is a smooth contact: the "
                     f"tangential impulse is identically zero and this run is "
                     f"physically model B. Set e_tan < 1.")
    if str(cfg.get("spin_init", "fluid")) not in ("fluid", "zero"):
        errs.append(f"spin_init = {cfg.get('spin_init')!r} not in fluid|zero.")
    if _model != "A" and float(cfg["tau"]) == 0.0:
        warns.append(f"model {_model} with tau = 0: a tracer is a point "
                     f"particle with no spin; this run executes as model A.")

    w = physics_window(cfg)
    dense_ok = bool(int(cfg.get("model_study", 0)))
    if dense_ok and (w["below_floor"] or w["above_ceiling"]):
        warns.append(
            f"MODEL STUDY: phi = {w['phi']:.4g} is {w['phi']/1e-3:.0f}x the "
            f"one-way-coupling limit. Two-way momentum feedback is NOT "
            f"modelled. This is a deliberate choice and must be stated as one.")
    if w["phi"] >= 0.5:
        errs.append(
            f"phi = {w['phi']:.3g} at radius_frac = {cfg['radius_frac']}, "
            f"N = {cfg['N']}. Above ~0.5 the disks cannot be placed without "
            f"overlapping (phi > 1 is geometrically impossible), so this is "
            f"not a dilute suspension at all -- it is a jammed one, and every "
            f"assumption behind one-way coupling and Stokes drag is gone. "
            f"Reduce the radius or N, or uncrop.")
    if str(cfg.get("collisions", "on")).lower() in ("off", "both"):
        # The collision FLOOR does not apply -- nothing collides. The phi
        # CEILING still does: the radius is kept, so this leg occupies the
        # same physical area fraction as its collisional partner.
        if w["above_ceiling"] and not dense_ok:
            warns.append(
                f"collisionless leg at phi = {w['phi']:.1e}: above the "
                f"one-way ceiling. The radius is kept for seeding and drag, "
                f"so this leg is as dense as its partner even though nothing "
                f"collides.")
    elif w["below_floor"] and not dense_ok:
        errs.append(
            f"N = {cfg['N']} is below the floor {w['floor']:.0f} at "
            f"radius_frac = {cfg['radius_frac']}: "
            + ("collisionless (MFP = %.2f Lz)" % w["mfp_over_Lz"]
               if cfg["N"] < w["N_collisional"] else
               "g(sigma) unmeasurable (~%.0f contact pairs)" % w["pairs"])
            + ". Raise N or the radius.")
    # With a mean free path far beyond the domain, the two requested legs are
    # likely to follow the same trajectory because no contacts occur.
    if (str(cfg.get("collisions", "on")).lower() == "both"
            and w["mfp_over_Lz"] > 10.0):
        warns.append(
            f"collisions='both' but MFP = {w['mfp_over_Lz']:.0f} Lz: "
            f"essentially no contacts will occur, so the two legs will be "
            f"IDENTICAL trajectories and the pair adds nothing. For a "
            f"near-tracer anchor run collisions='on' alone (it doubles as "
            f"its own control).")
    if (str(cfg.get("collisions", "on")).lower() != "off") and w["above_ceiling"]:
        warns.append(f"N = {cfg['N']} exceeds the one-way-coupling ceiling "
                     f"{w['N_oneway']:.0f} (phi = {w['phi']:.1e} > 1e-3): "
                     f"two-way momentum feedback may matter.")

    # Frame-count checks; values are capped at run time where appropriate.
    _rt = int(cfg.get("record_target", 0))
    # --- Stokes closure: tau, a, and rho_p/rho_f form one relation --------
    _a = radius_of(cfg)
    _tau = float(cfg["tau"])
    _rr = rho_ratio_of(cfg)
    _mode = cfg.get("_mode", "A")
    if _tau == 0.0:
        warns.append("tau = 0 is a TRACER: radius forced to 0, no collisions "
                     "possible in either leg.")
    elif _mode in ("B", "C"):
        if _a > A_MAX:
            errs.append(
                f"rho_ratio = {_rr:g} with tau = {_tau:g} needs a = {_a:.4e}, "
                f"above A_MAX = {A_MAX:.4e} -- the centre would leave the "
                f"slab. Max tau at this ratio is "
                f"{(2.0/9.0) * _rr * A_MAX**2 / NU:.4g}; or raise rho_ratio "
                f"to {4.5 * NU * _tau / A_MAX**2:.4g}.")
        else:
            warns.append(
                f"rho_ratio pinned at {_rr:g} (mode {_mode}): "
                + (f"radius DERIVED as {_a:.4e} "
                   f"(radius_frac {_a / DZ_MAX:.4g}); radius_frac ignored."
                   if _mode == "B" else
                   f"tau DERIVED as {_tau:.6g} from radius_frac "
                   f"{_a / DZ_MAX:.4g}."))
    else:
        _rho = _rr
        if _rho > 2.5e4:
            warns.append(
                f"rho_p/rho_f = {_rho:.4g} is required by tau = {_tau:g} at "
                f"a = {_a:.4e}. No real material reaches that (densest solids "
                f"in air are ~2e4). Valid as a tau_p study, but do not "
                f"describe it as a material.")

    # Storage depends on `stream`: histories either accumulate as float64
    # lists in memory or are written as float32 memmaps, one frame at a time.
    _ns = _n_saved(cfg)
    _streaming = bool(int(cfg.get("stream", 0)))
    if _streaming:
        _gb_disk = 2 * _ns * int(cfg["N"]) * 2 * 4 / 1e9
        if _gb_disk > 2.0:
            warns.append(f"stream=1: {_ns} frames x N={cfg['N']} = "
                         f"{_gb_disk:.1f} GB written to DISK per leg "
                         f"(float32 memmaps under the case folder; RAM stays "
                         f"~1 frame). 'both' writes two legs -- check free "
                         f"space, and delete the _stream_tau* folders once "
                         f"the .npz is saved.")
    else:
        _gb = 2 * _ns * int(cfg["N"]) * 2 * 8 / 1e9
        if _gb > 2.0:
            warns.append(f"{_ns} frames x N={cfg['N']} = {_gb:.1f} GB per "
                         f"leg held in RAM until the run ends (positions + "
                         f"velocities). 'both' runs two legs sequentially, "
                         f"so the peak is one leg. stream=1 drops this to "
                         f"~1 frame of RAM by writing frames to disk.")
    if int(cfg["n_snap"]) > n_engine:
        warns.append(f"n_snap ({cfg['n_snap']}) > engine steps ({n_engine}); "
                     f"capped at {n_engine}.")
    if _rt > 0 and _rt < _ns:
        warns.append(f"record_target ({_rt}) < n_snap ({_ns}): the "
                     f"engine discards frames before they can be saved. "
                     f"Use 0 to follow n_snap.")
    if cfg["anim_frames"] > _ns:
        warns.append(f"anim_frames ({cfg['anim_frames']}) > saved frames "
                     f"({_ns}); particle animations capped at {_ns}.")
    if cfg["streak_snaps"] > N_SNAPSHOTS:
        warns.append(f"streak_snaps ({cfg['streak_snaps']}) > {N_SNAPSHOTS} "
                     f"DNS snapshots; capped at {N_SNAPSHOTS}.")
    # diagnose_tail uses the engine's position history, so diag_frames is
    # bounded by T/dt rather than by the subsampled archive length.
    _n_engine = int(cfg["T"] / cfg["dt"])
    if cfg["diag_frames"] > _n_engine:
        warns.append(f"diag_frames ({cfg['diag_frames']}) > engine frames "
                     f"({_n_engine}); capped at {_n_engine}.")
    if cfg["dt"] > 0.4 * cfg["tau"] and cfg["tau"] > 0:
        warns.append(f"dt = {cfg['dt']} > 0.4*tau_p ({0.4*cfg['tau']:.3f}): "
                     f"integration under-resolved for this tau_p. "
                     f"Use dt <= {0.4*cfg['tau']:.3f}.")
    return errs, warns


# --------------------------------------------------------------- reporting
def tau_flow_of(cfg):
    """Resolve tau_flow, measuring it if the user asked for auto."""
    v = cfg.get("tau_flow", TAU_FLOW_DEFAULT)
    if isinstance(v, str) and v == "auto":
        v = measure_tau_flow()
        cfg["tau_flow"] = v
        print(f"  measured tau_flow = {v:.4f} (file time units)")
    return float(v)


def show_plan(cfg):
    resolve_closure(cfg)
    n_engine = int(cfg["T"] / cfg["dt"])
    TAU_FLOW = tau_flow_of(cfg)
    w = physics_window(cfg)
    a = w["a"]
    tau = cfg["tau"]

    print("\nPHYSICS  (non-dimensional, h = 1, velocities as stored)")
    print("-" * 68)
    print(f"  tau_p                 {tau:>10.4g}   "
          + ("tracer (v = u)" if tau == 0 else
             f"tau_p/tau_flow = {tau/TAU_FLOW:.2f}  "
             + ("(near-tracer)" if tau < 0.3 else
                "(~flow time)" if tau < 1.2 else "(ballistic)")))
    print(f"  radius_frac           {cfg['radius_frac']:>10.4g}   "
          f"a = {a:.4e}  (a/DZ_MAX, ceiling {RADIUS_CEILING:.3f})")
    print(f"  centre depth          {a:>10.4e}   "
          f"{a/A_MAX*100:.0f}% of slab; "
          + ("ON a node -> no wall-normal interpolation"
             if np.any(np.isclose(Y_NODES, a, rtol=1e-9))
             else "between nodes -> wall-normal interp active"))
    print(f"  N                     {cfg['N']:>10d}   "
          f"phi = {w['phi']:.2e}, MFP = {w['mfp_over_Lz']:.2f} Lz, "
          f"~{w['pairs']:.0f} contact pairs")
    if tau > 0:
        print(f"  rho_p/rho_f           {density_ratio(tau, a):>10.0f}")
    eb = float(cfg.get("e_ball", 1.0))
    print(f"  restitution e         {eb:>10.3g}   "
          + ("elastic" if eb == 1.0 else "inelastic (gamma = %.3f)"
             % (0.5 * (1 + eb))))
    _mdl, _etn, _mun = model_of(cfg)
    _sw = "rotation=%d tan_res=%d p_friction=%d wall_friction=%d" % tuple(
        int(x) for x in switches_of(cfg))
    print(f"  model                 {_mdl:>10}   "
          + {"A": "smooth disks, no rotation",
             "B": "passive rotation (tau_rot = 0.3 tau_p = %.4g), smooth "
                  "contact; spins init: %s" % (0.3 * tau, cfg.get("spin_init")),
             "C": "tangential restitution e_tan = %g (tau_rot = %.4g); spins "
                  "init: %s" % (_etn, 0.3 * tau, cfg.get("spin_init")),
             "D": "e_tan = %g, Coulomb cap mu_pp = %g (tau_rot = %.4g); "
                  "spins init: %s" % (_etn, _mun, 0.3 * tau,
                                      cfg.get("spin_init")),
             "E": "e_tan = %g, mu_pp = %g, wall friction mu_w g_eff = %g "
                  "(tau_rot = %.4g); spins init: %s"
                  % (_etn, _mun, float(cfg.get("mu_w", 0)) * float(cfg.get("g_eff", 0)),
                     0.3 * tau, cfg.get("spin_init"))}.get(_mdl, "?")
          + f"   [{_sw}]")
    print(f"  N window              floor {w['floor']:.0f}  "
          f"<=  N = {cfg['N']}  <=  ceiling {w['ceiling']:.0f}"
          + ("   [OK]" if not (w["below_floor"] or w["above_ceiling"])
             else "   [OUT OF RANGE]"))

    # tracer plan
    tr = str(cfg.get("tracer", "auto")).strip()
    if tr.lower() == "off":
        tr_msg = ("NO tau_p = 0 baseline (tracer off) -- "
                  "excess-over-tracer diagnostics unavailable")
    elif cfg["tau"] == 0:
        tr_msg = "this run IS the tracer (tau_p = 0)"
    elif tr == "rerun":
        tr_msg = "will run a fresh tau_p = 0 baseline (--tracer rerun)"
    elif tr == "auto":
        cand = (f"case_tau{cfg['tau']:g}_r{cfg['radius_frac']:g}"
                + ("_crop%dx%d" % crop_of(cfg) if crop_of(cfg) else "")
                + "/snap_tau0.npz")
        tr_msg = (f"reuse {cand} if present, else run once (--tracer auto)")
    else:
        tr_msg = f"reuse {tr}"
    print(f"  tracer                {'':>10}   {tr_msg}")
    if str(cfg.get("collisions", "on")).lower() in ("off", "both"):
        print(f"  collisions            {'':>10}   OFF (engine fast path; "
              f"fluid sampled at z = {radius_of(cfg):.3e}, matching the "
              f"collisional partner)")

    mb = cfg["N"] * 2 * 4 / 1e6
    print("\nSNAPSHOT COUNTS")
    print("-" * 68)
    print(f"  DNS file (fixed)            {N_SNAPSHOTS:>6}   spacing {DT_SNAP:g}")
    if bool(int(cfg.get("stream", 0))):
        print(f"  engine frames (streamed)    {n_engine:>6}   "
              f"({n_engine*cfg['N']*2*2*4/1e9:.2f} GB to disk as float32; "
              f"RAM ~{cfg['N']*2*2*8/1e6:.0f} MB)")
    else:
        print(f"  engine frames (in memory)   {n_engine:>6}   "
              f"({n_engine*cfg['N']*2*8/1e9:.2f} GB, discarded)")
    _a = radius_of(cfg)
    _shown = rho_ratio_of(cfg)
    _mode = cfg.get("_mode", "A")
    _given = {"A": "given a, given tau -> rho derived",
              "B": "given rho, given tau -> a derived",
              "C": "given rho, given a -> tau derived"}[_mode]
    print(f"  closure                 {'mode ' + _mode:>10}   {_given}")
    print(f"  tau_p                   {float(cfg['tau']):>10.6g}")
    print(f"  radius a                {_a:>10.4e}   "
          f"(a+ = {_a * si.RE_TAU:.4f}, a/DZ_MAX = "
          f"{_a / DZ_MAX if DZ_MAX else float('nan'):.4g})")
    print(f"  rho_p/rho_f             {_shown:>10.4g}   "
          + ("pinned" if _mode in ("B", "C") else "derived"))
    _ns = _n_saved(cfg)
    print(f"  n_snap -> .npz              {_ns:>6}   "
          f"spacing {cfg['T']/max(_ns-1,1):.3f}, "
          f"{_ns*mb:.0f} MB per run"
          + ("   [n_snap=-1: every engine step]"
             if int(cfg.get("n_snap", 50)) < 0 else ""))
    print(f"  diag_frames                 {cfg['diag_frames']:>6}   summary stats")
    print(f"  conv_frames                 {cfg['conv_frames']:>6}   convergence curve")
    print(f"  streak_snaps                {cfg['streak_snaps']:>6}   DNS, for lambda")
    print(f"  anim_frames                 {cfg['anim_frames']:>6}   "
          f"{cfg['anim_frames']/cfg['fps']:.0f} s at {cfg['fps']} fps")
    print(f"  time_avg                    {cfg['time_avg']:>6}   particle density only")
    print("-" * 68)

    errs, warns = validate(cfg)
    for wmsg in warns:
        print(f"  WARN  {wmsg}")
    for emsg in errs:
        print(f"  ERROR {emsg}")
    if not errs and not warns:
        print("  all checks passed.")
    print()
    return errs, warns


def ask(cfg):
    """Prompt for every value; Enter accepts the default. Re-show physics live."""
    # Ask for the model first, then its parameters. Crop precedes the physical
    # inputs because it sets the area used for phi, mean free path, and the N
    # bounds. rho_ratio precedes tau and radius_frac because it determines
    # which closure variables are free.
    _model_keys = ["model", "spin_init", "e_tan", "mu_pp", "mu_w", "g_eff"]
    _first = ["crop_stream", "crop_span", "rho_ratio", "tau", "radius_frac"]
    order = _model_keys + _first + [k for k in DEFAULTS
                                    if k not in _first and k not in _model_keys]

    print("\n" + "=" * 68)
    print("  RUN SETUP  --  type a value and press Enter,")
    print("                 or press Enter alone to keep the [default].")
    print("=" * 68)
    print("  MODEL (notes_sep26 hierarchy) -- one letter, then only its own "
          "parameters:")
    print("    A  smooth disks, no rotation                       (nothing)")
    print("    B  + rotation, contact smooth (same trajectories)  "
          "(spin_init)")
    print("    C  + tangential restitution at contact             "
          "(spin_init, e_tan)")
    print("    D  + Coulomb friction on that impulse              "
          "(spin_init, e_tan, mu_pp)")
    print("    E  + wall friction, sliding / rolling on the wall  "
          "(spin_init, e_tan, mu_pp, mu_w, g_eff)\n")
    print("(rho_ratio, tau, radius_frac and N drive the physics; the rest "
          "are frame counts.)")
    print("  CLOSURE  tau_p = (2/9)(rho_p/rho_f) a^2 / nu  -- give any TWO:")
    print("    rho_ratio = 0            -> you set tau and radius_frac, "
          "rho is reported")
    print("    rho_ratio > 0, tau >= 0  -> radius_frac is DERIVED and ignored")
    print("    rho_ratio > 0, tau <  0  -> tau is DERIVED from radius_frac\n")
    for k in order:
        # a model parameter is asked only for the models that use it
        if k in ("spin_init", "e_tan", "mu_pp", "mu_w", "g_eff") \
                and k not in MODEL_PARAMS.get(model_of(cfg)[0], ()):
            continue
        v = DEFAULTS[k]
        while True:
            s = input(f"\n  {k}   [default: {v}]\n"
                      f"      {HELP[k]}\n  > ").strip()
            if not s:
                break
            try:
                # one coercion path shared with the CLI, so the "auto"
                # sentinel and the type rules cannot drift apart
                cfg[k] = _coerce(k, s)
                break
            except ValueError:
                print(f"      '{s}' is not a valid value for {k} "
                      f"(expected {'a number' if type(v).__name__ in ('int', 'float') else 'text'}"
                      + (" or 'auto'" if k in _AUTO_KEYS else "")
                      + "). Try again, or press Enter for the default.")
        # live feedback after the model: which parameters it will ask for
        if k == "model":
            _m = model_of(cfg)[0]
            if _m not in MODEL_PARAMS:
                print(f"      !! {_m!r} is not a model; use A, B, C, D or E. "
                      f"Continuing with A.")
                cfg["model"] = "A"; _m = "A"
            _need = MODEL_PARAMS[_m]
            print(f"      -> model {_m}: "
                  + (f"its own parameters: {', '.join(_need)}" if _need
                     else "no model parameters") + "\n")
        # live feedback after each physical knob
        if k in ("radius_frac", "N", "tau", "rho_ratio",
                 "crop_stream", "crop_span"):
            resolve_closure(cfg)
            w = physics_window(cfg)
            L_, W_, A_ = domain_of(cfg)
            flag = ("  <-- BELOW FLOOR, raise N" if w["below_floor"]
                    else "  <-- above one-way ceiling" if w["above_ceiling"]
                    else "")
            print(f"      -> closure {cfg.get('_mode', 'A')}: "
                  f"tau = {float(cfg['tau']):.6g}, "
                  f"a = {cfg['_a']:.4e} "
                  f"(radius_frac {float(cfg['radius_frac']):.4g}), "
                  f"rho_p/rho_f = {cfg['rho_ratio_derived']:.6g}")
            print(f"      -> area = {A_:.3f}"
                  + ("" if crop_of(cfg) is None else
                     " (cropped %dx%d)" % crop_of(cfg))
                  + f", a = {w['a']:.3e}, phi = {w['phi']:.1e}, "
                  f"MFP = {w['mfp_over_Lz']:.2f} Lz, "
                  f"N in [{w['floor']:.0f}, {w['ceiling']:.0f}]{flag}\n")
    return cfg



def _tracer_meta_matches(path, N, a, dt, T, e_ball, sample_a, cfg=None):
    cfg = cfg or {}
    """Pre-check a candidate snap_tau0.npz against this configuration.
    Same fields run_case validates definitively; this only selects a
    candidate, so a false positive is caught later, never silently used."""
    try:
        from snapshots import load_snapshots
        m = dict(load_snapshots(path).get("meta", {}))
    except Exception:
        return False
    m.setdefault("e_ball", 1.0)
    m.setdefault("sample_a", -1.0)
    # A tracer is a point particle with archived radius 0; its drag height is
    # stored as sample_a. It cannot collide, so collisions_on is not needed to
    # distinguish compatible tracer archives.
    want = dict(tau=0.0, N=N, radius=0.0, dt=dt, T=T, e_ball=float(e_ball),
                sample_a=(float(sample_a) if sample_a is not None else float(a)))
    if "seed" in m and "seed" in cfg:
        want["seed"] = int(cfg["seed"])
    return all(np.isclose(float(m.get(k, np.nan)), float(v), rtol=1e-9)
               for k, v in want.items())


def resolve_tracer(cfg, outdir, a, sample_a):
    """
    Decide where the tau_p = 0 baseline comes from. Returns a path to reuse,
    or None to run the tracer.

        rerun    always run a fresh tracer
        auto     (default) 1) reuse outdir/snap_tau0.npz if present;
                 2) otherwise SEARCH SIBLING case_* folders that share this
                    configuration's suffix (_r/_crop/_e/_nocoll tokens) for a
                    metadata-matched tracer -- so a tau_p sweep reuses one
                    baseline with no path bookkeeping;
                 3) otherwise run it once.
        <path>   reuse that file (validated by run_case).

    The sibling search restricts to directories with the SAME name suffix
    because crop is not in the snapshot metadata: two tracers with identical
    (N, radius, dt, T) but different crops would be indistinguishable by meta
    alone, and pairing across crops would be a wrong baseline.
    """
    import glob
    tracer_arg = str(cfg["tracer"]).strip()
    if tracer_arg.lower() == "off":
        return None                      # no baseline; run_tracer=False
    if cfg["tau"] == 0 or tracer_arg == "rerun":
        return None
    if tracer_arg not in ("auto", "off"):
        return tracer_arg                       # explicit path, checked in main
    cand = os.path.join(outdir, "snap_tau0.npz")
    if os.path.exists(cand):
        print(f"  reusing existing tracer {cand} (tracer=auto)")
        return cand
    # sibling search: case_tau*_<same suffix>/snap_tau0.npz
    base = os.path.basename(outdir)             # case_tau5_r0.5_crop16[_e..][..]
    parts = base.split("_", 2)                  # ['case', 'tauX', suffix]
    suffix = parts[2] if len(parts) == 3 else ""
    # A point tracer is independent of the contact model, so search sibling
    # folders both with and without the model tag.
    import re
    suffix_nomodel = re.sub(r"_m[BCDE](?:_(?:et|mu|muw|g)[^_]*)*", "", suffix)
    pats = {os.path.join(os.path.dirname(outdir) or ".",
                         f"case_tau*_{sfx}", "snap_tau0.npz")
            for sfx in (suffix, suffix_nomodel)}
    pat = " | ".join(sorted(pats))
    e_ball = float(cfg.get("e_ball", 1.0))
    for hit in sorted(set(h for pt in pats for h in glob.glob(pt))):
        if os.path.abspath(os.path.dirname(hit)) == os.path.abspath(outdir):
            continue
        if _tracer_meta_matches(hit, int(cfg["N"]), float(a),
                                float(cfg["dt"]), float(cfg["T"]),
                                e_ball, sample_a, cfg):
            print(f"  reusing tracer from sibling run {hit} (tracer=auto, "
                  f"metadata-matched; run_case re-validates)")
            return hit
    print(f"  no matching tracer found (checked {outdir} and siblings "
          f"'case_tau*_{suffix}'"
          + (f", 'case_tau*_{suffix_nomodel}'" if suffix_nomodel != suffix else "")
          + "); running tau_p=0 once")
    return None


# --------------------------------------------------------------------- run
def _momentum_plots(folders, cfg, out):
    """
    momentum_energy_plots.py on the archive(s) just written: per-particle
    linear and angular momentum with contact ticks, total KE / P / L. Never
    fatal -- the archive is the deliverable, the figure is drawn from it.
    """
    if not int(cfg.get("momentum_plots", 1)):
        return
    try:
        import momentum_energy_plots as mep
        mep.main(list(folders) + ["--n-particles",
                                  str(int(cfg.get("plot_particles", 4))),
                                  "--out", out])
    except SystemExit as e:
        print(f"  [skip] momentum/energy figure: {e}")
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"  [skip] momentum/energy figure: {type(e).__name__}: {e}")


def _pz_diagnostics(folder, cfg):
    """notes sec. 8 statistics from the archive just written (C, D, E)."""
    if not int(cfg.get("pz_diag", 1)) or model_of(cfg)[0] not in ("C", "D", "E"):
        return
    try:
        import vertical_impulse as vi
        args = [folder, "--out", os.path.join(folder, "vertical_impulse.png")]
        if float(cfg.get("pz_threshold", 0.0)) > 0.0:
            args += ["--threshold", str(float(cfg["pz_threshold"]))]
        vi.main(args)
    except SystemExit as e:
        print(f"  [skip] sec. 8 diagnostics: {e}")
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"  [skip] sec. 8 diagnostics: {type(e).__name__}: {e}")


def _leg_mode(cfg):
    v = str(cfg.get("collisions", "on")).strip().lower()
    if v not in ("on", "off", "both"):
        raise SystemExit(f"collisions must be on | off | both, got {v!r}")
    return v


def main(cfg, dry_run=False):
    """
    Dispatch on cfg["collisions"].

    "both" runs the collisional leg and then the collisionless leg in ONE
    invocation. They are not merely configured alike -- they are generated by
    the same code path from the same seed:

      * the radius is IDENTICAL in both legs (the collisionless leg keeps its
        true radius and disables contact in the engine), so seed_particles
        performs the same overlap rejections and consumes the random stream
        identically -> byte-identical initial POSITIONS;
      * the velocity draw happens after placement from that same stream, so
        vel_init="random" gives byte-identical initial VELOCITIES too, and
        "fluid"/"zero" are deterministic functions of the positions;
      * sample_a is set on both legs, so the fluid is read at the same height.

    Running the legs separately later still pairs, as long as the seed and
    every physical parameter match -- this option just removes the chance of
    getting one of them wrong.
    """
    mode = _leg_mode(cfg)
    if mode != "both":
        return _run_leg(cfg, dry_run=dry_run)

    outdirs = []
    for k, leg in enumerate(("on", "off")):
        print("\n" + "=" * 66)
        print(f"  LEG {k + 1} of 2: collisions {leg.upper()}")
        print("=" * 66)
        sub = dict(cfg)
        sub["collisions"] = leg
        outdirs.append(_run_leg(sub, dry_run=dry_run))
    if not dry_run:
        _verify_paired(outdirs, cfg)
        # the two legs overlaid: same particles, same axes
        _momentum_plots(outdirs, cfg,
                        os.path.join(outdirs[0], "momentum_energy_paired.png"))
    return outdirs[0]


def _verify_paired(outdirs, cfg):
    """
    Confirm the two legs really did start from the same state.

    This reads the saved frames rather than trusting the configuration: a
    difference of exactly 0.0 is the only acceptable answer, and anything
    else means the pairing is broken and every paired statistic downstream
    is measuring the seeding as well as the contact.
    """
    import numpy as _np
    tag = f"tau{cfg['tau']:g}"
    # run_one_case identifies a collisionless leg by its folder suffix and
    # archive metadata, whereas run_dns_study.run_paired uses a filename
    # suffix because both legs share one folder. Accept either convention and
    # use the archive flag as the source of truth.
    def _find(outdir, want_on):
        for cand in (f"snap_{tag}.npz", f"snap_{tag}_nocoll.npz"):
            fp = os.path.join(outdir, cand)
            if os.path.exists(fp):
                try:
                    z = _np.load(fp)
                    on = bool(float(z["meta_collisions_on"])) \
                        if "meta_collisions_on" in z.files else None
                except Exception:
                    on = None
                if on is None or on == want_on:
                    return fp
        return None

    fa = _find(outdirs[0], True)
    fb = _find(outdirs[1], False)
    print("\n" + "=" * 66)
    print("  PAIRED CHECK")
    print("=" * 66)
    if fa is None or fb is None:
        which = (outdirs[0] if fa is None else outdirs[1])
        print(f"  cannot verify: no snapshot for the "
              f"{'collisional' if fa is None else 'collisionless'} leg "
              f"in {which} (looked for snap_{tag}.npz and "
              f"snap_{tag}_nocoll.npz)")
        return
    a, b = _np.load(fa), _np.load(fb)
    dp = float(_np.abs(a["positions"][0] - b["positions"][0]).max())
    print(f"  initial positions   max|diff| = {dp:.3e}"
          + ("   IDENTICAL" if dp == 0.0 else "   <-- NOT PAIRED"))
    if "velocities" in a.files and "velocities" in b.files:
        dv = float(_np.abs(a["velocities"][0] - b["velocities"][0]).max())
        print(f"  initial velocities  max|diff| = {dv:.3e}"
              + ("   IDENTICAL" if dv == 0.0 else "   <-- NOT PAIRED"))
    else:
        print("  initial velocities  not saved (save_velocity=False) -- "
              "positions alone were checked")
    nc = int(b["total_collisions"]) if "total_collisions" in b.files else -1
    print(f"  collisionless leg logged {nc} collisions"
          + ("   (expected 0)" if nc == 0 else
             "   <-- expected 0; the engine ignored collisions_enabled"))
    if dp != 0.0:
        print("  Do NOT use these two runs as a pair. Check that the seed, "
              "radius, N, T, dt and crop are identical.")


def _run_leg(cfg, dry_run=False):
    errs, _ = show_plan(cfg)
    if errs:
        sys.exit("\nfix the ERROR(s) above and re-run.")
    if dry_run:
        print("dry run: configuration valid, nothing executed.")
        return None

    import fast_pairs                                    # noqa: F401
    import run_dns_study as rds
    from run_one_case import run_case

    a = radius_of(cfg)
    crop = crop_of(cfg)
    coll_off = str(cfg.get("collisions", "on")).strip().lower() == "off"
    collisions_enabled = not coll_off
    # Record the same explicit sampling height for both legs so their metadata
    # compare directly; here sample_a and radius are equal.
    sample_a = a
    if coll_off:
        # Keep the physical radius for the collisionless control and disable
        # contact in the engine. This preserves the sampling height, initial
        # non-overlap constraint, and nominal area fraction across the pair.
        print(f"  collisionless leg: radius KEPT at {a:.3e} (seeding, phi and "
              f"drag height all match the collisional partner); contact "
              f"disabled in the engine")
    eb = float(cfg.get("e_ball", 1.0))
    model, e_tan, mu_pp = model_of(cfg)
    spin_init = str(cfg.get("spin_init", "fluid"))
    # the model enters the folder name for B, C and D so that a rotating
    # case can never be paired with, or reuse the tracer of, the wrong file;
    # model A keeps the pre-rotation names
    _mtag = {"A": "", "B": "_mB", "C": f"_mC_et{e_tan:g}",
             "D": f"_mD_et{e_tan:g}_mu{mu_pp:g}",
             "E": (f"_mE_et{e_tan:g}_mu{mu_pp:g}_muw{float(cfg.get('mu_w', 0)):g}"
                   f"_g{float(cfg.get('g_eff', 0)):g}")}[model]
    outdir = (f"case_tau{cfg['tau']:g}_r{cfg['radius_frac']:g}"
              + ("_crop%dx%d" % (crop[0], crop[1]) if crop else "")
              + (f"_e{eb:g}" if eb != 1.0 else "")
              + _mtag
              + ("_nocoll" if coll_off else ""))

    # Configure the tracer and case runs. The frame-count settings are applied
    # by rebinding the three helper callables whose defaults run_case uses;
    # n_snap, seed, a, crop, and vel_init are passed directly.
    import run_dns_study as _rds
    from functools import partial
    _orig_diag = _rds.diagnose_tail
    _rds.diagnose_tail = partial(_orig_diag, max_frames=int(cfg["diag_frames"]))
    # run_one_case imported diagnose_tail by name, so rebind there too
    import run_one_case as _roc
    _roc.diagnose_tail = _rds.diagnose_tail

    # conv_frames: run_case calls fig_convergence with the default max_frames,
    # so patching the default reaches it.
    import figures as _fig
    _orig_conv = _fig.fig_convergence
    _fig.fig_convergence = partial(_orig_conv, max_frames=int(cfg["conv_frames"]))
    _roc.fg.fig_convergence = _fig.fig_convergence

    # run_case supplies n_show explicitly, so wrap the helper to apply the
    # requested series_panels value.
    import snapshots as _snp
    _orig_series = _snp.fig_snapshot_series
    def _series_forced(*a_, **kw_):
        kw_["n_show"] = int(cfg["series_panels"])
        return _orig_series(*a_, **kw_)
    _snp.fig_snapshot_series = _series_forced
    _roc.fig_snapshot_series = _series_forced

    print(f"=== engine: tracer + tau_p = {cfg['tau']:g}, "
          f"a = {a:.3e} ({cfg['radius_frac']:g} x max step) ===")

    # A tracer depends on the flow and configuration, but not on tau_p. One
    # baseline can therefore serve cases with matching N, radius, dt, T, crop,
    # seed, and vel_init; run_case validates these against the archive metadata.
    #       tracer = "rerun"  -> always run tau_p = 0 (original behaviour)
    #       tracer = "auto"   -> reuse outdir/snap_tau0.npz if it already exists
    #       tracer = <path>   -> reuse that specific file
    tracer_snap = resolve_tracer(cfg, outdir, a, sample_a)
    tracer_arg = str(cfg["tracer"]).strip()
    if (tracer_arg.lower() not in ("auto", "rerun", "off")
            and cfg["tau"] != 0):
        if not os.path.exists(tracer_arg):
            # Tracer archives normally live inside a case output directory.
            import glob
            hits = (glob.glob("snap_tau0.npz")
                    + glob.glob(os.path.join("*", "snap_tau0.npz"))
                    + glob.glob(os.path.join("*", "*", "snap_tau0.npz")))
            msg = [f"--tracer {tracer_arg!r}: file not found."]
            if hits:
                msg.append("Existing tracer files under the current folder:")
                msg += [f"    {os.path.abspath(h)}" for h in hits]
                msg.append("Point --tracer at one of these (quote the path "
                           "if it contains spaces).")
            else:
                msg.append("No snap_tau0.npz found under the current folder. "
                           "Run the first case WITHOUT --tracer (auto mode "
                           "generates it inside that case's outdir), then "
                           "reuse it for later cases.")
            sys.exit("\n".join(msg))
        tracer_snap = tracer_arg
        print(f"  reusing tracer from {tracer_arg}")

    if "progress_every" not in inspect.signature(run_case).parameters:
        sys.exit("run_one_case.run_case has no progress_every argument. "
                 "Use the patched run_one_case.py.")
    if "run_tracer" not in inspect.signature(run_case).parameters:
        sys.exit("run_one_case.run_case has no run_tracer argument. "
                 "Use the patched run_one_case.py.")
    if "record_target" not in inspect.signature(run_case).parameters:
        sys.exit("run_one_case.run_case has no record_target argument. "
                 "Use the patched run_one_case.py -- without it the engine "
                 "silently keeps only its own default number of states.")
    if "collisions_enabled" not in inspect.signature(run_case).parameters:
        sys.exit("run_one_case.run_case has no collisions_enabled argument. "
                 "Update run_one_case.py -- without it a '--collisions off' "
                 "leg would still collide while being labelled a control.")
    if "wall_friction" not in inspect.signature(run_case).parameters:
        sys.exit("run_one_case.run_case has no rotation/tan_res/p_friction/"
                 "wall_friction arguments. Update run_one_case.py -- without "
                 "them a rotating run would silently execute the smooth engine.")
    _rot, _tr, _pf, _wf = switches_of(cfg)
    run_case(tau=cfg["tau"], N=cfg["N"], a=a, T=cfg["T"], dt=cfg["dt"],
             rotation=_rot, tan_res=_tr, p_friction=_pf,
             wall_friction=_wf, mu_w=float(cfg.get("mu_w", 0.0)),
             g_eff=float(cfg.get("g_eff", 0.0)),
             pz_threshold=float(cfg.get("pz_threshold", 0.0)),
             e_tan=float(cfg.get("e_tan", 0.0)),
             mu_pp=float(cfg.get("mu_pp", 0.3)), spin_init=spin_init,
             sample_a=sample_a, e_ball=float(cfg.get("e_ball", 1.0)),
             crop=crop, vel_init=cfg["vel_init"],
             n_snap=(None if int(cfg["n_snap"]) < 0 else int(cfg["n_snap"])),
             seed=cfg["seed"], outdir=outdir, inline=False,
             tracer_snap=tracer_snap,
             collisions_enabled=collisions_enabled,
             record_target=_record_target(cfg),
             run_tracer=(str(cfg.get("tracer", "auto")).strip().lower()
                         != "off"),
             progress_every=int(cfg.get("progress_every", 40)),
             stream=bool(int(cfg.get("stream", 0))),
             model_study=bool(int(cfg.get("model_study", 0))))

    snap_t = os.path.join(outdir, "snap_tau0.npz")
    snap_c = os.path.join(outdir, f"snap_tau{cfg['tau']:g}.npz")
    # momentum / angular momentum / energy figure from the archive just
    # written -- drawn here, before the structure figures and animations, so
    # it exists even if one of those later steps is skipped
    print("\n=== momentum / angular momentum / energy ===")
    _momentum_plots([outdir], cfg, os.path.join(outdir, "momentum_energy.png"))
    if model_of(cfg)[0] in ("C", "D", "E") and int(cfg.get("pz_diag", 1)):
        print("\n=== sec. 8: diagnostic vertical impulses ===")
        _pz_diagnostics(outdir, cfg)
    # The density outputs require a tracer archive. Check the file itself
    # because it may have been copied from a sibling run.
    _has_tracer = os.path.exists(snap_t)
    if not _has_tracer:
        print(f"\n[skip] {os.path.basename(snap_t)} not present -- "
              f"density.png and density.mp4 compare the case against the "
              f"tracer and need it. Everything else is unaffected.")

    # --- structures + viz are optional; degrade cleanly if absent
    try:
