        import structures as st
        import viz
    except ImportError as e:
        print(f"\n[skip] structures/viz not importable ({e.name}); "
              f"engine outputs are in {outdir}/")
        print(f"\ndone -> {outdir}/")
        return outdir

    print(f"\n=== structures (lambda from "
          f"{min(cfg['streak_snaps'], N_SNAPSHOTS)} DNS snapshots) ===")
    # Sample all structure diagnostics at the stored plane nearest y = a.
    # DatasetSpec.load_plane_stack requires an integer plane index, and using
    # one plane consistently keeps lambda and the field maps comparable. The
    # reported offset records the difference from the particle-centre height.
    _at_a = bool(a) and float(a) > 0.0
    _plane_idx = (int(np.argmin(np.abs(np.asarray(Y_NODES) - float(a))))
                  if _at_a else st.PLANE_TOP)
    if _at_a:
        _yn = float(Y_NODES[_plane_idx])
        print(f"  particle plane y = a = {float(a):.5e}")
        print(f"  fields sampled at the nearest NODE {_plane_idx}, "
              f"y = {_yn:.5e} ({100 * abs(_yn - float(a)) / float(a):+.0f}% "
              f"from a) -- the DNS reader takes whole planes, so lambda and "
              f"the field figures cannot interpolate. Quote this height, not a.")
    else:
        print(f"  a = 0 (tracer): sampling the slab top, "
              f"y = {Y_NODES[st.PLANE_TOP]:.5g}")
    m = st.streak_spacing_mean(plane=_plane_idx,
                               n=min(cfg["streak_snaps"], N_SNAPSHOTS))
    _sd = m.get("lambda_sd")
    print(f"  lambda = {m['lambda']:.4f}"
          + (f" +/- {_sd:.4f}" if _sd is not None else "")
          + f"   from {m['n_snapshots']} snapshots"
          + (f",  R at the minimum = {m['R_min']:+.3f}" if "R_min" in m else ""))
    if _sd is not None and _sd > 0.15 * m["lambda"]:
        print(f"    the spread across snapshot groups is "
              f"{100*_sd/m['lambda']:.0f}% of lambda -- the first minimum is "
              f"not well\n    determined here, so quote lambda with this "
              f"error bar, not to 4 decimals.")

    # Field maps show one snapshot because time-averaging a convecting streak
    # field would smear its spatial structure. The quantitative curves and
    # thresholds are already averaged over streak_snaps snapshots; view_snap
    # selects the instant used for the map.
    _vs = int(cfg.get("view_snap", -1))
    k_view = (N_SNAPSHOTS - 1 if _vs < 0
              else min(_vs, N_SNAPSHOTS - 1))
    if _vs >= N_SNAPSHOTS:
        print(f"  view_snap {_vs} > last snapshot {N_SNAPSHOTS - 1}; "
              f"using the last.")
    # plane=, not a=: `a` would be turned into a float height by
    # _plane_default and then handed to streak_spacing_mean, which cannot take
    # one. Passing the node index explicitly bypasses that entirely.
    viz.streak_analysis(k=k_view, plane=_plane_idx,
                        out=os.path.join(outdir, "streaks.png"))
    viz.flow_structures(k=k_view, crop=crop, plane=_plane_idx,
                        out=os.path.join(outdir, "structures_full.png"))
    if _has_tracer:
        viz.particle_density_figure(snap_t, snap_c,
                                    out=os.path.join(outdir, "density.png"),
                                    time_avg=cfg["time_avg"],
                                    n_grid=(crop[1] if crop else N_SPAN))

    # Render animations last. They may be regenerated from the saved archives,
    # so failures here should not prevent the second leg of a paired run.
    if int(cfg.get("anim_frames", 0)) <= 0:
        print("\n=== animations skipped (anim_frames = 0) ===")
        print(f"\ndone -> {outdir}/")
        return outdir

    n_anim_flow = min(cfg["anim_frames"], N_SNAPSHOTS)
    n_anim_part = min(int(cfg["anim_frames"]), _n_saved(cfg))
    print(f"\n=== animations (flow {n_anim_flow}, particle {n_anim_part} "
          f"frames, {cfg['fps']} fps) ===")
    # Treat each animation independently so a rendering error does not stop
    # the remaining study.
    def _anim(fn, what, *a_, **k_):
        try:
            fn(*a_, **k_)
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"  [skip] {what}: {type(e).__name__}: {e}")
            print(f"         the .npz and the figures are unaffected; "
                  f"regenerate this from the saved frames if you want it.")

    _anim(viz.animate_streaks, "streaks_comoving.mp4",
          os.path.join(outdir, "streaks_comoving.mp4"), plane=_plane_idx,
          n_frames=n_anim_flow, comoving=True, threshold=1.0, fps=cfg["fps"])
    _anim(viz.animate_flow, "flow.mp4",
          os.path.join(outdir, "flow.mp4"), n_frames=n_anim_flow,
          comoving=True, crop=crop, fields=("u_fluct", "div2d"),
          fps=cfg["fps"])
    if _has_tracer:
        _anim(viz.animate_density_pair, "density.mp4",
              snap_t, snap_c, os.path.join(outdir, "density.mp4"),
              time_avg=cfg["time_avg"], fps=cfg["fps"],
              n_grid=(crop[1] if crop else N_SPAN))

    print(f"\ndone -> {outdir}/")
    return outdir


def _coerce(k, v):
    d = DEFAULTS[k]
    if v == "auto":
        # sentinel meaning "measure it at use". Only valid where something
        # CAN be measured -- accepting it everywhere would let --tau auto or
        # --N auto through as strings and blow up deep inside physics_window.
        if k not in _AUTO_KEYS:
            raise ValueError(
                f"'auto' is not valid for {k}; it is only supported for "
                f"{', '.join(_AUTO_KEYS)}")
        return v
    if isinstance(d, str):
        return v
    if isinstance(d, int):
        return int(float(v))
    return float(v)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description="DNS slab study - physical knobs + every snapshot count",
        formatter_class=argparse.ArgumentDefaultsHelpFormatter)
    g_study = ap.add_argument_group("study options")
    for k, v in DEFAULTS.items():
        # keep argparse type flexible: parse as str, coerce ourselves
        g_study.add_argument("--" + k.replace("_", "-"), default=None,
                             help=HELP[k])
    g_data = ap.add_argument_group(
        "dataset options (applied before import; also settable via "
        "dataset.py, cached in dataset_config.json)")
    for k in DATASET_KEYS:
        g_data.add_argument("--" + k.replace("_", "-"), default=None,
                            help=ds.HELP[k])
    ap.add_argument("--defaults", action="store_true",
                    help="accept all defaults without prompting")
    ap.add_argument("--show", action="store_true",
                    help="print the plan (physics + counts) and exit")
    ap.add_argument("--dry-run", action="store_true",
                    help="validate the configuration, run nothing")
    a = vars(ap.parse_args())
    # dataset keys were already consumed by _bootstrap_dataset() at import
    for k in DATASET_KEYS:
        a.pop(k, None)

    cfg = dict(DEFAULTS)
    given = {k: _coerce(k, a[k]) for k in DEFAULTS if a.get(k) is not None}
    cfg.update(given)

    if a["show"]:
        show_plan(cfg)
        sys.exit(0)

    # One log per invocation, timestamped so repeated runs never overwrite
    # each other. Written in the working directory first (outdir does not
    # exist yet, and the prompts happen before it is even named), then copied
    # into the case folder at the end so the record travels with the data.
    _stamp = datetime.datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")
    _log = f"run_study_{_stamp}.txt"
    with tee_output(_log):
        if not a["defaults"] and not a["dry_run"] and not given:
            cfg = ask(cfg)
        _outdir = main(cfg, dry_run=a["dry_run"])
    if LOG_OUTPUT:
        try:
            if _outdir and os.path.isdir(_outdir):
                shutil.copy2(_log, os.path.join(_outdir, "run_log.txt"))
                print(f"  log -> {_log}  and  {_outdir}/run_log.txt")
            else:
                print(f"  log -> {_log}")
        except Exception as _e:
            print(f"  log -> {_log}  (copy into the case folder failed: {_e})")
run_dns_study.pyParameter resolution, paired runs and sweep analysisSee code ↗

The study runner keeps the Stokes response-time closure explicit.

"""
Run clustering and collective-behaviour analyses for inertial particles in a
near-wall DNS slab using ``modified_engine.simulate_drag_friction``.

    python run_dns_study.py

The Stokes closure for a sphere is

    tau_p = (2/9) (rho_p/rho_f) a^2 / nu.

Any two of response time, radius, and density ratio determine the third.
``resolve_case`` supports fixed-size, fixed-material, and radius-driven
sweeps, with viscosity read from the dataset. A tracer has tau_p = 0 and
a = 0.

The collisional and collisionless legs may be run separately or as a pair.
The collisionless control retains the physical radius for sampling, seeding,
and area fraction, but disables contact. This keeps the initial conditions
comparable between legs.

Two references are used in the analysis. The optional tracer baseline accounts
for clustering caused by projecting a three-dimensional flow onto a
wall-parallel plane. A uniform Poisson sample with the same particle count
calibrates DBSCAN, binning, and the VMR noise floor. Diagnostics are averaged
over recorded frames, with their frame-to-frame spread reported.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import io
import os
import sys
import contextlib
import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.spatial import cKDTree
from scipy.sparse import csr_matrix
from sklearn.cluster import DBSCAN

# progress.py updates one line with "\r" in a terminal. Spyder does not handle
# that display well, so use one line per report there and in log files.
IN_SPYDER = (any("spyder" in k.lower() for k in sys.modules)
             or "SPY_PYTHONPATH" in os.environ
             or "SPYDER_ARGS" in os.environ)

import fast_pairs          # cKDTree pair search
import modified_engine as eng
from progress import monitor, SweepProgress
from snapshots import save_snapshots

# Store particle velocities in every snapshot. Required for the
# velocity-dependent diagnostics; set False only to save disk.
SAVE_VELOCITY = True

# Apply dataset options before dns_fluid imports because slab_interp fixes the
# geometry when it is imported.
from run_study import _bootstrap_dataset, DATASET_KEYS       # noqa: E402
_bootstrap_dataset()

from dns_fluid import (dns_slab_fluid, seed_particles, report_setup,
                       density_ratio, mean_free_path, tau_to_st, TAU_LIST,
                       A_MAX, A_DEFAULT, N_DEFAULT, DZ_MAX,
                       T_MAX, DOMAIN, AREA, RE_TAU, to_wall_units,
                       cropped_domain, particle_window, max_feasible_radius)
from slab_interp import N_SPAN, N_STREAM, N_PLANES, Y_NODES, NU

L, W = DOMAIN["L"], DOMAIN["W"]          # streamwise, spanwise
CROP = None                              # set by configure()


def configure(crop=None):
    """
    Restrict the study to a sub-block of the wall-parallel grid.

        configure((64, 128))     (n_stream, n_span)
        configure(None)          the full domain

    The wall-parallel directions have different point counts, so a crop must
    specify both dimensions.

    Cropping introduces an artificial periodic seam: wrapping places the last
    column next to the first, and the cubic stencil spreads the discontinuity
    over about four cells. ``slab_interp.seam_jump`` measures it on the active
    grid.

    The admissible particle-count window also narrows as the area shrinks. The
    largest radius admitting any N scales with sqrt(area), so halving the area
    reduces the usable radius by about 30%.
    """
    global L, W, AREA, CROP
    CROP = crop
    if np.isscalar(crop) and crop is not None:
        raise ValueError(
            f"configure({crop}) is ambiguous: the grid is {N_STREAM} "
            f"streamwise x {N_SPAN} spanwise, so one number crops a different "
            f"fraction from each. Pass (n_stream, n_span).")
    d = DOMAIN if crop is None else cropped_domain(crop)
    L, W = d["L"], d["W"]
    AREA = L * W
    n = "full" if crop is None else f"{crop[0]}x{crop[1]}"
    print(f"domain: crop={n}  Lx={L:.4f}  Lz={W:.4f}  area={AREA:.3f}"
          + ("" if crop is None else
             f"  ({AREA/(DOMAIN['L']*DOMAIN['W']):.3f} of full)"))
    amax = max_feasible_radius(AREA)
    print(f"        largest radius admitting any N here: {amax:.4e} "
          f"(radius_frac {amax/DZ_MAX:.3f})")
    return {"L": L, "W": W, "AREA": AREA}


# ============================================================================
#  Stokes closure
#
#      tau_p = (2/9) * (rho_p/rho_f) * a^2 / nu
#
#  Three quantities share one equation, so exactly two are free. The helpers
#  below solve the relation for each unknown and check the round trip.
#
#  nu comes from the dataset (slab_interp.NU), never from a literal. A wrong
#  nu shifts every density ratio in the study by the same factor and would be
#  invisible in the output, so it is printed once at the top of every run.
# ============================================================================

STOKES_PREFACTOR = 2.0 / 9.0


def tau_from(a, rho_ratio, nu=None):
    """tau_p implied by a radius and a density ratio."""
    nu = NU if nu is None else float(nu)
    return STOKES_PREFACTOR * float(rho_ratio) * float(a) ** 2 / nu


def rho_ratio_from(a, tau, nu=None):
    """
    rho_p/rho_f implied by a radius and a response time.

    ``resolve_case`` also compares this value with ``density_ratio(tau, a)``
    to keep the reported material density consistent with the closure.
    """
    nu = NU if nu is None else float(nu)
    a = float(a)
    if a <= 0.0:
        return np.inf if tau > 0 else np.nan
    return float(tau) * nu / (STOKES_PREFACTOR * a * a)


def radius_from(tau, rho_ratio, nu=None):
    """
    Radius implied by a response time and a density ratio.

    tau_p = 0 gives a = 0: a tracer at finite material density has no size and
    is therefore collisionless.
    """
    nu = NU if nu is None else float(nu)
    tau = float(tau)
    if tau <= 0.0:
        return 0.0
    return float(np.sqrt(tau * nu / (STOKES_PREFACTOR * float(rho_ratio))))


def resolve_case(tau=None, a=None, rho_ratio=None, nu=None, verbose=True):
    """
    Complete a case from any two of (tau_p, a, rho_p/rho_f).

        resolve_case(tau=8, a=2.722e-3)      -> R follows      (material sweep)
        resolve_case(tau=8, rho_ratio=731)   -> a follows      (size sweep)
        resolve_case(a=2.722e-3, rho_ratio=731) -> tau follows

    Giving all three is allowed only if they are consistent to 0.1%; the
    mismatch is otherwise raised rather than silently resolved in favour of
    one of them.
    """
    nu = NU if nu is None else float(nu)
    given = {k: v for k, v in
             (("tau", tau), ("a", a), ("rho_ratio", rho_ratio))
             if v is not None}

    if len(given) < 2:
        raise ValueError(
            f"resolve_case needs TWO of (tau, a, rho_ratio); got {list(given)}."
            f"\n  tau_p = (2/9) (rho_p/rho_f) a^2 / nu  is one equation, so "
            f"one quantity is always determined by the other two.")

    if len(given) == 3:
        tau_chk = tau_from(a, rho_ratio, nu)
        if not np.isclose(tau_chk, tau, rtol=1e-3):
            raise ValueError(
                f"the three given values are inconsistent with the Stokes "
                f"closure:\n  tau given    = {tau:.6g}\n  tau implied  = "
                f"{tau_chk:.6g}  from a={a:.6g}, R={rho_ratio:.6g}, "
                f"nu={nu:.6g}\n  Supply any TWO and let the third follow.")
    elif "tau" not in given:
        tau = tau_from(a, rho_ratio, nu)
    elif "a" not in given:
        a = radius_from(tau, rho_ratio, nu)
    else:
        rho_ratio = rho_ratio_from(a, tau, nu)

    tau, a = float(tau), float(a)
    rho_ratio = float(rho_ratio)

    # Cross-check against dns_fluid.density_ratio. It uses the equivalent form
    # R = 4.5 tau nu/a^2, so a disagreement indicates inconsistent viscosity.
    #
    #     nu = 1.25e-4    -> Re_tau = 394.5, R = 607   (solver parameter file)
    #     nu = 1.5045e-4  -> Re_tau = 360.0, R = 731   (what a run printed)
    #
    # Both values give the same t_nu, so compare Re_tau when selecting the
    # appropriate viscosity. Trajectories use tau_p directly, but the reported
    # material density, Maxey-Riley terms, and mass loading all depend on R.
    try:
        R_ref = float(density_ratio(tau, a))
        if a > 0 and not np.isclose(R_ref, rho_ratio, rtol=5e-3):
            print(f"  *** DENSITY-RATIO MISMATCH ***\n"
                  f"      dns_fluid.density_ratio({tau:g}, {a:.4e}) = "
                  f"{R_ref:.1f}\n"
                  f"      Stokes closure (2/9)R a^2/nu with nu = {nu:.4e} = "
                  f"{rho_ratio:.1f}\n"
                  f"      ratio {R_ref/rho_ratio:.4f}; implied nu in "
                  f"density_ratio = {STOKES_PREFACTOR*R_ref*a*a/tau:.4e}\n"
                  f"      Trajectories are unaffected (the engine takes "
                  f"tau_p), but the MATERIAL DENSITY and everything derived "
                  f"from it are wrong in one of the two. Fix before quoting.")
    except Exception:
        pass

    if verbose:
        src = "+".join(sorted(given))
        print(f"  Stokes closure [{src} given, nu={nu:.6e}]: "
              f"tau_p={tau:g}  a={a:.6e}  rho_p/rho_f={rho_ratio:.1f}"
              + ("   (tracer: point particle, no cross-section)"
                 if a <= 0 else ""))
    return {"tau": tau, "a": a, "rho_ratio": rho_ratio, "nu": nu}


def _stokes_self_test():
    """Check that each form of the Stokes closure gives the same case."""
    a0, R0 = 2.722e-3, 731.0
    t0 = tau_from(a0, R0)
    ok = (np.isclose(radius_from(t0, R0), a0, rtol=1e-12)
          and np.isclose(rho_ratio_from(a0, t0), R0, rtol=1e-12)
          and radius_from(0.0, R0) == 0.0)
    print(f"  Stokes closure round-trip: {'PASS' if ok else 'FAIL'}  "
          f"(tau=0 -> a={radius_from(0.0, R0):.1f})")
    return ok


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- diagnostics
def vmr_occupancy(p, nbins):
    H, _, _ = np.histogram2d(np.mod(p[:, 0], L), np.mod(p[:, 1], W),
                             bins=nbins, range=[[0, L], [0, W]])
    c = H.ravel()
    return float(c.var() / c.mean()), float(100.0 * np.mean(c > 0))


def periodic_dbscan(p, eps, min_samples=10):
    P = np.column_stack([np.mod(p[:, 0], L), np.mod(p[:, 1], W)])
    P[P[:, 0] >= L, 0] -= L
    P[P[:, 1] >= W, 1] -= W
    tree = cKDTree(P, boxsize=[L, W])
    pairs = tree.sparse_distance_matrix(tree, max_distance=eps,
                                        output_type="coo_matrix")
    G = csr_matrix((pairs.data, (pairs.row, pairs.col)), shape=(len(P),) * 2)
    try:
        from sklearn.neighbors import sort_graph_by_row_values
        G = sort_graph_by_row_values(G, warn_when_not_sorted=False)
    except ImportError:
        pass
    return DBSCAN(eps=eps, min_samples=min_samples,
                  metric="precomputed").fit(G).labels_


def correlation_dimension(p):
    """D2 from C(r) ~ r^D2.  D2 = 2 uniform in 2D; lower = clustered."""
    P = np.column_stack([np.mod(p[:, 0], L), np.mod(p[:, 1], W)])
    P[P[:, 0] >= L, 0] -= L
    P[P[:, 1] >= W, 1] -= W
    tree = cKDTree(P, boxsize=[L, W])
    r = np.logspace(np.log10(0.01 * W), np.log10(0.20 * W), 20)
    C = tree.count_neighbors(tree, r, cumulative=True).astype(float) - len(P)
    ok = C > 0
    if ok.sum() < 4:
        return np.nan
    return float(np.polyfit(np.log(r[ok]), np.log(C[ok]), 1)[0])


def diagnose(p, nbins_list=(8, 16, 24)):
    N = len(p)
    eps = 1.8 * np.sqrt(AREA / N)
    out = {"N": N, "eps": eps, "D2": correlation_dimension(p)}
    for nb in nbins_list:
        v, o = vmr_occupancy(p, nb)
        out[f"vmr_{nb}"] = v
        out[f"occ_{nb}"] = o
    lab = periodic_dbscan(p, eps)
    ids, cnt = np.unique(lab[lab >= 0], return_counts=True)
    out["n_clusters"] = float(len(ids))
    out["noise_pct"] = float(100.0 * np.mean(lab == -1))
    out["largest_pct"] = float(100.0 * cnt.max() / N) if len(cnt) else 0.0
    return out


def transient_time(tau, n_tau=5.0):
    """Estimated time for the initial velocity to be forgotten."""
    return n_tau * float(tau)


def diagnose_tail(res, tau, n_tau=0.0, max_frames=25):
    """
    Average diagnostics over the recorded frames and return their spread as
    ``_sd``.

    n_tau = 0 (the default) analyses the full window.
    """
    times = np.asarray(res["event_times"], float)
    hist = res["position_history"]
    keep = np.where(times >= transient_time(tau, n_tau))[0]
    if keep.size == 0:
        keep = np.array([len(times) - 1])
    sel = keep[np.linspace(0, keep.size - 1,
                           min(max_frames, keep.size)).astype(int)]
    per = [diagnose(np.asarray(hist[i])) for i in sel]
    out = {}
    for k in per[0]:
        vals = np.array([q[k] for q in per], float)
        out[k] = float(vals.mean())
        out[k + "_sd"] = float(vals.std())
    out["n_frames"] = int(len(sel))
    out["t_window"] = (float(times[sel[0]]), float(times[sel[-1]]))
    return out


def poisson_null(N, reps=5, seed=0):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    acc = [diagnose(np.column_stack([rng.uniform(0, L, N),
                                     rng.uniform(0, W, N)]))
           for _ in range(reps)]
    return {k: float(np.mean([a[k] for a in acc])) for k in acc[0]}


# ------------------------------------------------------------------------ run
def run_one(tau, radius, N, T, dt, seed=0, e_ball=1.0,
            collisions_enabled=True,
            vel_init="random", vel_scale=None, progress=True, inline=True,
            save_snap=None, n_snap=50, sample_a=None, rho_ratio=None,
            record_target=None, progress_every=40, stream_dir=None,
            rotation=False, tan_res=False, p_friction=False,
            e_tan=0.0, mu_pp=0.3, spin_init="fluid",
            wall_friction=False, mu_w=0.0, g_eff=0.0, pz_threshold=0.0):
    """
    One engine run.

    Model hierarchy from notes_sep26:
        rotation    spins evolve under the fluid torque (Eq. 2)
        tan_res     tangential restitution e_tan at contact (Eqs. 7-8)
        p_friction  Coulomb cap |P_par| <= mu_pp P_perp (Eq. 9)
      ... & wall_friction             -> model E  (wall friction: sliding /
                                                   rolling, mu_w, g_eff)
      rotation & tan_res & p_friction -> model D  (D only on top of C)
      rotation & tan_res              -> model C
      rotation only                   -> model B  (same trajectories as A)
      otherwise                       -> model A  (tan_res / p_friction
                                                   without rotation are
                                                   ignored, with a warning)
    e_tan    : e_parallel in [-1, 1], read when tan_res is on (1 = smooth,
               i.e. B again).
    mu_pp    : particle-particle friction coefficient >= 0, read when
               p_friction is on.
    spin_init: "fluid" -> omega_p(0) = omega_f(X_p(0), 0) (the rotational
               analogue of vel_init="fluid"); "zero" -> omega_p(0) = 0.
    pz_threshold : notes sec. 8; > 0 counts per frame the contacts whose
               hypothetical vertical impulse |Pz~| (per unit mass) exceeds
               it. The per-contact Pz~ and U~_y are archived regardless.
    A point tracer (radius 0) has no spin: it always runs as model A.

    collisions_enabled : False retains the physical radius, sampling height,
        non-overlapping initial configuration, and nominal area fraction, but
        disables contact in ``modified_engine.simulate_drag_friction``.

    radius   : the physical radius a. radius = 0 is a tracer and is
        collisionless by construction (no cross-section to collide with).
    tau      : response time in the file's time units.
    sample_a : height at which the fluid is sampled, decoupled from the
        engine radius. With collisionless particles this is normally just `radius`, but it
        remains available so a zero-radius tracer can still be sampled at a
        finite height if that is what a comparison needs.
    """
    radius = float(radius)
    is_tracer = radius <= 0.0 or tau <= 0.0
    # a particle with no cross-section cannot collide, whatever the flag says
    if radius < 1e-9:
        collisions_enabled = False
    rotation, tan_res, p_friction = bool(rotation), bool(tan_res), bool(p_friction)
    if is_tracer and rotation:
        print("  tracer leg: rotation requested but a point particle has no "
              "spin -- running the tracer as model A")
        rotation = False
    if not rotation and (tan_res or p_friction):
        print("  NOTE: tan_res / p_friction need rotation (a tangential "
              "impulse changes the spins); ignored -> model A")
    tan_res = tan_res and rotation
    if p_friction and rotation and not tan_res:
        print("  NOTE: p_friction needs tan_res -- D is the Coulomb cap on "
              "C's tangential impulse, which tan_res = 0 leaves zero. "
              "Ignored -> model B")
    p_friction = p_friction and tan_res
    if tan_res and float(e_tan) == 1.0:
        print("  NOTE: e_tan = 1 is a smooth contact -- the tangential impulse "
              "is identically zero and this run is physically model B.")
    e_tan_eff = float(e_tan) if tan_res else 1.0
    mu_eff = float(mu_pp) if p_friction else -1.0
    if p_friction and mu_eff < 0.0:
        raise ValueError(f"mu_pp must be >= 0 with p_friction, got {mu_pp}")
    _wf_req = bool(wall_friction)
    wall_friction = _wf_req and p_friction
    if _wf_req and not p_friction:
        print("  NOTE: wall_friction needs the full contact law (model D) "
              "underneath; ignored")
    model = ("E" if wall_friction else "D" if p_friction else "C" if tan_res
             else "B" if rotation else "A")

    if sample_a is not None:
        field = dns_slab_fluid(radius=float(sample_a), crop=CROP)
    elif radius > 0:
        field = dns_slab_fluid(radius=radius, crop=CROP)
    else:
        # Point tracer with no stated sampling height: use the node nearest
        # the wall inside the slab, and say so -- a tracer sampled at a
        # different height from its inertial partner is not a baseline.
        print("  NOTE: tracer with no sample_a -- sampling at the first "
              "interior node; pass sample_a to match an inertial leg.")
        field = dns_slab_fluid(radius=None, plane=1, crop=CROP)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    # Seed every leg with the physical radius so paired runs use the same
    # overlap rejections, random stream, and initial configuration.
    a_place = float(sample_a) if sample_a is not None else radius
    if a_place < 1e-9:
        pos = np.column_stack([rng.uniform(0, L, N), rng.uniform(0, W, N)])
    else:
        pos, _ = seed_particles(N, a_place, rng=rng, L=L, W=W)

    # progress.py reports drift as the mean minimum-image displacement from
    # the initial position, as a percentage of Lx. It saws up and down with
    # period Lx/U_mean (~60 time units here) because the wrap folds particles
    # back once they pass Lx/2, which caps it at 50%. That oscillation is the
    # cloud sweeping round the periodic box, not a divergence. A drift pinned
    # at 0 would be the thing to worry about: it would mean the particles are
    # not being advected.
    lbl = f"tau={tau:g}" + ("" if collisions_enabled else " collisionless")
    mon = None
    if progress:
        # Set report cadence in engine time rather than in steps, so the
        # report count is what stays fixed as dt changes. progress_every is
        # the number of reports over the whole run; the 0.5 floor keeps a
        # short run from reporting on every other step.
        #
        # Each report runs an O(N) minimum-image drift pass, so this is not
        # free -- at N = 2e4 a few hundred reports is fine, a few thousand
        # is not. Raise it to watch a run, not as a default.
        _pe = max(1, int(progress_every))
        mon = monitor(field, T, label=lbl, every=max(T / _pe, 0.5),
                      L=L, W=W, diagnostics=True, inline=inline)
        if T / _pe < 0.5:
            print(f"  progress_every={_pe} would report every "
                  f"{T / _pe:.3g} time units; floored at 0.5 "
                  f"({int(T / 0.5)} reports).")

    u0 = field(pos, 0.0)
    # initial spins: the rotational analogue of vel_init
    omega0 = None
    if rotation:
        if getattr(field, "omega", None) is None:
            raise AttributeError("rotation needs a fluid field with .omega "
                                 "(dns_fluid.dns_slab_fluid provides it)")
        if str(spin_init) == "fluid":
            omega0 = field.omega(pos, 0.0)
        elif str(spin_init) == "zero":
            omega0 = np.zeros((N, 3))
        else:
            raise ValueError(f"spin_init must be 'fluid' or 'zero', got "
                             f"{spin_init!r}")
    if vel_init == "fluid":
        vel = u0
    elif vel_init == "zero":
        vel = np.zeros_like(pos)
    else:                                   # random, uncorrelated with the flow
        s = (float(np.sqrt((u0 ** 2).sum(axis=1).mean() / 2.0))
             if vel_scale is None else vel_scale)
        vel = rng.normal(0.0, s, size=pos.shape)

    # The engine keeps only `record_target` states:
    #     record_every = max(1, n_steps // record_target)
    # Default to the number that will be saved. Pass record_target=n_steps (or
    # anything larger, which is clamped) to keep every
    # engine step. That costs positions AND velocities in RAM for the whole
    # run: at N = 10000 and 10001 frames it is 3.2 GB per leg, and the engine
    # holds them as Python lists of arrays until the run ends.
    n_steps = int(np.ceil(T / dt))
    if record_target is None:
        # n_snap=None means "save every frame the engine kept", so the engine
        # must be told to keep them all.
        record_target = n_steps if n_snap is None else int(n_snap)
    record_target = max(1, min(int(record_target), n_steps))
    # Only meaningful when the histories actually live in RAM. With
    # stream_dir set the same states go to disk as float32 and RAM stays at
    # one frame, so this note would be flatly wrong there.
    if stream_dir is None:
        _mem = 2 * (record_target + 1) * N * 2 * 8 / 1e9
        if _mem > 2.0:
            print(f"  NOTE: record_target={record_target} keeps "
                  f"{record_target + 1} states = {_mem:.1f} GB in RAM for "
                  f"this leg (positions + velocities). stream=1 sends them "
                  f"to disk instead ({_mem / 4:.1f} GB float32, ~1 frame of "
                  f"RAM).")

    # The engine still needs a positive radius internally; a true zero would
    # trip its validation. Use a value far below any resolvable length and
    # leave collisions off, which is what a tracer is.
    r_eng = radius if radius > 0 else 1e-9

    # Report the streaming destination before engine output is redirected.
    if stream_dir is not None:
        _nf_est = (n_steps // max(1, n_steps // max(1, record_target))) + 2
        print(f"  STREAMING ON -> {stream_dir}/ "
              f"(~{2 * _nf_est * N * 2 * 4 / 1e9:.2f} GB float32 to disk; "
              f"RAM ~1 frame). Watch positions.npy grow there.")
    else:
        print(f"  streaming OFF: histories held in RAM "
              f"(stream=1 in run_study writes them to disk instead)")

    with contextlib.redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()):
        res = eng.simulate_drag_friction(
            pos, vel, np.ones(N), np.full(N, r_eng),
            L=L, W=W, T_final=T, dt=dt,
            tau=max(float(tau), 1e-12),           # tau=0 -> v = u (tracer)
            mu_frict=0.0, g=0.0,                  # one-way, no wall friction
            fluid_velocity=(mon if mon is not None else field), e_ball=e_ball,
            boundary="periodic", integrator="strang",
            collisions_enabled=bool(collisions_enabled),
            record_target=record_target,
            csv_filename=f"diag_tau{tau:g}_r{radius:g}"
                         f"{'' if model == 'A' else '_m' + model}"
                         f"{'' if collisions_enabled else '_nocoll'}.csv",
            progress_interval=10 ** 9,
            stream_dir=stream_dir,
            # fluid_omega is the field's own half-curl, not the progress
            # wrapper, which only forwards
            # the velocity call
            rotation=rotation,
            fluid_omega=(field.omega if rotation else None),
            omega0=omega0,
            e_tan=e_tan_eff,
            mu_pp=mu_eff,
            wall_friction=wall_friction,
            mu_w=(float(mu_w) if wall_friction else 0.0),
            g_eff=(float(g_eff) if wall_friction else 0.0),
            pz_threshold=float(pz_threshold))

    if mon is not None:
        mon.done(extra=f"collisions={res.get('total_collisions', 0)}")
        res["progress_history"] = mon.history

    # Confirm streaming from the files actually written, including their frame
    # count and size on disk.
    if stream_dir is not None:
        import os as _os
        _pp = _os.path.join(stream_dir, "positions.npy")
        if _os.path.exists(_pp):
            _k = len(res["position_history"])
            print(f"  streamed {_k} frames to {stream_dir}/ "
                  f"({_os.path.getsize(_pp) * 2 / 1e9:.2f} GB on disk); "
                  f"delete that folder once the .npz is confirmed.")
        else:
            print(f"  WARNING: stream_dir was set but {_pp} does not exist "
                  f"-- streaming did NOT happen; the engine may be a stale "
                  f"copy without stream_dir support.")

    if collisions_enabled is False and res.get("total_collisions", 0) != 0:
        print(f"  WARNING: collisionless leg logged "
              f"{res['total_collisions']} collisions -- the engine is not "
              f"honouring collisions_enabled. Check modified_engine.")

    if save_snap:
        save_snapshots(res, save_snap, n_frames=n_snap, tau=tau,
                       save_velocity=SAVE_VELOCITY,
                       L=float(L), W=float(W),
                       crop=("full" if CROP is None else
                             "%dx%d" % (CROP[0], CROP[1])),
                       N=N, radius=radius, dt=dt, T=T, e_ball=float(e_ball),
                       sample_a=(float(sample_a) if sample_a is not None
                                 else float(radius)),
                       # Record whether contact was enabled; zero observed
                       # events does not imply a collisionless run.
                       collisions_on=(1.0 if collisions_enabled else 0.0),
                       rho_ratio=(float(rho_ratio) if rho_ratio is not None
                                  else -1.0),
                       nu=float(NU), seed=int(seed),
                       # rotation provenance -- pairing checks and the
                       # A/B/C comparison read these
                       model=model, rotation=(1.0 if rotation else 0.0),
                       tan_res=(1.0 if tan_res else 0.0),
                       p_friction=(1.0 if p_friction else 0.0),
                       e_tan=float(e_tan_eff), mu_pp=float(mu_eff),
                       wall_friction=(1.0 if wall_friction else 0.0),
                       mu_w=(float(mu_w) if wall_friction else 0.0),
                       g_eff=(float(g_eff) if wall_friction else 0.0),
                       pz_threshold=float(pz_threshold),
                       spin_init=(str(spin_init) if rotation else "none"),
                       # fixed model constants (3/10 tau_p, 2/5 m r^2),
                       # recorded so the archive is self-describing
                       tau_rot_factor=float(eng.TAU_ROT_FACTOR),
                       inertia_coeff=float(eng.INERTIA_COEFF))
    return res


# ------------------------------------------------------------- paired driver
LEG_CHOICES = ("coll", "nocoll", "both")


def run_paired(tau, a, rho_ratio, N, T, dt, legs="both", seed=0, e_ball=1.0,
               vel_init="random", progress=True, inline=True,
               save_snap=True, n_snap=50, outdir=".", tag=None,
               record_target=None, progress_every=40, stream_dir=None):
    """
    Run the requested legs of one case through the same carrier flow.

        legs = "coll"   collisional only
               "nocoll"  collisionless only (true radius, contact disabled)
               "both"   both, from the identical seed and initial state

    Returns {"coll": res, "nocoll": res} for whichever were run.

    With legs="both" the two runs share the seed, the radius, the sampling
    height and therefore the initial configuration exactly. Their difference
    is contact and nothing else, which is what makes a paired statistic valid.
    """
    if legs not in LEG_CHOICES:
        raise ValueError(f"legs must be one of {LEG_CHOICES}, got {legs!r}")
    want = ("coll", "nocoll") if legs == "both" else (legs,)
    tag = tag if tag is not None else f"tau{tau:g}"
    out = {}
    for leg in want:
        on = (leg == "coll")
        fn = (os.path.join(outdir, f"snap_{tag}.npz") if on else
              os.path.join(outdir, f"snap_{tag}_nocoll.npz"))
        out[leg] = run_one(
            tau, a, N, T, dt, seed=seed, e_ball=e_ball,
            collisions_enabled=on, vel_init=vel_init,
            progress=progress, inline=inline, progress_every=progress_every,
            stream_dir=stream_dir,
            save_snap=(fn if save_snap else None), n_snap=n_snap,
            sample_a=(a if a > 0 else None), rho_ratio=rho_ratio,
            record_target=record_target)

    if len(out) == 2:
        p0 = np.asarray(out["coll"]["position_history"][0])
        p1 = np.asarray(out["nocoll"]["position_history"][0])
        d = float(np.abs(p0 - p1).max())
        status = "identical" if d == 0.0 else f"DIFFER by {d:.3e}"
        print(f"  paired check: initial configurations {status}"
              + ("" if d == 0.0 else
                 "  <-- the legs are not paired; check the seed and radius"))
    return out


# ------------------------------------------------------------------- figures
def figure_paired(out, tau, a, rho_ratio, N, outdir=".", tag=None):
    """
    Two panels for one case: VMR16(t) for each leg, and the paired difference.

    The paired difference is plotted separately because it is usually much
    smaller than either curve.
    """
    import matplotlib
    matplotlib.use("Agg")
    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt

    if "coll" not in out or "nocoll" not in out:
        print("  [figure_paired needs both legs -- skipped]")
        return None
    tag = tag if tag is not None else f"tau{tau:g}"

    def series(res):
        t = np.asarray(res["event_times"], float)
        hist = res["position_history"]
        sel = np.linspace(0, len(t) - 1, min(120, len(t))).astype(int)
        v = [vmr_occupancy(np.asarray(hist[i]), 16)[0] for i in sel]
        return t[sel], np.asarray(v, float)

    tc, vc = series(out["coll"])
    tg, vg = series(out["nocoll"])
    n = min(len(tc), len(tg))

    fig, (ax, bx) = plt.subplots(1, 2, figsize=(10.5, 3.4), dpi=150)
    ax.plot(tg[:n], vg[:n], "-", lw=1.5, color="#1F77B4", label="collisionless")
    ax.plot(tc[:n], vc[:n], "-", lw=1.5, color="#E8722C", label="collisional")
    ax.set_xlabel("$t$"); ax.set_ylabel(r"VMR$_{16}$")
    ax.set_title("(a) clustering, both legs", loc="left")
    ax.legend(fontsize=8)

    bx.plot(tc[:n], vc[:n] - vg[:n], "-", lw=1.5, color="#1B9E77")
    bx.axhline(0.0, color="0.5", ls="--", lw=0.9)
    bx.set_xlabel("$t$"); bx.set_ylabel(r"$\Delta$VMR$_{16}$ = coll $-$ collisionless")
    bx.set_title("(b) the collision effect", loc="left")

    fig.suptitle(rf"$\tau_p={tau:g}$, $a={a:.3e}$, "
                 rf"$\rho_p/\rho_f={rho_ratio:.0f}$, $N={N}$, "
                 rf"$\phi={N*np.pi*a**2/AREA:.3e}$", y=1.04, fontsize=9.5)
    fig.tight_layout()
    path = os.path.join(outdir, f"paired_{tag}.png")
    fig.savefig(path, bbox_inches="tight")
    fig.savefig(path.replace(".png", ".pdf"), bbox_inches="tight")
    plt.close(fig)
    print(f"  wrote {path}")
    return path


def figure_sweep(rows, outdir=".", ylab="VMR$_{16}$", key="vmr_16"):
    """
    Plot collisional and collisionless sweeps with their difference below.

    Error bars show the frame-to-frame spread; the counting-statistics floor is
    much smaller.
    """
    import matplotlib
    matplotlib.use("Agg")
    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt

    have = sorted({r["leg"] for r in rows})
    fig, (ax, bx) = plt.subplots(2, 1, figsize=(6.4, 5.4), dpi=150,
                                 sharex=True,
                                 gridspec_kw={"height_ratios": [2, 1]})
    style = {"coll": ("#E8722C", "s-", "collisional"),
             "nocoll": ("#1F77B4", "o-", "collisionless")}
    for leg in have:
        sub = sorted((r for r in rows if r["leg"] == leg),
                     key=lambda r: r["tau"])
        c, m, lab = style.get(leg, ("#1B9E77", "^-", leg))
        ax.errorbar([r["tau"] for r in sub], [r[key] for r in sub],
                    yerr=[r[key + "_sd"] for r in sub], fmt=m, ms=4,
                    color=c, capsize=3, label=lab)
    ax.set_ylabel(ylab); ax.legend(fontsize=8)
    ax.set_title("clustering vs inertia", loc="left")

    if {"coll", "nocoll"} <= set(have):
        cc = {r["tau"]: r for r in rows if r["leg"] == "coll"}
        gg = {r["tau"]: r for r in rows if r["leg"] == "nocoll"}
        taus = sorted(set(cc) & set(gg))
        d = [cc[t][key] - gg[t][key] for t in taus]
        sd = [np.hypot(cc[t][key + "_sd"], gg[t][key + "_sd"]) for t in taus]
        bx.errorbar(taus, d, yerr=sd, fmt="D-", ms=4, color="#1B9E77",
                    capsize=3)
        bx.axhline(0.0, color="0.5", ls="--", lw=0.9)
    bx.set_xlabel(r"$\tau_p$")
    bx.set_ylabel(r"$\Delta$ (coll $-$ collisionless)")
    fig.tight_layout()
    path = os.path.join(outdir, "sweep.png")
    fig.savefig(path, bbox_inches="tight")
    fig.savefig(path.replace(".png", ".pdf"), bbox_inches="tight")
    plt.close(fig)
    print(f"  wrote {path}")
    return path


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------- main
def main(N=N_DEFAULT, T=T_MAX, dt=0.02,
         tau_list=TAU_LIST,
         a=None, rho_ratio=None,
         legs="both", tracer=False,
         e_ball=1.0, vel_init="random", seed=0,
         progress=True, inline=None, save_snap=True, n_snap=50,
         crop=None, outdir=".", make_figures=True, record_target=None):
    """
    Sweep tau_p, running the requested legs at each value.

    Specify the case with exactly one of:

        a=<radius>          fixed radius, rho_p/rho_f varies along the sweep
                            (a material sweep: same particle size, different
                             material at each tau_p)

        rho_ratio=<R>       fixed material, the radius varies along the sweep
                            (a size sweep: same material, different particle
                             size at each tau_p)

    These represent different physical experiments and need not give the same
    result.

    legs   : "coll" | "nocoll" | "both"
    tracer : include a tau_p = 0 point-particle leg. Off by default because it
             is the most expensive run in the study -- tracers follow the
             compressible in-plane field exactly, so they cluster harder than
             inertial particles and collide far more often (measured: 16.2 h
             against 29 min at phi = 0.07).
    """
    if inline is None:
        inline = not IN_SPYDER          # one line per report inside Spyder
    if (a is None) == (rho_ratio is None):
        raise ValueError(
            "specify exactly one of a=<radius> (fixed size, R varies) or "
            "rho_ratio=<R> (fixed material, size varies). Giving neither "
            "leaves the case undetermined; giving both over-determines every "
            "tau_p in the sweep and one of them will be silently ignored.")
    if legs not in LEG_CHOICES:
        raise ValueError(f"legs must be one of {LEG_CHOICES}, got {legs!r}")
    if T > T_MAX:
        raise ValueError(f"T={T} exceeds the record ({T_MAX})")
    if crop is not None or CROP is not None:
        configure(crop)
    os.makedirs(outdir, exist_ok=True)

    print("self-test:")
    if not _stokes_self_test():
        raise RuntimeError("Stokes closure self-test failed")

    taus = list(tau_list)
    if tracer and 0.0 not in taus:
        taus = [0.0] + taus
    if not tracer:
        taus = [t for t in taus if t > 0.0]
        print("  tracer leg OFF (pass tracer=True to include tau_p = 0)")

    # resolve every case up front, so an infeasible one is caught before
    # hours of integration rather than after
    cases = []
    print("\ncases:")
    for tv in taus:
        c = resolve_case(tau=tv, a=a, rho_ratio=rho_ratio, verbose=True)
        if c["a"] > A_MAX:
            raise ValueError(
                f"tau_p={tv:g} needs a={c['a']:.4e} > slab depth {A_MAX:.4e}. "
                f"Lower tau_p, or raise rho_ratio (a ~ 1/sqrt(R)).")
        if c["a"] > 0:
            w = particle_window(c["a"], AREA)
            phi = N * np.pi * c["a"] ** 2 / AREA
            if phi >= 0.5:
                raise ValueError(
                    f"phi = {phi:.3g} at N={N}, a={c['a']:.4e}: the disks "
                    f"cannot be placed without overlapping.")
            c["phi"] = phi
            c["feasible"] = bool(w["feasible"])
        else:
            c["phi"] = 0.0
            c["feasible"] = True
        cases.append(c)

    report_setup(N, max(c["a"] for c in cases), 0.5, T, L=L, W=W)
    print(f"\n  nu = {NU:.6e} (from the dataset's own wall scales)")
    print(f"     check: Re_tau = u_tau/nu should equal the measured Re_tau, "
          f"and t_nu = nu/u_tau^2 the measured t_nu. If nu is nan here, "
          f"slab_interp could not read the wall scales and EVERY density "
          f"ratio below is meaningless.")
    if not np.isfinite(NU):
        raise RuntimeError(
            "nu is not available from slab_interp -- the Stokes closure "
            "cannot be evaluated. Pass nu explicitly to resolve_case().")
    print(f"  legs: {legs}   tracer: {'on' if tracer else 'off'}")
    for c in cases:
        print(f"     tau={c['tau']:<7g} a={c['a']:.4e}  "
              f"rho_p/rho_f={c['rho_ratio']:8.1f}  phi={c['phi']:.3e}"
              f"   [St+ = {tau_to_st(c['tau']):.1f}]"
              + ("" if c["feasible"] else "   [N OUTSIDE the one-way window]"))

    null = poisson_null(N)
    print(f"\nPoisson null   VMR16={null['vmr_16']:.4f}  D2={null['D2']:.4f}  "
          f"clusters={null['n_clusters']:.1f}")
    print(f"VMR counting floor ~{np.sqrt(2/16**2):.3f} on the 16x16 grid\n")

    hdr = (f"{'tau_p':>8}{'leg':>7}{'VMR16':>9}{'+/-':>8}{'VMR24':>9}"
           f"{'D2':>9}{'clusters':>10}{'coll':>11}")
    print(hdr); print("-" * len(hdr))

    rows = []
    sweep = SweepProgress(taus, label="tau")
    for c, tv in zip(cases, sweep):
        out = run_paired(
            c["tau"], c["a"], c["rho_ratio"], N, T, dt, legs=legs, seed=seed,
            e_ball=e_ball, vel_init=vel_init, progress=progress, inline=inline,
            save_snap=save_snap, n_snap=n_snap, outdir=outdir,
            tag=f"tau{c['tau']:g}", record_target=record_target)
        sweep.tick()
        for leg, res in out.items():
            d = diagnose_tail(res, c["tau"])
            d.update({"tau": c["tau"], "a": c["a"],
                      "rho_ratio": c["rho_ratio"], "phi": c["phi"],
                      "leg": leg,
                      "collisions": res.get("total_collisions", 0)})
            rows.append(d)
            print(f"{c['tau']:>8g}{leg:>7}{d['vmr_16']:>9.3f}"
                  f"{d['vmr_16_sd']:>8.3f}{d['vmr_24']:>9.3f}"
                  f"{d['D2']:>9.3f}{d['n_clusters']:>10.1f}"
                  f"{d['collisions']:>11d}", flush=True)
        if make_figures and len(out) == 2:
            figure_paired(out, c["tau"], c["a"], c["rho_ratio"], N,
                          outdir=outdir, tag=f"tau{c['tau']:g}")

    # the paired collision effect, which is the point of running both legs
    if legs == "both":
        print("\nPAIRED COLLISION EFFECT  (coll - collisionless, same flow, same seed,"
              " same initial state):")
        cc = {r["tau"]: r for r in rows if r["leg"] == "coll"}
        gg = {r["tau"]: r for r in rows if r["leg"] == "nocoll"}
        for tv in sorted(set(cc) & set(gg)):
            e = cc[tv]["vmr_16"] - gg[tv]["vmr_16"]
            sd = np.hypot(cc[tv]["vmr_16_sd"], gg[tv]["vmr_16_sd"])
            print(f"  tau={tv:<7g} dVMR16 = {e:+.3f} +/- {sd:.3f}   "
                  f"({'resolved' if abs(e) > sd else 'NOT resolved'})")

    base = next((r for r in rows if r["tau"] == 0.0), None)
    if base is not None:
        print("\nEXCESS over the tracer baseline:")
        print("  NOTE the sign. A wall-parallel cut is not 2D-incompressible,")
        print("  so tracers cluster from the projection alone -- typically")
        print("  MORE than inertial particles. A negative excess is the")
        print("  expected result here, not an error.")
        for r in rows:
            if r["leg"] != base["leg"]:
                continue
            e = r["vmr_16"] - base["vmr_16"]
            sd = np.hypot(r["vmr_16_sd"], base["vmr_16_sd"])
            print(f"  tau={r['tau']:<7g} dVMR16 = {e:+.3f} +/- {sd:.3f}   "
                  f"({'significant' if abs(e) > sd else 'NOT significant'})")

    if make_figures and len(rows) > 1:
        figure_sweep(rows, outdir=outdir)

    sweep.summary()
    return rows, null


def regime_table(N=N_DEFAULT, a=A_DEFAULT, T=T_MAX, dt=0.02, tau_hi=0.5):
    """
    2x2 inertia x collisions, with GHOSTS for the collisionless column.

    The interaction is the reason for the design: if collisions matter more
    for inertial particles than for tracers, that difference is the
    interaction, and neither main effect alone reveals it.
    """
    c = resolve_case(tau=tau_hi, a=a)
    print(f"2x2 REGIME TABLE  (N={N}, a={a:.4e}, "
          f"rho_p/rho_f={c['rho_ratio']:.0f}, T={T})\n")
    hdr = f"{'regime':<36}{'VMR16':>9}{'D2':>9}{'coll':>11}"
    print(hdr); print("-" * len(hdr))
    null = poisson_null(N)
    print(f"{'uniform random null':<36}{null['vmr_16']:>9.3f}"
          f"{null['D2']:>9.3f}{'-':>11}")
    cell = {}
    for tv, lbl in [(0.0, "tracer"), (tau_hi, "inertial")]:
        for on, cl in [(False, "nocoll"), (True, "+ collisions")]:
            radius = a if tv > 0 else 0.0
            res = run_one(tv, radius, N, T, dt, progress=False,
                          collisions_enabled=on,
                          sample_a=(a if radius > 0 else None))
            d = diagnose_tail(res, tv)
            cell[(tv, on)] = d["vmr_16"]
            print(f"{lbl + ', ' + cl:<36}{d['vmr_16']:>9.3f}{d['D2']:>9.3f}"
                  f"{res.get('total_collisions', 0):>11d}", flush=True)
    inert = 0.5 * ((cell[(tau_hi, True)] + cell[(tau_hi, False)])
                   - (cell[(0.0, True)] + cell[(0.0, False)]))
    coll = 0.5 * ((cell[(tau_hi, True)] + cell[(0.0, True)])
                  - (cell[(tau_hi, False)] + cell[(0.0, False)]))
    inter = ((cell[(tau_hi, True)] - cell[(tau_hi, False)])
             - (cell[(0.0, True)] - cell[(0.0, False)]))
    print(f"\n  inertia effect     {inert:+9.3f}")
    print(f"  collision effect   {coll:+9.3f}")
    print(f"  INTERACTION        {inter:+9.3f}"
          + ("   <- larger than either main effect"
             if abs(inter) > max(abs(inert), abs(coll)) else ""))
    return cell


if __name__ == "__main__":
    # EDIT HERE. Give a= for a fixed-size sweep, or rho_ratio= for a
    # fixed-material sweep -- exactly one of the two.
    main(N=10000,
         tau_list=[2.0, 4.0, 8.0],
         a=2.722e-3,          # fixed radius; rho_p/rho_f follows each tau_p
         # rho_ratio=731.0,   # ... or fixed material; the radius follows
         legs="both",
         tracer=False,
         T=200.0, dt=0.02, seed=43, vel_init="random",
         crop=(32, 32), outdir="case_sweep", n_snap=2000)
run_one_case.pyRuns and analyses one particle-response caseSee code ↗

Snapshot limits use one convention from the command line through the analysis layer.

"""
Run and analyse one particle response time.

    python run_one_case.py                    # tau_p = 0.5, the default
    python run_one_case.py --tau 1.0
    python run_one_case.py --tau 0.5 --N 50000 --outdir case_tau0.5_N50k

Default comparison

    1. tracer baseline   tau_p = 0    same N, same radius, same collisions
    2. the case          tau_p = X    (response time, file time units)

    A wall-parallel slice of a 3D field is not
    2D-incompressible, so inertia-free particles cluster from the projection
    alone. The difference from the tracer isolates inertial preferential
    concentration.

    Running both at the same sampling height keeps the flow sampling
    comparable, so their difference isolates inertia alone.

Outputs written to ``outdir``

    snap_tau0.npz, snap_tauX.npz   50 particle configurations each (~8 MB)
    summary.txt                    every diagnostic, both runs, with the null
    snapshot_tauX.png               positions + DBSCAN segmentation
    compare.png                    tracer vs case, same instant, no statistics
    series_tauX.png                 8 configurations through the run
    scales_tauX.png                 g(r), C(r), VMR vs scale, Voronoi
    convergence_tauX.png            diagnostics vs time, transient shaded
    collective_tauX.png             relative velocity, correlation, caustics
    angular_tauX.png                polar structure, against the null
    particles_tauX.gif              animation

    Downstream plots read the .npz archives and can be regenerated without
    rerunning the engine.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import fast_pairs                      # cKDTree pair search
# Apply dataset options before dns_fluid imports, using the shared bootstrap so
# all entry points configure the same geometry.
from run_study import _bootstrap_dataset          # noqa: E402
_bootstrap_dataset()

                       st_to_tau, density_ratio, report_setup,
                       dns_slab_fluid, NU, to_wall_units,
                       particle_window, max_feasible_radius)
import run_dns_study as rds

from snapshots import save_snapshots, load_snapshots, fig_snapshot_series, \
                      fig_snapshot_compare, animate_snapshots

L, W = DOMAIN["L"], DOMAIN["W"]
