        for a in getattr(artist, "collections", []):
            a.set_rasterized(True)
    return artist


def _install_pair_shim():
    """Wrap ``Figure.savefig`` once to write both formats at ``DPI``."""
    from matplotlib.figure import Figure
    if getattr(Figure, "_house_shim", False):
        return
    _orig = Figure.savefig
    recent = {}                 # (id(fig), stem) -> counter at write

    def savefig(self, fname, *a, **kw):
        import os as _os
        import matplotlib as _mpl
        name = str(fname)
        stem, ext = _os.path.splitext(name)
        ext = ext.lower()
        if not ext:
            # A suffix-free path takes its format from Matplotlib's defaults.
            ext = "." + str(_mpl.rcParams.get("savefig.format", "png"))
            ext = ext.lower()
            name = stem + ext
        tight = (kw.get("bbox_inches", _mpl.rcParams.get("savefig.bbox"))
                 == "tight")
        if tight:
            _TIGHT_SEEN.append(_os.path.basename(name))
            if EXACT_SIZE:
                # Some backends still consult the rcParam for a literal None.
                kw["bbox_inches"] = self.bbox_inches
                kw["pad_inches"] = 0.0
        if ext not in (".png", ".pdf") or not AUTO_PAIR:
            return _orig(self, fname, *a, **kw)
        d = kw.get("dpi", DPI)
        # Matplotlib also accepts the string "figure" as a dpi value.
        try:
            kw["dpi"] = max(float(d), float(DPI))
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            kw["dpi"] = DPI                 # 150 dpi is not a deliverable
        # figstyle.save() already calls savefig once for each format.
        key = (id(self), _os.path.abspath(stem))
        seen = recent.get(key, set())
        if ext in seen:
            return _orig(self, stem + ext, *a, **kw)
        out = _orig(self, stem + ext, *a, **kw)
        other = ".pdf" if ext == ".png" else ".png"
        if other in seen:
            return out
        recent[key] = seen | {ext, other}
        if len(recent) > 64:
            recent.clear()
        try:
            _orig(self, stem + other, *a, **kw)
        except Exception as exc:            # noqa: BLE001
            print(f"  [housestyle] {other} sibling failed for "
                  f"{_os.path.basename(stem)}: {exc}")
            return out
        pdf = stem + ".pdf"
        try:
            mb = _os.path.getsize(pdf) / 1e6
            if mb > PDF_WARN_MB:
                print(f"  [housestyle] {_os.path.basename(pdf)} is "
                      f"{mb:.1f} MB -- a dense layer is not rastered; "
                      f"wrap it in housestyle.dense()")
        except OSError:
            pass
        return out

    Figure.savefig = savefig
    Figure._house_shim = True


def report():
    """Print what the shim saw. Called at the end of a figure run."""
    if _TIGHT_SEEN:
        u = sorted(set(_TIGHT_SEEN))
        verb = "overridden for" if EXACT_SIZE else "left in place for"
        print(f"  [housestyle] tight cropping {verb} {len(u)} "
              f"figure(s): {', '.join(u[:6])}"
              f"{' ...' if len(u) > 6 else ''}")
        if EXACT_SIZE:
            print("    saved at the declared size instead; check these "
                  "for clipped labels and fix the LAYOUT, not the save")
    return list(_TIGHT_SEEN)


# ------------------------------------------------------------- R28 widths
WIDE = fs.WIDE          # 7.00 in, full text width / double column
HALF = fs.COL           # 3.40 in, single column
GOLDEN = fs.GOLDEN

# ------------------------------------------------- R5/R6 semantic styles
# Keep colour and dash pattern together for use as ``ax.plot(..., **COLL)``.
COLL = dict(color=fs.RED, linestyle="-", zorder=3)
GHOST = dict(color=fs.BLUE, linestyle="--", zorder=3)
NULL = dict(color=fs.GREY, linestyle=":", zorder=2, linewidth=0.9)
SUBSET = dict(color="#8C3B00", linestyle="-.", zorder=3)
FIT = dict(color=fs.BLACK, linestyle="--", zorder=4, linewidth=1.0)

LABEL = {"COLL": "collisional", "GHOST": "collisionless",
         "NULL": "null", "SUBSET": "subset", "FIT": "fit"}

# ------------------------------------------------------ R7 colourmaps
DIVERGING = fs.DIVERGING        # zero-centred: u', divergence, log R
SEQUENTIAL = fs.SEQUENTIAL      # non-negative: density, |S|, probability

# ------------------------------------------------- R12 the named nulls
# Store named reference values in one place.
NULLS = {"R": 1.0, "VMR": 1.0, "divv": 0.0, "contact": 1.0,
         "S": 0.0, "corr": 0.0, "excess": 0.0}


def styled(kind, **kw):
    """Semantic style with per-call overrides, e.g. styled('COLL', lw=1.6)."""
    base = dict(globals()[kind.upper()])
    base.update(kw)
    return base


def ref(ax, y=None, x=None, key=None, label=None, **kw):
    """Draw a thin, neutral reference line behind the data.

    Pass ``key`` to take the value from ``NULLS``.
    """
    if key is not None:
        y = NULLS[key] if y is None and x is None else y
    st = dict(NULL)
    st.update(kw)
    st.setdefault("linewidth", 0.9)
    if x is not None:
        return ax.axvline(x, label=label, **st)
    return ax.axhline(y, label=label, **st)


def band(ax, x, lo, hi, kind="COLL", alpha=0.18, **kw):
    """Draw uncertainty as a transparent band beneath the curve."""
    st = dict(globals()[kind.upper()])
    return ax.fill_between(x, lo, hi, color=st["color"], alpha=alpha,
                           linewidth=0, zorder=st.get("zorder", 3) - 1.5,
                           **kw)


def stat(ax, text, loc="upper left", fontsize=7.5, **kw):
    """Place a short statistic in a fixed corner of the panel.

    A warning is printed when the annotation grows beyond two lines; longer
    interpretation belongs in the caption.
    """
    n = text.count("\n") + 1
    if n > 2:
        print(f"  [housestyle] R14: {n} lines in a panel annotation -- "
              f"the caption is the place for this")
    xy = {"upper left": (0.03, 0.97, "left", "top"),
          "upper right": (0.97, 0.97, "right", "top"),
          "lower left": (0.03, 0.03, "left", "bottom"),
          "lower right": (0.97, 0.03, "right", "bottom")}[loc]
    return ax.text(xy[0], xy[1], text, transform=ax.transAxes,
                   ha=xy[2], va=xy[3], fontsize=fontsize, zorder=5, **kw)


def paired_limits(*fields, symmetric=True, q=0.995):
    """Return one colour scale for the fields being compared.

    Shared limits keep visual differences between paired fields comparable.
    """
    import numpy as np
    allv = np.concatenate([np.asarray(F, float).ravel() for F in fields])
    allv = allv[np.isfinite(allv)]
    if not allv.size:
        return (0.0, 1.0)
    if symmetric:
        a = float(np.quantile(np.abs(allv), q))
        return (-a, a)
    return (float(np.quantile(allv, 1 - q)), float(np.quantile(allv, q)))


def legend(fig_or_ax, handles=None, labels=None, shared=False, **kw):
    """Add a compact legend, optionally shared across panels."""
    kw.setdefault("frameon", False)
    kw.setdefault("fontsize", 8)
    if shared:
        kw.setdefault("loc", "lower center")
        kw.setdefault("ncol", 3)
        return fig_or_ax.legend(handles, labels, **kw)
    return fig_or_ax.legend(**kw)


def check(fig, width=None):
    """Check figure properties that can be inspected automatically.

    Called before ``save``; reports issues without modifying the figure.
    """
    msg = []
    w, h = fig.get_size_inches()
    if width is None and not any(abs(w - t) < 0.02 for t in (WIDE, HALF)):
        msg.append(f"R28: width {w:.2f} in is neither WIDE ({WIDE}) "
                   f"nor HALF ({HALF})")
    axes = fig.get_axes()
    # figstyle.panel() stores the panel label in the left-aligned title.
    for k, ax in enumerate(axes):
        if len(axes) > 1 and not ax.get_title(loc="left").strip(
                ).startswith("("):
            msg.append(f"R2: panel {k} has no (a)-style label")
            break
    # A shared axis inherits the label from the axis it is joined to.
    def _labelled(ax, which):
        seen, stack = set(), [ax]
        while stack:
            a = stack.pop()
            if id(a) in seen:
                continue
            seen.add(id(a))
            axis = a.xaxis if which == "x" else a.yaxis
            if axis.get_label().get_text().strip():
                return True
            grp = (a.get_shared_x_axes() if which == "x"
                   else a.get_shared_y_axes())
            try:
                stack.extend(grp.get_siblings(a))
            except AttributeError:
                pass
        return False

    for ax in axes:
        for which in ("x", "y"):
            if not _labelled(ax, which):
                msg.append(f"R10: an {which}-axis is unlabelled")
                break
    for ax in axes:
        titles = [ax.get_title(loc=w) for w in ("left", "center")]
        t = max(titles, key=len).strip()
        if len(t) > 60:
            msg.append("R1: a panel title is caption-length")
            break
        if t.endswith("?"):
            msg.append("R1: a panel title is a question")
            break
    if msg:
        print("  [housestyle] pre-flight:")
        for m in dict.fromkeys(msg):
            print(f"    {m}")
    return msg


def save(fig, path, audit=True, **kw):
    """Run the checks, then save vector and raster copies."""
    if audit:
        check(fig)
    return fs.save(fig, path, **kw)


_install_pair_shim()


# figstyle helpers re-exported so a script needs one import, not two
panel = fs.panel
panels = fs.panels
minor = fs.minor
cbar = fs.cbar
image = fs.image
sym = fs.sym
field_limits = fs.field_limits
panel_figsize = fs.panel_figsize

__all__ = ["WIDE", "HALF", "GOLDEN", "COLL", "GHOST", "NULL", "SUBSET",
           "FIT", "LABEL", "DIVERGING", "SEQUENTIAL", "NULLS", "styled",
           "ref", "band", "stat", "paired_limits", "legend", "check",
           "save", "panel", "panels", "minor", "cbar", "image", "sym",
           "field_limits", "panel_figsize", "plt", "dense", "report",
           "DPI", "AUTO_PAIR", "PDF_WARN_MB", "EXACT_SIZE"]
thesisfig.pyThesis-specific panel layouts and figure formattingSee code ↗

The standard two-by-two canvas fixes the printed dimensions and panel spacing.

"""Figure helpers for the rotation and extension analysis scripts.

The module follows the main pipeline style: matching serif text and math,
7-inch figures, left-aligned panel labels, quiet reference lines, a shared
legend and paired PNG/PDF output.  A LaTeX caption file is written beside each
figure.  When ``figstyle`` and ``housestyle`` are unavailable, the local
fallback applies the same basic conventions.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import os
import numpy as np
import matplotlib as mpl
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt

try:                                     # use the shared style when available
    import figstyle as fs                # applies the rcParams on import
    import housestyle as hs
    NAVY, RED, BLUE = "#1f2a44", hs.COLL["color"], hs.GHOST["color"]
    GREY = hs.NULL["color"]
    GREEN, ORANGE, PURPLE, SKY = fs.GREEN, fs.ORANGE, fs.PURPLE, fs.SKY
    WIDE = hs.WIDE
except ImportError:                                        # pragma: no cover
    fs = hs = None
    NAVY, RED, BLUE, GREY = "#1f2a44", "#D55E00", "#0072B2", "#7F7F7F"
    GREEN, ORANGE, PURPLE, SKY = "#009E73", "#E69F00", "#CC79A7", "#56B4E9"
    WIDE = 7.0
    mpl.rcParams.update({
        "font.family": "serif",
        "font.serif": ["DejaVu Serif", "Times New Roman", "Times", "STIXGeneral"],
        "mathtext.fontset": "dejavuserif",
        "font.size": 8.5, "axes.labelsize": 9, "axes.titlesize": 9,
        "xtick.labelsize": 8, "ytick.labelsize": 8, "legend.fontsize": 8,
        "axes.linewidth": 0.7, "xtick.direction": "in", "ytick.direction": "in",
        "xtick.minor.visible": True, "ytick.minor.visible": True,
        "legend.frameon": False, "savefig.dpi": 600, "savefig.facecolor": "white",
        "pdf.fonttype": 42, "ps.fonttype": 42,
    })
REF = "0.55"                              # reference lines: thinner, grey
CYCLE = [RED, BLUE, GREEN, ORANGE, PURPLE, SKY]
# Match the absolute type sizes used by the other full-width figures.
FS = {"suptitle": 10.5, "subtitle": 7.0, "panel": 9.0, "axis": 8.5,
      "tick": 7.5, "note": 7.0, "stat": 7.0, "label": 9.5}


def figure_2x2(height_ratio=0.62):
    """The paired_legs canvas: 7.00 x 4.34 in, 2 x 2 panels."""
    fig = plt.figure(figsize=(WIDE, WIDE * height_ratio))
    gs = fig.add_gridspec(2, 2, top=0.845, bottom=0.105, left=0.100,
                          right=0.975, hspace=0.66, wspace=0.32)
    axes = [fig.add_subplot(gs[i, j]) for i in range(2) for j in range(2)]
    for ax in axes:
        cart(ax)
    return fig, axes


def cart(ax):
    """Cartesian panel: light grid below the data, open top/right."""
    ax.tick_params(labelsize=FS["tick"])
    for sp in ("top", "right"):
        ax.spines[sp].set_visible(False)
    for sp in ("left", "bottom"):
        ax.spines[sp].set_color("0.45")
    ax.grid(True, color="0.90", lw=0.6, zorder=0)
    ax.set_axisbelow(True)
    return ax


def title(ax, text):
    ax.set_title(text, fontsize=FS["panel"], pad=9, color="0.12")


def tag(ax, letter):
    """Panel label merged into the left title, as the house helper does."""
    if hs is not None:
        hs.panel(ax, letter)
        return
    t = ax.get_title()
    ax.set_title("")
    ax.set_title(f"({letter}) {t}" if t else f"({letter})", loc="left",
                 fontsize=FS["panel"], pad=9, color="0.12")


def labels(ax, x=None, y=None):
    if x is not None:
        ax.set_xlabel(x, fontsize=FS["axis"])
    if y is not None:
        ax.set_ylabel(y, fontsize=FS["axis"])


def legend_below(ax, ncol=3, dy=-0.26):
    """Place the legend below the panel so it does not cover the data."""
    ax.legend(frameon=False, fontsize=FS["note"], loc="upper center",
              bbox_to_anchor=(0.5, dy), ncol=ncol, handlelength=1.3,
              columnspacing=0.9, borderaxespad=0.0)


def legend_in(ax, loc="best", ncol=1):
    ax.legend(frameon=False, fontsize=FS["note"], loc=loc, ncol=ncol,
              handlelength=1.3, columnspacing=0.9)


def note(ax, x, y, text, color=None, ha="right", va="bottom"):
    ax.text(x, y, text, transform=ax.transAxes, ha=ha, va=va,
            fontsize=FS["note"], color=GREY if color is None else color)


def heading(fig, main, sub=None):
    fig.suptitle(main, fontsize=FS["suptitle"], color=NAVY, y=0.982)
    if sub:
        fig.text(0.5, 0.928, sub, ha="center", fontsize=FS["subtitle"], color=GREY)


def save(fig, out, caption=None):
    """PNG + PDF at 600 dpi at the declared size, plus <name>_caption.tex."""
    if out is None:
        return
    od = os.path.dirname(str(out))
    if od:
        os.makedirs(od, exist_ok=True)
    base = os.path.splitext(str(out))[0]
    if caption:
        with open(base + "_caption.tex", "w", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
            fh.write(caption)
        print(f"  wrote {os.path.basename(base)}_caption.tex")
    if hs is not None:
        hs.save(fig, base + ".png")
    else:                                                  # pragma: no cover
        fig.savefig(base + ".png", facecolor="white", dpi=600)
        fig.savefig(base + ".pdf", facecolor="white")
        print(f"  wrote {base}.png + .pdf")


def fmt(x, digits=3):
    """Compact number for captions: 1.23e-03 -> $1.23\\times10^{-3}$."""
    if x == 0:
        return "0"
    e = int(np.floor(np.log10(abs(x))))
    if -2 <= e <= 3:
        return f"{x:.{digits}g}"
    m = x / 10 ** e
    return rf"${m:.{max(digits - 1, 1)}g}\times10^{{{e}}}$"
momentum_energy_plots.pyMomentum and energy histories with collision markersSee code ↗

Collision times for a selected particle are extracted from the shared event log.

"""Plot momentum and energy histories from saved study archives.

The first two rows follow selected particles, showing their planar linear
momentum and spin angular momentum with logged collisions marked on the curves.
The remaining rows show system totals for translational and rotational energy,
linear momentum and spin angular momentum.  Particle traces come from the
first case supplied; total quantities are overlaid for every case.

    python momentum_energy_plots.py case_tau0.5_r2.3_mC_et0
    python momentum_energy_plots.py case_tau0.5_r2.3 case_tau0.5_r2.3_mB \\
           case_tau0.5_r2.3_mC_et0 case_tau0.5_r2.3_mD_et0_mu0.3 --particles 12 340 2210

``--particles`` selects particle indices.  By default, the four particles with
the most logged collisions in the first case are used.  The script reads the
existing archives and does not rerun the simulation.

Units: the pipeline runs m_p = 1 in file units, so p = U numerically and
I = (2/5) m a^2 = 0.4 a^2. Between contacts the fluid drives every quantity
through the drag and the torque; none of them is conserved globally -- only
the contact jumps conserve the pair's momentum and angular momentum. The
totals are therefore evolution curves rather than conservation checks.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse
import glob
import os
import sys

import numpy as np

try:
    import housestyle  # noqa: F401
except Exception:
    pass
import matplotlib
matplotlib.use("Agg")
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
import thesisfig as tf

HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
sys.path.insert(0, HERE)
from snapshots import load_snapshots            # noqa: E402

plt.rcParams.update({"text.color": "#111111", "axes.labelcolor": "#111111",
                     "xtick.color": "#111111", "ytick.color": "#111111",
                     "axes.edgecolor": "#111111", "font.size": 12,
                     "axes.labelsize": 13, "legend.fontsize": 10.5})
NEAR_BLACK = "#111111"


def _load(folder):
    cands = [c for c in sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(folder, "snap_tau*.npz")))
             if not c.endswith("snap_tau0.npz")]
    if not cands:
        sys.exit(f"no case archive in {folder}")
    d = load_snapshots(cands[0])
    m = d["meta"]
    d["folder"] = folder
    d["label"] = (f"{str(m.get('model', 'A'))}"
                  + (f" e_tan={float(m.get('e_tan', 1)):g}" if float(m.get("tan_res", 0)) else "")
                  + (f" mu={float(m.get('mu_pp', -1)):g}" if float(m.get("p_friction", 0)) else "")
                  + (f" muw g={float(m.get('mu_w', 0)) * float(m.get('g_eff', 0)):g}"
                     if float(m.get("wall_friction", 0)) else "")
                  + (" (no contact)" if float(m.get("collisions_on", 1)) == 0 else ""))
    d["a"] = float(m.get("radius", np.nan))
    d["I"] = 0.4 * d["a"] ** 2                  # m = 1
    d["model"] = str(m.get("model", "A"))
    d["e_tan"] = float(m.get("e_tan", 1.0))
    d["mu_pp"] = float(m.get("mu_pp", -1.0)) if float(m.get("p_friction", 0)) else -1.0
    if "velocities" not in d:
        sys.exit(f"{cands[0]} has no velocities (SAVE_VELOCITY was off)")
    return d


def _events_of(d, i):
    if "collision_t" not in d:
        return np.empty(0)
    ij = d["collision_ij"]
    return d["collision_t"][(ij[:, 0] == i) | (ij[:, 1] == i)]


def _pick(d, k=4):
    if "collision_ij" not in d or len(d["collision_ij"]) == 0:
        return list(range(min(k, d["positions"].shape[1])))
    counts = np.bincount(d["collision_ij"].ravel(), minlength=d["positions"].shape[1])
    return list(np.argsort(counts)[::-1][:k])


def _mark(ax, t, y, tev, color):
    """Small ticks on the curve at the particle's collision instants."""
    if tev.size == 0:
        return
    yi = np.interp(tev, t, y)
    ax.plot(tev, yi, ls="none", marker="|", ms=9, mew=1.4, color=color, alpha=0.9)


def _mathlabel(d):
    lab = f"model {d['model']}" if "model" in d else d.get("label", "")
    if d.get("model") in ("C", "D", "E") and "e_tan" in d:
        lab += rf", $e_\parallel$ = {d['e_tan']:g}"
    if d.get("mu_pp", -1) >= 0:
        lab += rf", $\mu_{{pp}}$ = {d['mu_pp']:g}"
    return lab


def _caption(cases, particles):
    d0 = cases[0]
    n = len(d0["collision_t"]) if "collision_t" in d0 else 0
    return (r"\caption{Momentum and angular momentum, " + _mathlabel(d0)
            + rf", $N = {d0['positions'].shape[1]:,}$, {n:,} contacts. "
            + rf"(a) Streamwise (solid) and spanwise (dotted) linear momentum of particles {', '.join(str(i) for i in particles)}, "
            + r"with a tick at each of their logged contacts; (b) their spin angular momentum about the wall normal "
            + r"$L_y = I\omega_y$ (solid) and the magnitude of the in-plane part (dotted): $L_y$ jumps only at contacts "
            + r"and only for the rough laws, the in-plane part never jumps; (c) translational and rotational kinetic energy "
            + r"of the whole system, the rotational part split into its wall-normal component; "
            + r"(d) the total spin angular momentum per particle"
            + (r", and the fraction of rolling particles for model E" if any("rolling_frac" in d for d in cases) else "")
            + r". None of the totals is conserved: the fluid drives every quantity through the drag and the torque; "
            + r"conservation holds contact by contact (Chapter 7).}" + "\n")


def figure(cases, particles, out):
    import thesisfig as tf_
    d0 = cases[0]
    t = d0["times"].astype(float)
    V = d0["velocities"].astype(float)
    S = d0["spins"].astype(float) if "spins" in d0 else None
    I0 = d0["I"]
    fig, (ax_a, ax_b, ax_c, ax_d) = tf_.figure_2x2(height_ratio=0.68)

    # (a), (b): selected particles of the first case
    for c, i in enumerate(particles):
        col = tf_.CYCLE[c % len(tf_.CYCLE)]
        tev = _events_of(d0, i)
        ax_a.plot(t, V[:, i, 0], color=col, lw=1.6, zorder=4, label=f"particle {i}")
        ax_a.plot(t, V[:, i, 1], color=col, lw=1.0, ls=":", zorder=3)
        _mark(ax_a, t, V[:, i, 0], tev, col)
        if S is not None:
            L = I0 * S[:, i, :]
            ax_b.plot(t, L[:, 2], color=col, lw=1.6, zorder=4)
            ax_b.plot(t, np.hypot(L[:, 0], L[:, 1]), color=col, lw=1.0, ls=":", zorder=3)
            _mark(ax_b, t, L[:, 2], tev, col)
    from matplotlib.lines import Line2D
    tf_.title(ax_a, "Linear momentum of selected particles")
    tf_.labels(ax_a, r"time  $t$", r"$p_x$ (solid),  $p_z$ (dotted)")
    h_, l_ = ax_a.get_legend_handles_labels()
    h_.append(Line2D([0], [0], ls="none", marker="|", ms=9, mew=1.4, color="0.3"))
    l_.append("ticks: logged contacts")
    ax_a.legend(h_, l_, frameon=False, fontsize=tf_.FS["note"], loc="upper center",
                bbox_to_anchor=(0.5, -0.26), ncol=3, handlelength=1.3, columnspacing=0.9,
                borderaxespad=0.0)
    tf_.title(ax_b, "Spin angular momentum, same particles")
    tf_.labels(ax_b, r"time  $t$", r"$L_y$ (solid),  $|L_{\rm in\text{-}plane}|$ (dotted)")
    if S is None:
        tf_.note(ax_b, 0.5, 0.5, f"no angular state ({_mathlabel(d0)})", ha="center", va="center")

    # (c), (d): totals of every case
    has_E = any("rolling_frac" in d for d in cases)
    many = len(cases) > 1
    for c, d in enumerate(cases):
        col = tf_.CYCLE[c % len(tf_.CYCLE)]
        lab = (_mathlabel(d) + ": ") if many else ""
        tt = d["times"].astype(float)
        Vd = d["velocities"].astype(float)
        Nn = Vd.shape[1]
        KEt = 0.5 * np.sum(Vd ** 2, axis=(1, 2)) / Nn
        ax_c.plot(tt, KEt, color=col, lw=2.0, zorder=4, label=f"{lab}translational")
        if "spins" in d:
            Sd = d["spins"].astype(float)
            KEr = 0.5 * d["I"] * np.sum(Sd ** 2, axis=(1, 2)) / Nn
            ax_c.plot(tt, KEr, color=col, lw=1.3, ls="--", zorder=4, label=f"{lab}rotational")
            ax_c.plot(tt, 0.5 * d["I"] * np.sum(Sd[:, :, 2] ** 2, axis=1) / Nn, color=col,
                      lw=1.0, ls=":", zorder=3, label=f"{lab}rotational, wall-normal part")
            Ls = d["I"] * Sd.sum(axis=1) / Nn
            if not has_E:
                ax_d.plot(tt, Ls[:, 2], color=col, lw=2.0, zorder=4, label=rf"{lab}$L_y$ (wall-normal)")
                ax_d.plot(tt, np.hypot(Ls[:, 0], Ls[:, 1]), color=col, lw=1.0, ls=":", zorder=3,
                          label=rf"{lab}$|L_{{\rm in\text{{-}}plane}}|$")
        if has_E and "rolling_frac" in d:
            ax_d.plot(tt, d["rolling_frac"].astype(float), color=col, lw=2.0, zorder=4,
                      label=f"{lab}rolling fraction")
            if "slip_rms" in d:
                sl = d["slip_rms"].astype(float)
                ax_d.plot(tt, sl / np.nanmax(sl) if np.nanmax(sl) > 0 else sl, color=col, lw=1.0,
                          ls=":", zorder=3, label=f"{lab}slip rms / max")
    tf_.title(ax_c, "Kinetic energy per particle")
    tf_.labels(ax_c, r"time  $t$", r"energy per particle")
    ax_c.set_yscale("log")
    tf_.legend_below(ax_c, ncol=3 if not many else 2)
    if has_E:
        tf_.title(ax_d, "Rolling fraction and slip (model E)")
        tf_.labels(ax_d, r"time  $t$", "fraction rolling;  slip rms / max")
        ax_d.set_ylim(0, 1.05)
    else:
        tf_.title(ax_d, "System spin angular momentum")
        tf_.labels(ax_d, r"time  $t$", r"$\sum L / N$")
    tf_.legend_below(ax_d, ncol=2)
    for k, a_ in enumerate((ax_a, ax_b, ax_c, ax_d)):
        tf_.tag(a_, "abcd"[k])
    tau = d0["meta"].get("tau", "?") if isinstance(d0.get("meta"), dict) else "?"
    tf_.heading(fig, rf"Momentum and angular momentum along trajectories    ($\tau_p$ = {tau})",
                rf"{_mathlabel(d0)};  $N$ = {d0['positions'].shape[1]:,};  "
                rf"$L = I\,\omega$ with $I = \frac{{2}}{{5}} m r^2$;  $m = 1$ so $p = U$ numerically")
    tf_.save(fig, out, caption=_caption(cases, particles))
    plt.close(fig)


def main(argv=None):
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__,
                                 formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    ap.add_argument("cases", nargs="+", help="case folders; per-particle rows use the first")
    ap.add_argument("--particles", nargs="*", type=int)
    ap.add_argument("--n-particles", type=int, default=4)
    ap.add_argument("--out")
    a = ap.parse_args(argv)
    cases = [_load(c) for c in a.cases]
    particles = a.particles if a.particles else _pick(cases[0], a.n_particles)
    print(f"cases: {[c['label'] for c in cases]}   particles: {particles}")
    for d in cases:
        V = d["velocities"].astype(float)
        print(f"  {d['label']:<22} KE_trans {0.5 * np.sum(V[-1] ** 2):.5g}"
              + (f"   KE_rot {0.5 * d['I'] * np.sum(d['spins'][-1].astype(float) ** 2):.5g}"
                 if "spins" in d else "")
              + f"   |P| {np.linalg.norm(V[-1].sum(axis=0)):.5g}   (last frame)")
    out = a.out or f"momentum_energy_{os.path.basename(a.cases[0])}.png"
    figure(cases, particles, out)
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    _rc = main()
    if _rc:            # non-zero only on failure; no SystemExit noise under Spyder
        sys.exit(_rc)
